### [1] [Lightweight SAR Ship Detection via Contrastive Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30380)
*Surendar Devasundaram,Saber Latibari Banafsheh,Abhijit Mahalanobis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结构化统一关系知识蒸馏框架SURGE，用于SAR舰船检测，通过对比学习在共享投影嵌入空间中迁移教师检测器的几何关系知识，在SSDD和HRSID基准上显著提升轻量学生检测器性能，甚至超越教师模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SAR舰船检测中，深度检测器计算量大难以实时部署，轻量模型虽效率高但难以捕获SAR后向散射中复杂的结构关系；现有知识蒸馏方法多依赖特征或logit匹配，强调局部激活相似性，忽略了目标表示间的几何关系。

Method: 提出SURGE（Structured Unified Relational knowledGE distillation）框架，在共享投影嵌入空间中利用对比InfoNCE损失，将强大教师检测器中的关系几何知识迁移至紧凑学生检测器。该框架架构无关，为两阶段、单阶段和基于Transformer的检测器提供统一的区域级蒸馏接口，无需修改部署架构。

Result: 在SSDD和HRSID基准上，两阶段检测器学生基线提升高达6.2 mAP和8.0 AP75，甚至超越教师性能。

Conclusion: 这是首个基于Transformer的SAR舰船检测器知识蒸馏框架，通过迁移关系几何知识有效提升了轻量学生检测器的性能，验证了结构化关系蒸馏在SAR舰船检测中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Deep convolutional and transformer-based detectors achieve strong performance for SAR ship detection but are often computationally prohibitive for real-time or onboard deployment. Lightweight models offer improved efficiency yet struggle to capture the complex structural relationships inherent in SAR backscatter. Most existing SAR knowledge-distillation approaches rely on feature or logit matching, which enforces localized activation similarity while neglecting the geometric relationships among object representations. We propose a Structured Unified Relational knowledGE distillation framework for SAR Ship detection (SURGE) that transfers relational geometry from a powerful teacher detector to a compact student detector using a contrastive InfoNCE objective in a shared projection embedding space. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first transformer-based SAR ship detector knowledge distillation framework in SAR domain. The framework is architecture-agnostic in the sense that it provides a common region-level distillation interface for two-stage, one-stage and transformer-based detectors without modifying their deployed architectures. Experiments on the SSDD and HRSID benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method yields substantial improvements for two-stage detectors, achieving up to 6.2 mAP and 8.0 AP75 gains over baseline student and even surpassing teacher performance

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度卷积和Transformer的检测器在SAR舰船检测中取得了优异性能，但往往计算成本过高，难以满足实时或在轨部署需求。轻量模型虽然提升了效率，但难以捕获SAR后向散射中固有的复杂结构关系。现有大多数SAR知识蒸馏方法依赖特征或logit匹配，强制局部激活相似性，却忽略了目标表示之间的几何关系。本文提出了一种面向SAR舰船检测的结构化统一关系知识蒸馏框架SURGE，该框架在共享投影嵌入空间中利用对比InfoNCE目标，将强大教师检测器的关系几何知识迁移至紧凑学生检测器。据我们所知，本文工作是SAR领域中首个基于Transformer的SAR舰船检测器知识蒸馏框架。该框架架构无关，为两阶段、单阶段和基于Transformer的检测器提供了统一的区域级蒸馏接口，无需修改其部署架构。在SSDD和HRSID基准上的实验表明，所提方法为两阶段检测器带来了显著提升，在学生基线基础上最高获得6.2 mAP和8.0 AP75的增长，甚至超越了教师性能。

</details>


### [2] [SANA-Streaming: Real-time Streaming Video Editing with Hybrid Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30409)
*Yuyang Zhao,Yicheng Pan,Qiyuan He,Jincheng Yu,Junsong Chen,Tian Ye,Haozhe Liu,Enze Xie,Song Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SANA-Streaming，一个系统-算法协同设计的框架，在消费级GPU上实现了高分辨率实时流式视频编辑，核心采用混合扩散Transformer架构、循环反向正则化训练策略和高效系统协同设计，在单张RTX 5090上达到1280×704分辨率24 FPS的实时编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V）在直播、游戏等交互应用中至关重要，但现有方法难以同时满足时序一致性和高推理吞吐量的严格要求，尤其缺乏能在消费级GPU上运行的实用解决方案。

Method: (1) 混合扩散Transformer架构：部分模块使用softmax注意力增强局部建模，同时保持线性层的高效性；(2) 循环反向正则化：通过流匹配从生成内容预测源帧，增强时序一致性，无需成对长视频数据；(3) 高效系统协同设计：融合GDN核和针对NVIDIA Blackwell架构优化的混合精度量化，最大化Tensor Core利用率。

Result: 在单张RTX 5090 GPU上实现1280×704分辨率实时编辑，端到端24 FPS，DiT核心达58 FPS，在时序一致性和系统吞吐量上显著优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 通过系统-算法协同设计，成功在消费级GPU上实现了高分辨率实时流式视频编辑，证明了算法与硬件协同优化对于突破实时视频编辑瓶颈的有效性。

Original Abstract: Real-time streaming video-to-video editing (V2V) is critical for interactive applications such as live broadcasting and gaming, yet it remains a formidable challenge due to the stringent requirements for temporal consistency and inference throughput. In this paper, we present SANA-Streaming, a system-algorithm co-designed framework for high-resolution, real-time streaming video editing on consumer GPUs, with the following three core designs: (1) Hybrid Diffusion Transformer architecture introduces softmax attention in part of the blocks to improve local modeling capabilities while preserving the efficiency of linear layers. (2) Cycle-Reverse Regularization is a novel training strategy that enforces semantic consistency by predicting source frames from generated content via flow matching, improving temporal consistency without requiring paired long edited videos. (3) Efficient System Co-design combines fused GDN kernels and Mixed-Precision Quantization (MPQ) optimized for the NVIDIA Blackwell (RTX 5090) architecture. By profiling real-world throughput, our MPQ maximizes Tensor Core utilization while maintaining generation quality. The resulting system achieves real-time 1280 x 704 resolution editing at 24 end-to-end FPS on a single RTX 5090 GPU, with the DiT core running at 58 FPS. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-design approach significantly outperforms existing SOTA methods in both temporal coherence and system throughput.

中文摘要翻译: 实时流式视频到视频编辑（V2V）对于直播、游戏等交互应用至关重要，但由于其时序一致性和推理吞吐量的严苛要求，仍然是一个巨大的挑战。在本文中，我们提出了SANA-Streaming，一个系统-算法协同设计的框架，用于在消费级GPU上进行高分辨率实时流式视频编辑，其核心设计如下：(1) 混合扩散Transformer架构在部分模块中引入softmax注意力以增强局部建模能力，同时保持线性层的高效性；(2) 循环反向正则化是一种新颖的训练策略，通过流匹配从生成内容预测源帧来强制执行语义一致性，从而无需成对的长视频编辑数据即可提升时序一致性；(3) 高效系统协同设计结合了融合GDN核和针对NVIDIA Blackwell（RTX 5090）架构优化的混合精度量化（MPQ）。通过对真实吞吐量的分析，我们的MPQ在保持生成质量的同时最大化Tensor Core利用率。该系统的最终成果是在单张RTX 5090 GPU上实现了1280×704分辨率实时编辑，端到端帧率达到24 FPS，其中DiT核心运行速度为58 FPS。实验结果表明，我们的协同设计方法在时序一致性和系统吞吐量上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [3] [DTG-Restore: Training-Free Diffusion Refinement for Generative Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30431)
*Hidir Yesiltepe,Koutilya PNVR,Gaurav Pathak,Navaneeth Bodla,Bharat Singh,Pinar Yanardag,Jinrong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG)框架，通过解耦条件与无条件分支的时间步，增强受损低分辨率视频的结构保真度和时间稳定性，在合成与真实视频上均取得显著提升，并构建了GenWarp480基准数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型虽生成保真度高，但在利用先验进行视频恢复时，标准无分类器引导中条件分支与无条件分支强耦合，导致恢复效果受限。需解耦二者以改善结构畸变和伪影复制问题。

Method: 提出Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG)：在更干净的扩散时间步评估无条件分支，提供前瞻性先验以保持几何结构并抑制扭曲内容的复制；采样过程中逐步退火该时间偏置，使模型从结构校正过渡到细节精炼。作为即插即用模块，可与任意现成恢复方法结合。

Result: 在自建基准GenWarp480（含4400个480p畸变视频）上进行实验，涵盖面部扭曲、身体错位、空间伪影等生成式退化。结果表明，无需任何模型训练，本方法在结构保真度和时间稳定性上均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: DTG框架以无需训练的方式有效解耦了引导信号，在AI生成与真实视频恢复中均表现出更强的感知一致性和结构合理性，具有良好的通用性和即插即用特性。

Original Abstract: Recent progress in video diffusion models has enabled remarkable generative fidelity, yet leveraging these priors for restoration remains limited by the strong coupling between conditional and unconditional branches in standard classifier-free guidance. We introduce a training-free framework that enhances distorted and low-resolution videos by decoupling these signals in time. Our proposed Decoupled Time Guidance (DTG) evaluates the unconditional branch at a cleaner diffusion timestep, providing a lookahead prior that preserves geometry while suppressing replication of warped content. This temporal bias is annealed throughout sampling, allowing the model to transition from structure correction to detail refinement without retraining. Combined with any off-the-shelf restoration module in a plug-and-play manner, our approach improves perceptual coherence and restores plausible structure in AIgenerated and real-world videos alike. To facilitate evaluation, we curate GenWarp480, a benchmark of 4,400 distorted 480p videos synthesized from diverse text-to-video models. GenWarp480 focuses on characteristic generative degradations such as warped faces, body misalignments, and spatial artifacts, providing a purpose-built testbed for assessing robustness to generative errors. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves significant improvements in structural fidelity and temporal stability without any model training.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视频扩散模型在生成保真度方面取得了显著进展，但在利用这些先验进行恢复任务时，标准无分类器引导中条件分支与无条件分支之间的强耦合仍然带来了局限性。我们引入了一种无需训练框架，通过时间域上的信号解耦来增强失真和低分辨率视频。我们提出的解耦时间引导（DTG）在更干净的扩散时间步评估无条件分支，提供一种前瞻性先验，能够在抑制扭曲内容复制的同时保持几何结构。该时间偏置在采样过程中逐步退火，使得模型无需重新训练即可从结构校正过渡到细节精炼。该方法可与任意现成恢复模块以即插即用方式结合，在AI生成和真实视频中均能提升感知一致性并恢复合理结构。为便于评估，我们构建了GenWarp480基准数据集，包含从多种文本到视频模型合成的4400个失真480p视频。GenWarp480专注于特征性的生成退化类型，如扭曲面部、身体错位和空间伪影，为评估对生成误差的鲁棒性提供了专用测试平台。大量实验表明，我们的方法无需任何模型训练，即可在结构保真度和时间稳定性方面取得显著改进。

</details>


### [4] [Mitigating Content Shift and Hallucination in GenAI Image Editing via Structural Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30437)
*Luxi Zhao,Michael S. Brown*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后处理框架，用于解决黑盒GenAI图像编辑中生成结果与原始图像之间的结构不一致问题，通过空间与光度对应建立和融合阶段，在保留感知增强效果的同时保持像素级结构保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GenAI图像编辑器虽能通过文本提示生成视觉上吸引人的编辑结果，但其生成特性常导致空间错位、纹理失真和内容幻觉，破坏需要像素级保真度的下游工作流。现有方法缺乏在保留生成增强效果的同时强制结构一致性的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种后处理框架：首先建立输入图像与GenAI增强图像之间的粗粒度空间和光度对应关系，然后执行融合阶段，将所需的增强效果转移至输入图像，同时抑制幻觉内容。

Result: 与光真实感风格迁移和图像融合的代表性方法相比，所提方法在保持像素级结构一致性和输入分辨率的同时，更好地保留了美学质量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的"结构保持GenAI融合"后处理框架能有效解决黑盒GenAI图像编辑中的结构保真问题，在美学质量和结构一致性之间取得了良好平衡。

Original Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) image editors, such as Nano Banana, produce visually compelling results for retouching tasks, enabling non-experts to edit images through text prompts alone. However, the generative nature of these models often introduces spatial misalignment, texture distortion, and content hallucination, all of which are detrimental to downstream workflows that require pixel-level fidelity. We identify a problem setting we call "structure-preserving GenAI fusion" for black-box GenAI image retouching: retain the perceptual enhancements of a GenAI output while enforcing structural faithfulness to the original input image. To address this problem, we propose a post-processing framework that fuses an input image with its GenAI-enhanced counterpart by first establishing coarse spatial and photometric correspondences, then performing a fusion stage that transfers desired enhancements while suppressing hallucinated content. In the absence of direct prior work in this setting, we evaluate our framework against representative methods from photorealistic style transfer and image fusion. Our experiments demonstrate that our method better preserves aesthetic quality while maintaining pixel-level structural consistency and the input resolution.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI（GenAI）图像编辑器（如Nano Banana）能够在修图任务中产生视觉上引人注目的结果，使非专家用户仅通过文本提示即可编辑图像。然而，这些模型的生成特性常常引入空间错位、纹理失真和内容幻觉，这些都对需要像素级保真度的下游工作流有害。我们识别了一个称为"结构保持GenAI融合"的问题设置，用于黑盒GenAI图像修图：保留GenAI输出的感知增强效果，同时强制保持对原始输入图像的结构忠实性。为解决该问题，我们提出一个后处理框架，该框架首先建立输入图像与其GenAI增强版本之间的粗粒度空间和光度对应关系，然后执行融合阶段，将期望的增强效果转移过来，同时抑制幻觉内容。由于该设置下缺乏直接的先前工作，我们将所提框架与光真实感风格迁移和图像融合的代表性方法进行了对比评估。实验表明，我们的方法在保持像素级结构一致性和输入分辨率的同时，更好地保留了美学质量。

</details>


### [5] [Clustering Guided Domain-Specific Pretrained Foundation Model Very High-Resolution Arctic Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30467)
*Amal S. Perera,Chandi Witharana,Elias Manos,Michael Pimenta,Anna K. Liljedahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个面向北极地区的遥感基础模型，通过多样性感知的区域级影像筛选和掩码自编码器自监督预训练，在超高空间分辨率卫星影像分析中显著优于ImageNet初始化和通用地球观测基础模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有通用遥感基础模型在北极地区精细尺度映射任务中表现不佳，缺乏针对北极超高空间分辨率影像的领域专用预训练模型。

Method: 使用光谱和采集元数据描述符进行可扩展的亲和传播聚类，从267 TB Vantor VHSR影像中筛选约300万张影像块；采用领域自适应的MAE重构目标对ViT-Large编码器进行自监督预训练。

Result: 在四个北极标注数据集上，相比ImageNet初始化的ViT-Large基线，前景平均F1分数分别达到0.87、0.72、0.93和0.87，提升约5-8个百分点；相比Prithvi-EO-2.0，最小增益至少提升15个百分点。

Conclusion: 在固定架构和MAE目标的前提下，优化预训练数据集的区域尺度分布可以产生可复用的北极领域编码器，为多种VHSR遥感应用提供更具迁移性的特征表示。

Original Abstract: This study introduces a novel Arctic-focused remote sensing foundation model (RSFM) by combining diversity-aware regional-scale image curation with masked autoencoder (MAE) self-supervised pretraining of a Vision Transformer (ViT) encoder for very-high-spatial-resolution (VHSR) satellite image analysis. Spectral and acquisition-metadata descriptors were used in a scalable affinity-propagation clustering workflow to select approximately 3 million chips from 267 TB of Vantor VHSR imagery This curation strategy was designed to reduce oversampling of visually repetitive or low-information areas while preserving broad scene diversity across the study domain. We pretrained a ViT-Large encoder on the curated corpus using a domain-adapted MAE reconstruction objective, producing Arctic-specific transformer weights for downstream feature mapping. The pretrained encoder was integrated into an existing location-aware detection and segmentation framework and evaluated across four hand-labeled Arctic datasets. Compared to ImageNet-initialized ViT-Large baseline, Arctic MAE pretraining produced consistent improvements in foreground mean F1 scores of 0.87, 0.72, 0.93, and 0.87, for infrastructure, IWP, RTS, and TCNs, with approximately 5-8 percentage increase. The proposed model also outperformed Prithvi-EO-2.0 in all downstream comparisons, with the smallest gain corresponding to at least a 15 percentage improvement mean F1, suggesting that domain-specific self-supervised pretraining on curated Arctic VHSR imagery provides more transferable representations for fine-scale Arctic mapping than a general-purpose Earth observation foundation model. These results demonstrate that optimizing the pretraining data distribution at regional scale, while keeping the architecture and MAE objective fixed, can produce a reusable Arctic-domain encoder for multiple VHSR remote sensing applications.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究提出了一种新颖的面向北极的遥感基础模型，通过结合多样性感知的区域级影像筛选与掩码自编码器的自监督预训练，对Vision Transformer编码器进行训练，用于超高空间分辨率卫星影像分析。利用光谱和采集元数据描述符，在可扩展的亲和传播聚类流程中从267 TB Vantor VHSR影像中筛选了约300万张影像块。该筛选策略旨在减少视觉重复或低信息区域的过采样，同时保留研究区域内广泛的场景多样性。我们使用领域自适应的MAE重构目标在筛选后的语料库上预训练了ViT-Large编码器，生成了面向下游特征映射的北极专用Transformer权重。将预训练编码器集成到现有的位置感知检测与分割框架中，并在四个手工标注的北极数据集上进行评估。与ImageNet初始化的ViT-Large基线相比，北极MAE预训练在基础设施、IWP、RTS和TCNs上的前景平均F1分数分别达到0.87、0.72、0.93和0.87，提升了约5-8个百分点。所提出的模型在所有下游比较中均优于Prithvi-EO-2.0，最小增益对应至少15个百分点的平均F1提升，表明在筛选后的北极VHSR影像上进行领域专用的自监督预训练，能够为精细尺度的北极映射提供比通用地球观测基础模型更具迁移性的特征表示。这些结果表明，在固定架构和MAE目标的前提下，优化区域尺度的预训练数据分布，可以产生一个可复用的北极领域编码器，适用于多种VHSR遥感应用。

</details>


### [6] [A Novel Global Context-aware Deep Neural Network for Enhanced Brain Tumor Segmentation using Magnetic Resonance Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30510)
*Sourjya Mukherjee,Ananya Bhattacharjee,R. Murugan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合空间和通道注意力的GCSER-UNet模型，在多模态MRI脑肿瘤分割任务上取得了优于现有方法的性能，在TCGA LGG和BraTS 2020数据集上分别达到94%和95%/92%/90%的Dice分数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑肿瘤的严重性需要精确的分割以辅助诊断，但手动识别成本高、劳动强度大且存在错误风险，因此需要自动化的高精度分割方法。

Method: 提出全局上下文感知的挤压激励残差UNet（GCSER-UNet），融合空间注意力和通道注意力机制，增强模型捕捉空间依赖性和上下文信息的能力，用于从多模态MRI切片中提取肿瘤区域。

Result: 在TCGA LGG数据集上达到94% Dice分数（SOTA为91.8%）；在BraTS 2020数据集上，对Whole Tumor、Tumor Core、Enhancing Tumor分别达到95%、92%、90%的Dice分数（SOTA分别为94%、93%、88%）。

Conclusion: GCSER-UNet在脑肿瘤精确分割中表现出优异性能，能够辅助神经科医生进行脑癌管理和治疗规划。

Original Abstract: Brain cancer's severity necessitates precise brain tumor segmentation, which is crucial for effective brain tumor diagnosis. Manual identification, burdened by high costs, labor, and error risks, highlights the need for automated methods. In this study, we introduce the Global Context-aware Squeeze and Excite Residual UNet (GCSER-UNet), which facilitates a fusion of spatial and channel-wise attention and thus enhances the model's capacity to capture intricate spatial dependencies and contextual information. GCSER-UNet efficiently extracts tumor segments from multimodal MRI slices, delivering exceptional performance. Evaluations on benchmark databases exhibit its superiority, achieving a notable 94 percent dice score on the TCGA LGG dataset, surpassing the state-of-the-art dice score of 91.8 percent. In the BraTS 2020 dataset, the proposed GCSER-UNet ensemble approach yielded dice scores of 95 percent, 92 percent, and 90 percent for the tumor regions - Whole Tumor (W), Tumor Core (T), and Enhancing Tumor (E), respectively. The current state-of-the-art dice scores were 94 percent, 93 percent, and 88 percent. These compelling outcomes highlight the efficacy of GCSER-UNet in precise brain tumor segmentation and thus can aid neurologists in effective brain cancer management and treatment planning.

中文摘要翻译: 脑癌的严重性使得精确的脑肿瘤分割至关重要，这是有效脑肿瘤诊断的关键。手动识别因成本高、劳动强度大且存在错误风险，凸显了自动化方法的必要性。在本研究中，我们提出了全局上下文感知的挤压激励残差UNet（GCSER-UNet），该模型融合了空间注意力和通道注意力，从而增强了模型捕捉复杂空间依赖性和上下文信息的能力。GCSER-UNet能够从多模态MRI切片中高效提取肿瘤区域，展现出卓越的性能。在基准数据集上的评估证明了其优越性：在TCGA LGG数据集上达到了94%的Dice分数，超过了当前最先进的91.8%。在BraTS 2020数据集上，所提出的GCSER-UNet集成方法在肿瘤区域——全肿瘤（W）、肿瘤核心（T）和增强肿瘤（E）上分别取得了95%、92%和90%的Dice分数，而当前最先进方法的Dice分数分别为94%、93%和88%。这些令人信服的结果突显了GCSER-UNet在精确脑肿瘤分割中的有效性，从而能够辅助神经科医生进行有效的脑癌管理和治疗规划。

</details>


### [7] [OmniMem: Scalable and Adaptive Memory Retrieval for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30519)
*Lin Zhao,Yushu Wu,Yifan Gong,Yanzhi Wang,Pu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniMem提出了一种显式全范围记忆检索框架，通过稀疏KV检索解决自回归视频生成中长序列历史缓存访问成本高、局部偏差和内存膨胀问题，在长视频生成任务中动态度提升52.3%，同时保持强一致性和可比内存占用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归视频生成模型在生成长视频时需要重复访问不断增长的历史KV缓存，现有方法通过截断KV缓存或压缩为隐式记忆来降低开销，但这两种方式都丢失了对查询相关历史细节的显式访问能力，导致长视频生成质量受限。

Method: OmniMem包含三个关键设计：(i) 自适应窗口排除（Adaptive Window Exclusion）——当足够的长程历史可用时，从选择候选中移除局部窗口块，将稀疏预算留给信息丰富的长程检索；(ii) 查询共享KV选择（Query-Shared KV Selection）——降低跨查询的多样性；(iii) 逐头分散KV访问（Per-Head Scattered KV Access）——避免将头特定的选择扩展到大的选定KV缓冲区，允许每个注意力头根据自身选择模式检索非连续的KV块。

Result: 在长视频生成实验上，OmniMem将动态度（Dynamic Degree）提升了52.3%，同时保持了与强基线相比的强一致性和可比的内存占用。

Conclusion: OmniMem通过显式全范围记忆检索框架，有效解决了自回归视频生成中长序列历史缓存访问的效率和效果问题，在保持内存开销可控的前提下显著提升了生成视频的动态性和一致性。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video generation extends videos by producing latent chunks sequentially, but scaling to long videos requires repeated access to a growing historical KV cache. Existing methods reduce this cost by truncating the KV cache or compressing it into implicit memory, but both lose explicit access to query-relevant historical details. We propose OmniMem, an explicit full-range memory retrieval framework that performs sparse KV retrieval over the historical cache. To make this practical for chunk-based AR video generation, OmniMem addresses two issues: (i) local bias in sparse KV selection and (ii) Union Explosion in memory access. Adaptive Window Exclusion removes local-window blocks from the selection candidates when sufficient long-range history is available, preserving the sparse budget for informative long-range retrieval. Query-Shared KV Selection reduces cross-query diversity, while Per-Head Scattered KV Access avoids expanding head-specific selections into a large selected KV buffer. This allows each attention head to retrieve non-contiguous KV blocks according to its own selection pattern. Experiments on long-video generation show that OmniMem improves Dynamic Degree by 52.3% and preserves strong consistency over strong baselines, while maintaining comparable memory usage.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归（AR）视频生成通过顺序生成潜在块来扩展视频，但扩展到长视频需要重复访问不断增长的历史KV缓存。现有方法通过截断KV缓存或将其压缩为隐式记忆来降低开销，但两者都丢失了对查询相关历史细节的显式访问能力。我们提出OmniMem，一个显式全范围记忆检索框架，它对历史缓存执行稀疏KV检索。为了使该方法适用于基于块的自回归视频生成，OmniMem解决了两个问题：(i) 稀疏KV选择中的局部偏差和(ii) 记忆访问中的联合爆炸问题。自适应窗口排除在足够的长程历史可用时，从选择候选中移除局部窗口块，将稀疏预算保留给信息丰富的长程检索。查询共享KV选择降低了跨查询的多样性，而逐头分散KV访问避免了将头特定的选择扩展到大的选定KV缓冲区。这使得每个注意力头能够根据自身的选择模式检索非连续的KV块。长视频生成实验表明，OmniMem在保持可比内存占用的同时，相比强基线将动态度提升了52.3%并保持了强一致性。

</details>


### [8] [Seeing Isn't Knowing: Do VLMs Know When Not to Answer Spatial Questions (and Why)?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30557)
*Yue Zhang,Zun Wang,Han Lin,Yonatan Bitton,Idan Szpektor,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialUncertain评估框架，发现现有VLM空间推理基准忽略观察缺陷（遮挡和视角模糊），导致模型在信息不充分时过度自信作答，准确率极低，呼吁关注模型何时应弃权及如何寻求可靠证据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有空间推理基准默认观测数据充分可靠，仅关注模型能否给出正确答案，忽略了模型是否能在信息不完整或误导时识别问题不可回答并主动弃权。本文旨在挑战这一假设。

Method: 构建SpatialUncertain控制评估框架，引入两种观测挑战：(1) 遮挡——隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角模糊——产生误导性视觉线索。为每种配置设计在清晰观测下可回答、但在挑战下需弃权的空间问题，并评估模型能否识别哪些额外视角可消除模糊性。

Result: 在多个前沿开源和闭源VLM上发现两类一致失败模式：(1) 模型易过度自信作答，遮挡下平均准确率约30%，视角模糊下低于10%；(2) 即使提供额外视角，部分模型在识别哪些视角提供可靠证据时接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁从仅关注答案正确性转向评估模型是否知道何时弃权以及如何寻求可靠证据。

Original Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for vision-language models (VLMs) deployed in real-world environments. However, visual observations are inherently limited representations of a 3D world: occlusion can render objects invisible, and perspective can make geometric properties misleading. Despite this, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks typically assume that observations are sufficient and reliable, focusing on whether models produce correct answers rather than whether they recognize when a question cannot be answered and what additional observations would be needed. In this work, we challenge this assumption by constructing a controlled evaluation framework, SpatialUncertain, and introducing two types of observation challenges: (1) occlusion, which hides target information, and (2) perspective ambiguity, which produces misleading visual cues. For each configuration, we design spatial questions that are answerable under clean observations but require abstention under the introduced challenges. We further evaluate whether models can identify which additional viewpoints would resolve perspective ambiguity. Our results across a diverse set of frontier open- and closed-source VLMs reveal two consistent failure modes. First, models are prone to overconfident answering, attempting to solve spatial reasoning tasks even when visual evidence is incomplete or misleading, with average accuracy around 30\% under occlusion and below 10\% under perspective ambiguity. Second, even when additional views are available, some models perform near random chance in identifying which would provide reliable evidence. Together, our findings call for moving beyond answer correctness toward evaluating whether models know when to abstain and how to seek reliable evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 空间推理是部署在真实环境中的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的一项基本能力。然而，视觉观察本质上是对三维世界的有限表征：遮挡可能使物体不可见，透视可能使几何属性具有误导性。尽管如此，现有的空间推理基准通常假设观察是充分且可靠的，关注模型能否给出正确回答，而非模型是否能够识别何时问题无法被回答以及需要哪些额外的观察。在本工作中，我们挑战了这一假设，构建了一个受控评估框架SpatialUncertain，并引入了两类观察挑战：(1) 遮挡，隐藏目标信息；(2) 视角模糊，产生误导性的视觉线索。针对每种配置，我们设计了在清晰观测下可回答、但在所引入的挑战下需要弃权的空间问题。我们进一步评估了模型能否识别哪些额外视角可以消除视角模糊。我们在多种前沿开源和闭源VLM上的实验结果揭示了两类一致的失败模式。首先，模型倾向于过度自信地作答，即使在视觉证据不完整或具有误导性时仍试图解决空间推理任务，在遮挡下平均准确率约为30%，在视角模糊下低于10%。其次，即使提供额外视角，某些模型在识别哪些视角能提供可靠证据时表现接近随机水平。综合来看，我们的研究结果呼吁从仅关注答案正确性转向评估模型是否知道何时弃权以及如何寻求可靠证据。

</details>


### [9] [VLM3: Vision Language Models Are Native 3D Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30561)
*Zhipeng Cai,Zhuang Liu,Yunyang Xiong,Zechun Liu,Vikas Chandra,Yangyang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM3，通过焦距统一、文本像素参考和数据混合缩放三种简单技术，使标准视觉语言模型无需复杂架构设计即可掌握多种3D任务，表明VLM本质上是原生3D学习者。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前3D理解仍严重依赖专家视觉模型和复杂任务特定设计，但视觉语言模型（VLM）在语义理解上已显示出巨大潜力。本文旨在论证VLM本身就是原生3D学习者，无需复杂模型架构、大规模模型、繁重数据增强或复杂损失函数即可有效进行3D学习。

Method: 提出VLM3方法，核心包括三要素：1）焦距统一（focal length unification）；2）基于文本的像素参考（text-based pixel reference）；3）数据混合与缩放（data mixture and scaling）。该方法保持标准VLM架构和基于文本的训练形式，不依赖模型架构改变、大模型、数据增强、复杂损失或回归公式。

Result: VLM3在深度估计精度上大幅提升（0.84 -> 0.9），同时实现了像素对应、相机姿态估计和物体级3D理解等多种3D任务，精度媲美专家视觉模型，且保持了标准架构和基于文本的训练。

Conclusion: VLM3开创了一种简单且可扩展的3D学习新范式，证明VLMs是原生3D学习者，为统一、简洁的3D视觉理解开辟了新方向。

Original Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) enable a unified model to solve various vision tasks through prompting. They have shown promising performance in semantic understanding. However, 3D understanding still largely relies on expert vision models with complex task-specific designs. The key argument this work wants to make is that VLMs are native 3D learners. Our in-depth large scale study shows that 1) focal length unification, 2) text-based pixel reference and 3) data mixture and scaling, are all you need for effective 3D learning. Model architecture changes, large models, heavy data augmentations, and complex losses including the regression formulation, many of which form the foundation of expert vision models, are actually not necessary conditions. As a result, we propose VLM3, a scalable method with the simplest design that enables standard VLMs to master diverse 3D tasks. VLM3 not only advances the VLM depth estimation accuracy by a large margin (0.84 -> 0.9), but also enables diverse 3D tasks such as pixel correspondence, camera pose estimation and object-level 3D understanding, matching expert vision model accuracy while maintaining standard architectures and text-based training. We believe VLM3 opens up a new paradigm for simple and scalable 3D learning.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）能够通过提示（prompting）以统一模型解决多种视觉任务。它们在语义理解方面已展现出令人瞩目的性能。然而，3D理解仍然在很大程度上依赖于具有复杂任务特定设计的专家视觉模型。本文旨在论证的关键观点是：视觉语言模型本质上是原生的3D学习者。我们深入的大规模研究表明：1）焦距统一、2）基于文本的像素参考、3）数据混合与缩放，三者即构成有效3D学习的全部必要条件。模型架构改变、大模型、繁重数据增强以及包括回归公式在内的复杂损失——这些构成专家视觉模型基础的技术——实际上并非必要条件。基于此，我们提出VLM3，一种采用最简单设计的可扩展方法，使标准视觉语言模型能够掌握多样化的3D任务。VLM3不仅将VLM深度估计精度大幅提升（0.84 -> 0.9），还实现了像素对应、相机姿态估计和物体级3D理解等多种3D任务，在保持标准架构和基于文本训练的同时，达到了与专家视觉模型相媲美的精度。我们相信，VLM3为简单且可扩展的3D学习开辟了一种新范式。

</details>


### [10] [Prior Availability in Industrial Visual Sim-to-Real: A Review of CAD-Guided and CAD-Unavailable Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30581)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述将工业视觉仿真到真实迁移重新定义为基于先验可获取性的域差距问题，系统划分了CAD可获取、CAD不可获取和边界先验三类场景，并连接了通常独立评述的CAD基检测/位姿估计与工业异常检测/表面检测文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工业视觉sim-to-real研究多聚焦于合成图像到真实图像的迁移，但工业部署中存在更广泛的证据与决策不匹配问题（如传感器、光照、材料、标定、生产变异、罕见缺陷等）。同时，基于CAD的方法与工业异常/表面检测方法通常被分开评述，缺乏统一的梳理框架。

Method: 提出以先验可获取性为核心的组织框架，将工业视觉sim-to-real划分为三类：(1) CAD可获取设置——利用显式物体几何进行渲染、标定、位姿估计、分割和测试时几何验证；(2) CAD不可获取设置——用法向参考外观、特征分布、师生残差、合成异常假设、基础模型特征或视觉-语言先验替代几何；(3) 边界先验设置——近似模型、模板、参考视图或语义对应仅保留部分CAD作用。并在T-LESS/BOP、MVTec AD和VisA数据集上设置经验锚点进行实证。

Result: 经验锚点表明：CAD渲染数量本身不能缩小迁移差距；源域分布设计、检测器容量和少量真实标定更为关键。测试时CAD提供了通过掩码、位姿和深度一致性构成的独特验证通道；而CAD不可获取的检测依赖校准的正态性和特征偏差。

Conclusion: 反对使用单一跨任务排行榜进行评估，主张根据哪些先验支撑了部署决策来评估方法。

Original Abstract: Industrial visual sim-to-real is often described as transferring from synthetic images to real images, but industrial deployment usually involves a broader mismatch between available evidence and required decisions. A system may be built from CAD renderings, simulated RGB-D observations, normal reference images, synthetic defects, pretrained feature spaces, or language prompts, yet deployed under different sensors, lighting, materials, fixtures, calibration, production variation, and rare defect modes. This review reframes industrial visual sim-to-real as a domain-gap problem organized by prior availability. We distinguish CAD-available settings, where explicit object geometry can support rendering, calibration, pose estimation, segmentation, and test-time geometric verification; CAD-unavailable settings, where geometry is replaced by normal-reference appearance, feature distributions, teacher-student residuals, synthetic anomaly assumptions, foundation features, or vision-language priors; and boundary-prior settings, where approximate models, templates, reference views, or semantic correspondences preserve only part of the CAD role. This framing connects CAD-based detection and 6D pose-estimation literature with industrial anomaly and surface-inspection literature that is usually reviewed separately. To make the taxonomy concrete, we use empirical anchors on T-LESS/BOP, MVTec AD, and VisA. The anchors show that CAD render count alone does not close transfer; source-distribution design, detector capacity, and small real calibration can matter more. They also show that CAD at test time creates a distinct verification channel through mask, pose, and depth consistency, whereas CAD-unavailable inspection relies on calibrated normality and feature deviation. The review therefore argues against a single cross-task leaderboard and instead asks what prior grounds the deployment decision.

中文摘要翻译: 工业视觉的仿真到真实迁移通常被描述为从合成图像到真实图像的迁移，但工业部署往往涉及可用证据与所需决策之间更广泛的错配。一个系统可能基于CAD渲染、模拟RGB-D观测、法向参考图像、合成缺陷、预训练特征空间或语言提示构建，却在不同的传感器、光照、材料、夹具、标定、生产变异和罕见缺陷模式下部署。本综述将工业视觉仿真到真实迁移重新定义为按先验可获取性组织的域差距问题。我们区分了三种设置：CAD可获取设置，其中显式的物体几何可以支持渲染、标定、位姿估计、分割和测试时几何验证；CAD不可获取设置，其中几何被法向参考外观、特征分布、师生残差、合成异常假设、基础模型特征或视觉-语言先验所取代；以及边界先验设置，其中近似模型、模板、参考视图或语义对应仅保留部分CAD作用。这一框架将基于CAD的检测和6D位姿估计文献与通常被单独评述的工业异常和表面检测文献联系起来。为使该分类具体化，我们在T-LESS/BOP、MVTec AD和VisA上设置了经验锚点。这些锚点表明：仅靠CAD渲染数量并不能弥合迁移差距；源域分布设计、检测器容量和小规模真实标定可能更为关键。它们还表明，测试时的CAD通过掩码、位姿和深度一致性创建了独特的验证通道，而CAD不可获取的检测则依赖校准的正态性和特征偏差。因此，本综述反对使用单一的跨任务排行榜，而是提出应评估什么先验支撑了部署决策。

</details>


### [11] [ReGuLaR: Relation-Grounded Latent Reasoning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30587)
*Zihu Wang,Karthik Somayaji N. S,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReGuLaR 是一个关系接地潜在推理框架，通过显式地将潜在状态与视觉证据中的对象和对象间关系对齐，提升了大型视觉语言模型在复杂视觉推理任务上的表现，并构建了 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有链式思维推理依赖离散文本 rationale，难以编码连续视觉证据；而连续的潜在推理方法又未能充分利用视觉证据中组合性和关系结构。作者希望弥合潜在推理与视觉关系结构之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 ReGuLaR 框架，包含：(1) 训练阶段的 ReGFormer，专注于问题相关目标及目标间关系来指导潜在推理；(2) 推理阶段移除 ReGFormer，仅靠模型自身进行推理和生成答案；(3) 构建了 RGROUNDING-351K 数据集，标注了关键目标边界框和关系。

Result: 在多个不同基准上的大量实验表明，ReGuLaR 一致优于现有方法，取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 基于关系的潜在推理能够有效弥补视觉证据中关系和组合结构的不足，且训练时引入关系引导模块能在不增加推理开销的情况下显著提升性能。

Original Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly improved the reasoning ability of large vision-language models (LVLMs) by verbalizing intermediate reasoning steps in natural language. However, such discrete textual rationales are often insufficient for encoding continuous visual evidence. Recent work addresses this limitation by moving reasoning into continuous latent space. Despite promising progress, existing methods leave latent reasoning insufficiently connected to the compositional and relational structure of visual evidence. To address this gap, we introduce ReGuLaR, a relation grounded latent reasoning framework that explicitly grounds latent states in these critical yet overlooked visual evidence. ReGuLaR uses a training-time ReGFormer to focus latent reasoning on question-relevant objects and inter-object relations, while at inference time the model reasons and generates answers without invoking the ReGFormer. To support training ReGuLaR, we construct RGROUNDING-351K, a real-world vision-language dataset annotated with key object bounding boxes and inter-object relations. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks show that ReGuLaR consistently outperforms existing approaches and achieves state-of-the-art performance. We include our code in the submission and will release the code and training data publicly upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 链式思维推理通过以自然语言形式表达中间推理步骤，显著提升了大视觉语言模型的推理能力。然而，这种离散的文本 rationale 常常不足以编码连续的视觉证据。近期工作通过将推理移入连续潜在空间来应对这一局限。尽管取得了有前景的进展，现有方法仍使潜在推理与视觉证据的组合性和关系结构之间的连接不够充分。为解决这一问题，我们提出了 ReGuLaR，一个关系接地潜在推理框架，它将潜在状态显式地接地到这些关键但常被忽视的视觉证据上。ReGuLaR 使用训练阶段的 ReGFormer 将潜在推理聚焦于与问题相关的目标及目标间关系，而在推理阶段模型自行推理并生成答案，无需调用 ReGFormer。为支持 ReGuLaR 的训练，我们构建了 RGROUNDING-351K，一个标注有关键目标边界框和目标间关系的真实世界视觉语言数据集。在多个不同基准上的大量实验表明，ReGuLaR 一致优于现有方法，并取得了最先进的性能。我们将代码包含在提交中，并在论文被接收后公开发布代码和训练数据。

</details>


### [12] [Controllable Lung Nodule Synthesis via Histogram-Regularized Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30631)
*Arunkumar Kannan,Yanbo Zhang,Han Liu,Michael Baumgartner,Jianing Wang,Alexander Hertel,Bogdan Georgescu,Sasa Grbic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可控潜在扩散模型，用于在3D CT体积中合成肺结节。该模型引入基于直方图的强度分布正则化项，以更好地约束结节亚型特异性强度分布，生成更逼真、亚型一致的合成结节，并提升下游临床任务（尤其是少数亚型）的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的肺结节合成方法主要优化空间重建损失，鼓励体素级相似性，但未能充分约束病灶级别的强度分布，导致纹理过度平滑且不同结节亚型（实性、部分实性、磨玻璃结节）的衰减特征区分不足。

Method: 提出可控潜在扩散模型，在生成过程中结合了亚型条件、空间掩膜条件和亨氏单位（HU）直方图条件，并引入可微的特征空间直方图正则化项，而非仅依赖空间损失，以更好地对齐病灶级强度分布。

Result: 在肺CT数据上的实验表明，该方法在定量指标和视觉图灵测试中均实现了强视觉真实感；用于数据增强时，能提升下游临床任务性能，尤其对少数结节亚型有效，并显示出对亚型感知的恶性肿瘤分类的潜在益处。

Conclusion: 所提出的直方图正则化条件生成模型能有效合成视觉逼真、亚型一致的肺结节，缓解数据稀缺问题，并提升下游任务性能。

Original Abstract: While automated diagnosis systems have achieved remarkable success in computed tomography (CT)-based lung cancer screening, their development remains limited by the scarcity of diverse, annotated pulmonary nodule datasets. Diffusion-based generative models offer a promising strategy for data synthesis; however, many existing conditional approaches primarily optimize spatial reconstruction losses, which encourage voxel-wise similarity but may inadequately constrain lesion-level intensity distributions. As a result, these methods may produce over-smoothed texture profiles and underrepresent the distinct attenuation characteristics of different nodule subtypes, including solid, part-solid, and ground-glass nodules. To address this challenge, we propose a controllable latent diffusion model that synthesizes pulmonary nodules within full 3D CT volumes while accurately modeling nodule-specific intensity distributions. Specifically, rather than relying solely on spatial losses, we introduce a histogram-based regularization term that constrains voxel intensity distributions during the generative process. The model combines subtype, spatial mask, and Hounsfield unit (HU) histogram conditioning with the differentiable feature-space histogram regularization term to better align lesion-level intensity distributions, improving the visual plausibility and subtype consistency of synthesized nodules. Extensive experiments on lung CT data demonstrate that our framework achieves strong visual realism, validated through both quantitative metrics and a visual Turing test. Furthermore, when used for data augmentation, the generated nodules improve performance in downstream clinical tasks, particularly for underrepresented nodule subtypes, and show a potential benefit for subtype-informed malignancy classification.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管自动诊断系统在基于计算机断层扫描（CT）的肺癌筛查中取得了显著成功，但其发展仍受到多样化、标注完善的肺结节数据集稀缺的限制。基于扩散的生成模型为数据合成提供了一种有前景的策略；然而，许多现有的条件方法主要优化空间重建损失，这类损失鼓励体素级相似性，但可能不足以约束病灶级别的强度分布。因此，这些方法可能产生过度平滑的纹理特征，并低估不同结节亚型（包括实性结节、部分实性结节和磨玻璃结节）的不同衰减特征。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种可控潜在扩散模型，该模型能在完整3D CT体积中合成肺结节，同时精确建模结节特异性的强度分布。具体而言，我们并非仅依赖空间损失，而是在生成过程中引入了一种基于直方图的正则化项来约束体素强度分布。该模型将亚型、空间掩膜和亨氏单位（HU）直方图条件与可微的特征空间直方图正则化项相结合，以更好地对齐病灶级强度分布，从而提升合成结节的视觉真实感和亚型一致性。在肺CT数据上的大量实验表明，我们的框架通过定量指标和视觉图灵测试均验证了其强大的视觉真实感。此外，当用于数据增强时，生成的结节能提升下游临床任务的性能，尤其对于代表性不足的结节亚型，并显示出对亚型感知的恶性肿瘤分类的潜在益处。

</details>


### [13] [PInVerify: An Offline Embodied Benchmark for Active Instance Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30639)
*Yuhang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出主动实例验证（AIV）任务，要求智能体围绕候选物体主动选择视点，判断其是否匹配细粒度自然语言描述。作者构建了离线基准PInVerify（3000个场景、18类物体），并基于开源多模态大模型（≤8B参数）搭建了无需训练和LoRA微调的基线模型，实验表明最佳MLLM基线超过最佳嵌入基线4.9个百分点，LoRA微调智能体达到85.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有具身智能体在导航到目标物体方面取得了进展，但到达目标附近并不意味着智能体找到了正确的实例：细微的属性差异（如“白色花卉” vs “白色条纹”）通常需要近距离、多视角的检查。现有工作缺乏对这一“主动细粒度语义验证”环节的系统研究。

Method: 将AIV形式化为有限时域决策过程；构建PInVerify离线基准（包含6扇区导航拓扑、陷阱视图和不可达扇区）；搭建两类基线：无训练流水线和LoRA微调端到端智能体，均基于≤8B的开源MLLM；设计了属性分解、可见性加权多视角追踪器以及三种下一最佳视点（NBV）策略。

Result: 最佳MLLM基线超过最佳嵌入基线4.9个百分点；GT框消融显示+3.1个百分点的检测差距；在测试的NBV策略中未观察到主动视点选择的可靠增益；LoRA微调智能体（SFT+GSPO）达到85.6%。

Conclusion: PInVerify基准和基线为主动细粒度语义验证这一具身AI重要环节提供了参考基准，有望支持后续研究。目前主动视点选择策略尚无明显收益，表明该问题仍需进一步探索。

Original Abstract: Embodied agents have made strong progress in navigating to target objects, but reaching the goal vicinity does not guarantee that the agent has found the correct instance: subtle attribute differences (e.g., "white floral" vs. "white striped") often require close-range, multi-view inspection. We address this gap with Active Instance Verification (AIV), a task in which an agent actively selects viewpoints around a candidate object to decide whether it matches a fine-grained natural-language description. We formalize AIV as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce PInVerify, an offline embodied benchmark for AIV: 3,000 evaluation episodes across 18 object categories, delivered as multi-view captures with a 6-sector navigation topology that exposes trap views (navigable but uninformative) and unreachable sectors. As reference baselines we build a training-free pipeline and a LoRA-fine-tuned end-to-end agent around open-source multimodal large language models (MLLMs) at on-device scale ($\leq$8B parameters), with attribute decomposition, a visibility-weighted multi-view tracker, and three next-best-view (NBV) strategies. In our evaluation across Qwen3-VL (4B/8B), SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B, CLIP, and SigLIP2, the best MLLM-based baseline exceeds the best embedding baseline by 4.9 pp; GT-box ablations show a +3.1 pp detection gap; and we do not observe reliable gains from active viewpoint selection within the tested NBV strategies. A LoRA-fine-tuned agent (SFT+GSPO) reaches 85.6%. PInVerify aims to support further work on active, fine-grained semantic verification in embodied AI. Code: https://github.com/Avalon-S/PInVerify.

中文摘要翻译: 具身智能体在导航到目标物体方面取得了显著进展，但到达目标附近并不能保证智能体找到了正确的实例：细微的属性差异（如"白色花卉"与"白色条纹"）通常需要近距离、多视角的检查。针对这一不足，我们提出了主动实例验证（Active Instance Verification, AIV）任务，要求智能体围绕候选物体主动选择视点，以判断其是否匹配细粒度的自然语言描述。我们将AIV形式化为一个有限时域决策过程，并引入PInVerify——一个面向AIV的离线具身基准：包含18个物体类别、3000个评估场景，以多视角捕获形式呈现，并设计了6扇区导航拓扑，其中包含陷阱视图（可导航但无信息量）和不可达扇区。作为参考基线，我们构建了无需训练的流水线以及基于开源多模态大语言模型（MLLM，参数量≤8B，适用于设备端部署）的LoRA微调端到端智能体，集成了属性分解、可见性加权多视角追踪器以及三种下一最佳视点（NBV）策略。在Qwen3-VL（4B/8B）、SenseNova-SI-1.2-InternVL3-8B、CLIP和SigLIP2上的评估表明，最佳MLLM基线的性能超过最佳嵌入基线4.9个百分点；GT框消融实验显示存在+3.1个百分点的检测差距；在测试的NBV策略中，我们未观察到主动视点选择带来的可靠增益。LoRA微调智能体（SFT+GSPO）达到了85.6%的准确率。PInVerify旨在为具身AI中主动、细粒度的语义验证提供进一步研究支持。代码：https://github.com/Avalon-S/PInVerify。

</details>


### [14] [WristCompass: Kinematic Coupling as a Learnable Visual Concept for Ego-Camera Orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30671)
*Varun Nair,Vidyut Baradwaj,Jiahang He,Anya Singh,Jai Relan,Cabrel Happi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WristCompass，利用手腕间运动学耦合动力学（而非场景几何）来恢复第一人称视角相机朝向，仅用20万参数GRU即可在零样本迁移下达到接近10亿参数场景模型的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从操作视频中恢复第一人称相机朝向是解耦手与相机运动的前提，但场景几何方法在手部严重遮挡时失效（如TACO基准上VGGT模型不如常数预测器）。作者需要找到一种在场景几何缺失时仍然存在的替代视觉线索。

Method: 利用手臂-肩膀-头部运动链带来的腕部运动与相机朝向之间的运动学耦合动力学。提取4维腕间特征（代替126维全手关键点），通过GRU在短时间窗口内建模时序依赖，实现零样本跨数据集迁移。

Result: 仅在桌面操作数据上训练，WristCompass零样本迁移到Epic Kitchens烹饪视频，中位测地线误差14.3°，以20万GRU参数达到接近10亿参数场景模型VGGT的性能。

Conclusion: 运动学耦合动力学是一种紧凑、时序依赖且物理可迁移的视觉概念，为场景几何缺失下的相机朝向估计提供了有效且参数高效的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Recovering ego-camera orientation from manipulation video is a prerequisite for disentangling hand motion from camera motion, a key step in imitation learning from egocentric demonstrations. The obvious approach, inferring orientation from scene geometry, fails when hands occlude the frame: VGGT, a 1B-parameter scene reconstruction model, scores worse than a constant predictor on the TACO benchmark. We identify an alternative visual concept that is present precisely when scene geometry is absent: kinematic coupling dynamics, the structured physical relationship between wrist motion and camera orientation imposed by the arm-shoulder-head chain. We find that this concept is compact (4D inter-wrist features outperform 126D full hand keypoints), temporal (requiring a GRU over short windows rather than per-frame retrieval), and physically grounded (transferring zero-shot across datasets because it is rooted in anatomy rather than scene appearance). Trained only on tabletop manipulation, WristCompass transfers zero-shot to Epic Kitchens cooking video, achieving 14.3$^\circ$ median geodesic error and approaching the performance of a 1B-parameter scene model at 200K GRU parameters.

中文摘要翻译: 从操作视频中恢复第一人称相机的朝向，是解耦手部运动与相机运动的前提，也是从自我中心演示中进行模仿学习的关键步骤。一个直观的方法——从场景几何推断朝向——在手部遮挡画面时会失效：VGGT，一个10亿参数的场景重建模型，在TACO基准上的表现甚至不如常数预测器。我们发现了一个替代的视觉概念，它恰好出现在场景几何缺失之时：运动学耦合动力学，即由手臂-肩膀-头部运动链施加的腕部运动与相机朝向之间的结构化物理关系。我们发现这一概念是紧凑的（4维腕间特征优于126维全手关键点）、时序依赖的（需要短窗口上的GRU而非逐帧检索），并且具有物理可迁移性（由于根植于解剖结构而非场景外观，可在数据集间零样本迁移）。仅在桌面操作数据上训练，WristCompass便可零样本迁移到Epic Kitchens烹饪视频，达到14.3°的中位测地线误差，以20万GRU参数逼近10亿参数场景模型的性能。

</details>


### [15] [Seeing Before Agreeing: Aligning Multi-Agent Consensus with Visual Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30698)
*Yuhan Wang,Shuochen Chang,Yalin Feng,Dongsheng Ma,Yuanzi Li,Zhengren Wang,Yinglong Yang,Yufei Chen,Yikang Wang,Shaoxu Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EAGLE框架，通过暴露各VLM智能体的视觉证据（grounding regions）并进行证据对齐与相互验证，实现无需训练的多智能体VQA协作，显著提升可靠性和性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多智能体VQA方法主要沿用文本中心协议，聚焦于文本讨论而忽略视觉信息对齐；仅答案级一致不足以实现可靠的VQA，需要对齐的视觉证据（智能体依赖的图像区域支持）才能达成可信共识。

Method: 提出EAGLE（Evidence-Aligned Grounded Multi-agent Reasoning），一个无需训练的证据中心框架：显式暴露每个VLM智能体的grounding区域作为视觉证据，实现智能体间证据的相互验证，并利用证据一致性指导最终决策。

Result: 在六个VQA基准上，EAGLE在跨领域平均性能上达到最佳，同时保持轻量、可解释且易于部署。

Conclusion: 对齐的视觉证据是多智能体VQA可靠共识的关键；EAGLE通过证据中心的多智能体协调范式，在无需额外训练的情况下有效提升了VQA性能与可信度。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual question answering (VQA). To mitigate individual hallucinations and blind spots, aggregating diverse perspectives via multi-agent collaboration has emerged as a promising paradigm. While this approach has shown great success in textual QA, its potential in the multimodal domain remains under-explored. Existing multi-agent VQA methods predominantly adapt text-centric protocols, focusing on textual discussions while ignoring the alignment of visual information. In this work, we reveal a key insight: answer-level agreement is insufficient for reliable multi-agent VQA; \textit{aligned visual evidence} -- shared support from the image regions agents rely on -- is essential for trustworthy consensus. To leverage this insight, we propose EAGLE (\textbf{E}vidence-\textbf{A}ligned \textbf{G}rounded mu\textbf{L}ti-agent r\textbf{E}asoning), a training-free evidence-centered framework for coordinating multiple VLM agents. EAGLE explicitly exposes each agent's grounding regions as visual evidence, enables mutual verification over the evidence, and uses evidence consistency to guide final decision-making. Experiments on six VQA benchmarks show that EAGLE achieves best average performance across domains while remaining lightweight, interpretable, and practical for deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在视觉问答（VQA）上取得了强劲性能。为缓解个体幻觉和盲点，通过多智能体协作聚合多元视角已成为一种有前景的范式。尽管该方法在文本问答中取得了巨大成功，其在多模态领域的潜力仍未得到充分探索。现有的多智能体VQA方法主要沿用以文本为中心的协议，侧重于文本讨论而忽视了视觉信息的对齐。本文揭示了一个关键见解：答案级的一致对于可靠的多智能体VQA是不够的；对齐的视觉证据——即各智能体所依赖的图像区域的共享支持——对于达成可信共识至关重要。基于这一见解，我们提出EAGLE（证据对齐的接地多智能体推理），一个无需训练的证据中心框架，用于协调多个VLM智能体。EAGLE显式暴露每个智能体的接地区域作为视觉证据，实现证据上的相互验证，并利用证据一致性指导最终决策。在六个VQA基准上的实验表明，EAGLE在跨领域平均性能上达到最佳，同时保持轻量、可解释且易于部署。

</details>


### [16] [Mathematical Morphology in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30700)
*Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Aura Conci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将数学形态学引入机器学习，提出了基于形态学重建的快速聚类算法、一种新颖的距离度量（结合闵可夫斯基和切比雪夫距离）以及新型形态学分类器，有效利用了数据的形状、密度和分形信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准机器学习技术常忽略数据的形状和密度方面，而数学形态学作为成熟的视觉计算理论，可弥补这一不足。

Method: (1) 基于形态学重建的快速聚类算法，可准确保留聚类形状和密度，具有内在最大聚类感知、无代价噪声去除及由结构元素控制的多样化生长模式；(2) 结合闵可夫斯基和切比雪夫距离的新距离度量，对形态学膨胀高度高效；(3) 新型形态学分类器，独特地建模数据中的形状、密度和分形信息。

Result: 在Z²离散邻域迭代中，新距离度量比曼哈顿距离快约1.3倍，比欧氏距离快约329.5倍；在33个UCI数据集上使用k-NN分类器评估，该度量在26个案例中达到高于平均的准确率，在9个案例中取得最佳总体准确率。

Conclusion: 将数学形态学引入机器学习可有效利用形状和密度信息，所提出的聚类算法、距离度量和分类器在效率和准确性上均表现优异。

Original Abstract: This work introduces mathematical morphology-an established visual computing theory-into machine learning to exploit shape and density aspects often overlooked by standard techniques. We propose a fast clustering algorithm based on morphological reconstruction that accurately preserves cluster shapes and density. This scheme offers unique features: an intrinsic sense of maximal clusters, cost-free noise removal, and diverse growth patterns controlled by structuring elements.Additionally, we propose a novel distance metric combining Minkowski and Chebyshev distances, highly efficient for morphological dilations. In $Z^2$ discrete neighbourhood iterations, it is roughly 1.3 times faster than Manhattan and 329.5 times faster than Euclidean distances. When evaluated using a k-Nearest Neighbours (k-NN) classifier across 33 UCI datasets against 14 other distances, our metric achieved above-average accuracies most frequently (26 of 33 cases) and the best overall accuracy in 9 cases.Finally, we introduce novel morphological classifiers. Unlike current literature, this proposal uniquely models shape, density, and fractal information in datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 本工作将数学形态学——一种成熟的视觉计算理论——引入机器学习，以利用标准技术常忽略的形状和密度方面。我们提出了一种基于形态学重建的快速聚类算法，该算法能准确保留聚类形状和密度。该方案具有独特特点：内在的最大聚类感知、无代价的噪声去除，以及由结构元素控制的多样化生长模式。此外，我们提出了一种结合闵可夫斯基距离和切比雪夫距离的新型距离度量，对形态学膨胀操作极为高效。在Z²离散邻域迭代中，它比曼哈顿距离快约1.3倍，比欧氏距离快约329.5倍。在33个UCI数据集上使用k-最近邻（k-NN）分类器与14种其他距离度量进行评估时，我们的度量在26个案例中获得了高于平均的准确率（共33个案例），并在9个案例中取得了最佳总体准确率。最后，我们引入了新型形态学分类器。与现有文献不同，该方案独特地建模了数据集中的形状、密度和分形信息。

</details>


### [17] [Equivariant Latent Alignment via Flow Matching under Group Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30705)
*Sunghyun Kim,Jaehoon Hahm,Jeongwoo Shin,Joonseok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有几何感知生成模型和新视角合成方法中存在的潜在空间不对齐问题（即预期群作用与潜在空间中实际所需变换不一致），提出残差潜在流（Residual Latent Flow）框架，通过流模型纠正不对齐的潜在表示，提升等变关系一致性，显著减少潜在不对齐并提升SO(n)旋转群下的新视角合成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在构建等变潜在空间时，常面临潜在不对齐问题——即潜在空间中预期的群作用与实际所需的变换之间存在差异，导致学到的潜在表示无法一致地保持群对称性所施加的等变关系，从而限制了新视角合成的视觉一致性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出残差潜在流（Residual Latent Flow），一种基于流的框架，通过学习一个可逆的残差变换来修正不对齐的潜在表示，使其更好地满足底层等变关系。

Result: 在SO(n)旋转群下的综合实验表明，该方法显著降低了潜在不对齐程度，并有效提升了新视角合成的质量。

Conclusion: Residual Latent Flow能够有效纠正潜在空间中的等变不对齐问题，为几何感知生成模型和新视角合成提供了一种改进等变一致性的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Geometry-aware generative models and novel view synthesis approaches have shown strong potential in visual fidelity and consistency. In parallel, equivariant representation learning has emerged as a powerful framework for constructing latent spaces where analytically known group transformations could act directly, capturing geometric structure in data and enhancing both interpretability and generalization in novel view synthesis. However, we identify that existing approaches often suffer from latent misalignment, a discrepancy between the intended group action and the actually required transformations in the latent space. Consequently, the learned latents often fail to consistently preserve the equivariant relations imposed by the underlying group symmetry. To address this, we propose Residual Latent Flow, a flow-based framework that corrects the misaligned latents, thereby improving compliance with the underlying equivariance relation. Our comprehensive experiments show that our method significantly reduces latent misalignment and improves novel view synthesis quality, under rotation groups SO(n).

中文摘要翻译: 几何感知生成模型和新视角合成方法在视觉保真度和一致性方面展现出强大的潜力。同时，等变表示学习已成为一种构建潜在空间的有力框架，在该空间中已知的群变换可以直接作用，从而捕获数据中的几何结构，并增强新视角合成中的可解释性与泛化能力。然而，我们发现现有方法常存在潜在不对齐问题，即预期的群作用与潜在空间中实际所需的变换之间存在差异。因此，学到的潜在表示往往无法一致地保持由底层群对称性所施加的等变关系。为解决这一问题，我们提出残差潜在流（Residual Latent Flow），一种基于流的框架，用于纠正不对齐的潜在表示，从而提高对底层等变关系的遵循程度。我们在SO(n)旋转群下的综合实验表明，该方法显著减少潜在不对齐，并有效提升新视角合成质量。

</details>


### [18] [Vision-Based Localization in Dense Urban Environments: A Case Study of an Urban Village in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30714)
*Menglin Wu,Rui Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对城中村GPS信号差、地图数据不全导致的导航与应急困难，提出一种基于视觉的地理定位方案，采用低成本双摄像头系统采集全景与查询图像，以广州石牌村为例构建数据集，评估现有模型性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 城中村建筑密集导致GPS信号不可靠，且地图数据不完整，严重影响日常出行、最后1公里配送及应急疏散，亟需一种不依赖GPS的视觉定位方法。

Method: 提出低成本双摄像头数据采集流水线（全景相机+智能手机相机），同步捕获360度全景与查询图像；以广州石牌村为案例构建图像地理定位数据集；评估现有视觉定位模型在不同场景类型下的表现。

Result: 实验展示了视觉定位在密集城中村环境中的潜力与局限，识别了各模型在不同场景类型下的优势与不足。

Conclusion: 该视觉定位框架可增强GPS信号差区域的步行导航、最后1公里配送和应急管理能力，最终服务于城中村中的弱势群体。

Original Abstract: Urban villages, the widespread informal settlements which have emerged as a result of rapid urbanization, are now major residential hubs for migrant workers in large cities in China. The dense arrangement of buildings in these areas often leads to unreliable GPS signals, while incomplete mapping data further impairs accurate route planning and navigation. These issues not only hinder everyday mobility but also pose significant challenges for emergency response, as confusing road layouts and GPS inaccuracies can complicate evacuation efforts. To address these challenges, we propose a practical vision-based geo-localization solution tailored for dense urban environments. Our approach features a low-cost data collection pipeline utilizing a dual-camera system, comprising a panoramic camera and a smartphone camera, to capture synchronized 360-degree panoramas and query images. Using Shipai Village, a well-known densely populated urban village in Guangzhou, as a case study, we develop a specialized image geo-localization dataset. We then assess and compare the performance of existing models across various scene types to identify their strengths and weaknesses. The findings demonstrate both the potential and limitations of visual-based localization in dense urban-village environments. Our framework aims to enhance pedestrian navigation, last-mile delivery, and emergency management in areas with poor GPS coverage, ultimately supporting the vulnerable populations living within these informal settlements.

中文摘要翻译: 城中村是快速城市化过程中产生的广泛非正规住区，现已成为中国大城市外来务工人员的主要居住聚集地。这些区域建筑布局密集，常导致GPS信号不可靠，同时地图数据的不完整进一步削弱了精确路径规划与导航的能力。这些问题不仅影响日常出行，还对应急响应构成重大挑战——混乱的道路布局和GPS定位误差可能使疏散工作愈发困难。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种适用于密集城市环境的实用型视觉地理定位方案。该方法采用低成本的数采集流水线，利用包含全景相机和智能手机相机的双摄像头系统，同步捕获360度全景图像与查询图像。以广州著名的密集城中村——石牌村为案例，我们构建了专门的图像地理定位数据集，并评估和比较了现有模型在不同场景类型下的表现，以识别其各自优势与不足。研究结果展示了视觉定位在密集城中村环境中的潜力与局限性。本框架旨在增强GPS信号差区域的步行导航、最后一公里配送及应急管理能力，最终服务于这些非正规住区中的弱势群体。

</details>


### [19] [Simple Token-Efficient Vision-Language Model for Case-level Pathology Synoptic Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30716)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiahao Cheng,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的视觉-语言模型，用于从全切片图像（WSI）生成结构化的病理报告。模型采用三组件设计（冻结的病理块编码器、轻量MLP对齐器、大语言模型解码器），通过5倍放大率下的512×512块大幅降低序列长度（平均减少64倍），仅需半张H100 GPU即可训练，在多个指标上达到高分，为有限计算资源下的多WSI视觉语言模型研究提供了可复现的强基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从全切片图像生成临床可用的病理报告面临三大挑战：千兆像素分辨率导致视觉标记序列过长、病例级推理复杂度高（单个病例可能包含多个WSI，组织异质且发现模糊），以及GPU内存资源受限。现有方法难以在计算资源有限的情况下高效处理多WSI场景。

Method: 提出了一个三组件架构：1）冻结的病理块编码器；2）轻量两层MLP视觉-语言对齐器；3）大语言模型解码器，并引入WSI标记令牌来分隔病例中的不同切片。训练分两阶段：第一阶段仅训练对齐器，使用异质WSI-文本对进行WSI字幕生成；第二阶段在病例-报告对上进行病例级监督微调以生成结构化报告。为缩短序列长度，采用5倍放大率下的512×512块，相比常用的20倍放大率将平均序列长度降低最多64倍。结合高效训练技术，仅需半张NVIDIA H100 GPU即可训练。

Result: 模型在两个训练阶段均在ROUGE-L、METEOR、BLEU-4指标上取得高分，同时在内存和运行时间上大幅更高效。在基于AI的评估中，模型持续优于强基线方法。大量消融实验刻画了性能-效率的权衡，并识别出能提升多WSI场景鲁棒性的简单设计选择。

Conclusion: 本工作为高效的病理报告生成提供了一个强且可复现的基线，降低了在有限计算资源下开展多WSI视觉语言模型研究的门槛。

Original Abstract: Generating clinically useful pathology reports for pathology cases from whole-slide images (WSIs) is challenging due to gigapixel resolution, long visual-token sequences, and the complexity of case-level reasoning, where a single case may contain multiple WSIs with heterogeneous tissues and ambiguous findings. We present a simple token-efficient vision--language model for case-level synoptic report generation that remains practical under constrained GPU memory. Our architecture follows a minimal three-component design: a frozen pathology patch encoder, a lightweight two-layer MLP vision-language aligner, and a large language model decoder, with an explicit WSI marker token to separate slides within a case. Training proceeds in two supervised stages: (1) aligner-only WSI captioning using heterogeneous WSI-text pairs, and (2) case-level supervised fine-tuning on case-report pairs for structured report generation. To reduce sequence length, we represent each slide using $512 \times 512$ patches at $5\times$ magnification, which reduces the average sequence length by up to $64\times$ times compared to the commonly used $20\times$ patches. Combined with efficient training techniques, we enable practical training with only half a NVIDIA H100 GPU. Across both training stages, our approach achieves high ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4 scores while being substantially more efficient in memory and runtime. In AI-based evaluations, our model is consistently preferred over strong baselines. Extensive ablations characterize performance-efficiency trade-offs and identify simple choices that improve robustness in multi-WSI settings. Overall, this work provides a strong, reproducible baseline for efficient pathology report generation, lowering the barrier to multi-WSI VLM research under limited compute.

中文摘要翻译: 从全切片图像生成临床可用的病理报告具有挑战性，原因在于千兆像素分辨率、长视觉标记序列以及病例级推理的复杂性——单个病例可能包含多个全切片图像，涉及异质组织和模糊发现。我们提出了一种简单的令牌高效视觉-语言模型，用于病例级概要报告生成，该模型在GPU内存受限的条件下仍保持实用性。我们的架构采用最少的三组件设计：冻结的病理块编码器、轻量两层MLP视觉-语言对齐器，以及大语言模型解码器，并引入显式的全切片图像标记令牌来分隔病例中的不同切片。训练分为两个监督阶段：（1）仅对齐器的全切片图像字幕生成，使用异质全切片图像-文本对；（2）在病例-报告对上进行病例级监督微调，用于结构化报告生成。为缩短序列长度，我们采用5倍放大率下的512×512块表示每个切片，相比常用的20倍放大率块，可将平均序列长度降低最多64倍。结合高效训练技术，我们仅需半张NVIDIA H100 GPU即可实现实用训练。在两个训练阶段中，我们的方法均取得了高ROUGE-L/METEOR/BLEU-4分数，同时在内存和运行时间上大幅更高效。在基于AI的评估中，我们的模型持续优于强基线方法。大量消融实验刻画了性能-效率的权衡，并识别出能够提升多全切片图像场景鲁棒性的简单设计选择。总体而言，本工作为高效的病理报告生成提供了一个强且可复现的基线，降低了在有限计算资源下开展多全切片图像视觉语言模型研究的门槛。

</details>


### [20] [Immuno-VLM: Immunizing Large Vision-Language Models via Generative Semantic Antibodies for Open-World Trustworthiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30745)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Immuno-VLM借鉴免疫学阴性选择原理，利用大语言模型主动生成“语义抗体”（近分布异常文本描述）来约束已知类别决策空间，从而解决视觉-语言模型在开放世界中高置信度误判未知异常的问题，在ImageNet-1K和四个OOD基准上达到了SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在零样本识别中因缺乏显式负知识，会以高置信度将未知异常强行归入已知类别（即“语义傲慢”），导致开放世界部署中存在严重的安全隐患。现有开放集识别方法（被动密度估计或像素级异常生成）效率低且效果有限。

Method: 提出Immuno-VLM框架，借鉴生物免疫系统中的阴性选择原理：利用大语言模型的生成式推理主动产生“语义抗体”——即近分布异常（如相似物、上下文异常）的文本描述，通过这些描述约束已知类别的决策边界，实现主动的开放世界异常检测。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和四个具有挑战性的分布外（OOD）基准上，Immuno-VLM取得了新的最优结果（SOTA）。

Conclusion: Immuno-VLM通过将免疫阴性选择原理适配到高维潜在空间，并利用大语言模型主动生成语义抗体，有效解决了视觉-语言模型在开放世界中的可信度悖论，为开放集识别提供了新的生物启发式范式。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models have achieved unprecedented success in zero-shot recognition by aligning visual features with broad semantic concepts. However, this semantic abstraction creates a critical vulnerability in open-world deployment: the ``Hubris of Semantics'', where models force-fit unknown anomalies into known categories with high confidence due to the lack of explicit negative knowledge. To address this \textit{Open-World Trustworthiness Paradox}, we propose \textbf{Immuno-VLM}, a bio-inspired framework that adapts the biological principle of \textbf{Immunological Negative Selection} to high-dimensional latent spaces. Departing from traditional Open-Set Recognition methods that rely on passive density estimation or inefficient pixel-space outlier generation, Immuno-VLM leverages the generative reasoning of Large Language Models to actively hallucinate ``Semantic Antibodies'', textual descriptions of near-distribution outliers (e.g., look-alikes, contextual anomalies) that effectively bound the decision space of known classes.Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K and four challenging OOD benchmarks reveal that Immuno-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型通过将视觉特征与广泛的语义概念对齐，在零样本识别中取得了前所未有的成功。然而，这种语义抽象在开放世界部署中引入了一个关键漏洞——“语义傲慢”：由于缺乏显式的负知识，模型会以高置信度将未知异常强行归入已知类别。为了解决这一“开放世界可信度悖论”，我们提出了Immuno-VLM，一个受生物启发的框架，将“免疫阴性选择”的生物学原理适配到高维潜在空间。与依赖被动密度估计或低效像素级异常生成的传统开放集识别方法不同，Immuno-VLM利用大语言模型的生成式推理主动产生“语义抗体”——即近分布异常（例如相似物、上下文异常）的文本描述，从而有效约束已知类别的决策空间。在ImageNet-1K和四个具有挑战性的分布外基准上的大量实验表明，Immuno-VLM取得了新的最优结果。

</details>


### [21] [SLAP: The Semantic Least Action Principle for Variational Video-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30750)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义最小作用原理（SLAP），将视频插值建模为黎曼流形上的边值问题，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解，实现无需像素级渲染的语义一致插值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型视频语言模型因稀疏帧采样导致"时间间隙"，无法捕捉关键因果转换；现有生成式幻觉或自回归外推方法在长时间跨度下语义不一致，存在物体消失和能量不稳定性问题。

Method: 将经典力学与语义动力学建立严格同构，将潜在视频轨迹建模为黎曼流形上的路径，由语义拉格朗日量控制；将插值任务转化为边值问题，通过离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程求解。

Result: 大量实验验证了SLAP的有效性，在保持物体持久性和语义一致性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 从概率生成转向变分力学范式，SLAP通过语义最小作用原理自然地保证了物体持久性，无需像素级渲染。

Original Abstract: In the era of Large Video-Language Models (LVLMs), the computational necessity of sparse frame sampling creates a fundamental ``temporal gap'', rendering models blind to critical causal transitions. Existing solutions relying on generative hallucination (e.g., latent diffusion) or autoregressive extrapolation often fail to maintain semantic consistency over long horizons, suffering from object vanishing and energetic instability. We propose a paradigm shift from probabilistic generation to variational mechanics with the \textbf{Semantic Least Action Principle (SLAP)}. Drawing a rigorous isomorphism between classical mechanics and semantic dynamics, we model the latent video trajectory as a path on a Riemannian manifold governed by a Semantic Lagrangian. By formulating the interpolation task as a Boundary Value Problem (BVP) solved via the discrete Euler-Lagrange equations, SLAP naturally enforces object persistence without pixel-level rendering. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness of our proposed SLAP.

中文摘要翻译: 在大规模视频-语言模型的时代，稀疏帧采样的计算必要性造成了根本性的"时间间隙"，使得模型对关键的因果转换视而不见。现有依赖于生成式幻觉（如潜在扩散）或自回归外推的方法，在长时间跨度下往往无法保持语义一致性，存在物体消失和能量不稳定性问题。我们提出从概率生成到变分力学的范式转变，引入\textbf{语义最小作用原理（SLAP）}。通过在经典力学与语义动力学之间建立严格的同构关系，我们将潜在视频轨迹建模为由语义拉格朗日量控制的黎曼流形上的路径。通过将插值任务表述为边值问题，并利用离散欧拉-拉格朗日方程进行求解，SLAP自然地保证了物体持久性，而无需像素级渲染。大量实验表明了所提出的SLAP的有效性。

</details>


### [22] [DisPlace: Discriminative Place Projections for Multi-Reference Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30769)
*Dhyey Manish Rajani,Michael Milford,Tobias Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DisPlace框架，将多参考描述符融合为单一紧凑的地点表征，通过广义特征值问题最大化地点间可分性、抑制地点内变异，在多个数据集和描述符上显著优于现有融合方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉地点识别中的多参考融合策略要么均匀聚合所有参考描述符，要么依赖启发式选择，无法区分哪些描述符变化保留了地点稳定身份、哪些是由条件或视角变化引起的，导致融合效果不佳。

Method: 将描述符融合建模为广义特征值问题，通过最大化地点间可分性（between-place separability）同时抑制跨参考的地点内变异（within-place variation），而非保留整体描述符方差，从而自动学习哪些描述符维度的线性组合能保留地点身份、哪些捕获条件或视角特异性变化。

Result: 在Oxford RobotCar、Nordland、Pittsburgh30k和Google Landmarks v2上，使用6种SOTA描述符进行评估；在54种外观变化条件下49次优于7种多参考基线；在视角和非结构化场景下持续提升描述符级融合性能；推理时存储需求低于所有对比融合方法。

Conclusion: DisPlace通过有区别地利用跨参考遍历中的变异信息，能够有效学习保留地点身份的线性描述符组合，生成更紧凑、更具判别性的多参考融合表征，在多种挑战性条件下均优于现有方法且存储效率更高。

Original Abstract: A key challenge in Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is matching query images against reference maps captured under diverse environmental conditions and viewpoints. While multiple reference traversals improve robustness, existing fusion strategies either aggregate references uniformly or rely on heuristic selection, without distinguishing descriptor variations that preserve stable place identity from those caused by changing conditions or viewpoints. In this paper, we propose DisPlace, a multi-reference VPR framework that fuses multiple reference descriptors into a single compact and discriminative place representation. DisPlace formulates descriptor fusion as a generalized eigenvalue problem that maximizes between-place separability while suppressing within-place variation across references, rather than preserving overall descriptor variance. Unlike existing multi-reference fusion methods, DisPlace exploits variation across reference traversals to identify which linear combinations of descriptor dimensions preserve place identity and which capture condition- or viewpoint-specific variation. We evaluate DisPlace on Oxford RobotCar, Nordland, Pittsburgh30k, and Google Landmarks v2 across six state-of-the-art VPR descriptors. DisPlace outperforms seven multi-reference baselines in 49 out of 54 appearance-varying conditions, consistently improves descriptor-level fusion performance under viewpoint and unstructured settings, and requires less storage during inference than all compared fusion methods.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉地点识别（VPR）中的一个关键挑战是在不同环境条件和视角下采集的查询图像与参考地图进行匹配。虽然多参考遍历（multi-reference traversals）能提升鲁棒性，但现有的融合策略要么均匀聚合所有参考，要么依赖启发式选择，未能区分那些保留稳定地点身份的描述符变化与那些由变化条件或视角引起的变化。本文提出DisPlace，一种多参考VPR框架，它将多个参考描述符融合为单一紧凑且具有判别性的地点表征。DisPlace将描述符融合建模为一个广义特征值问题，该问题最大化地点间可分性，同时抑制跨参考的地点内变异，而非保留整体描述符方差。与现有的多参考融合方法不同，DisPlace利用跨参考遍历中的变异来识别哪些描述符维度的线性组合保留了地点身份，哪些捕获了条件或视角特异性变化。我们在Oxford RobotCar、Nordland、Pittsburgh30k和Google Landmarks v2上使用六种最先进的VPR描述符评估了DisPlace。在54种外观变化条件下，DisPlace在49种条件下优于七种多参考基线，在视角和非结构化环境下持续提升了描述符级融合性能，并且在推理时所需存储空间少于所有对比的融合方法。

</details>


### [23] [MechVQA: Benchmarking and Enhancing Multimodal LLMs on Comprehensive Mechanical Drawing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30794)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Yulin Li,Xiaosong Qiu,Xinyang Wang,Hua Zhou,Cao Dongxing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个机械工程图纸理解数据集MechVQA（含3.3k高密度图像和21K问答对，覆盖10个细粒度任务和三个能力层次），并开发了MechVL模型，通过多阶段训练在总分上超越最强闭源基线7.57个百分点，显著提升了多模态大语言模型在机械图纸上的理解能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在通用视觉问答任务上表现优异，但在机械工程图纸上仍然脆弱，主要原因是：标注密度高、领域知识薄弱，加上严格的投影规则和几何约束下空间关系推理不可靠，导致关键线索容易被遗漏并频繁出错。

Method: (1) 构建MechVQA数据集：通过半自动化的构建和质量控制流水线，生成包含3.3k高密度图像和21K问答对的数据集，覆盖10个细粒度任务（分属三个能力层次：识别、推理、判断）；(2) 开发MechVL模型：基于多阶段训练范式，建立强领域专业化基线模型。

Result: MechVL在MechVQA总分上超越最强闭源基线模型7.57个百分点，显著增强了机械图纸理解能力。

Conclusion: MechVQA数据集和MechVL模型为在机械设计与检测场景中部署多模态大语言模型提供了可复用的基础，有效弥合了通用MLLM在机械图纸理解方面的不足。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant achievements in general visual question answering (VQA) tasks. However, they remain brittle on mechanical engineering drawings, where high annotation density and weak domain knowledge, compounded by unreliable spatial relation reasoning under strict projection rules and geometric constraints, make decisive cues easy to miss and frequently lead to wrong answers. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first comprehensive mechanical drawing understanding dataset, MechVQA, created through a semi-automated construction and quality-control pipeline. MechVQA contains 3.3k high-density pictures with 21K question-answer pairs, spanning 10 different fine-grained tasks across three capability levels: Recognition, Reasoning, and Judging, providing a testbed to evaluate and improve MLLM understanding on real-world mechanical drawings. On top of MechVQA, we then develop the MechVL model through a multi-stage training paradigm, building a strong domain-specialized baseline. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that MechVL outperforms the strongest closed-source baseline by 7.57 percentage points on the MechVQA total score, significantly enhancing mechanical drawing understanding ability and providing a reusable foundation for deploying MLLMs in mechanical design and inspection scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在通用视觉问答（VQA）任务中取得了显著成就。然而，它们在机械工程图纸上仍然脆弱，这是因为高标注密度和薄弱的领域知识，加之严格的投影规则和几何约束下不可靠的空间关系推理，使得关键线索容易被遗漏并频繁导致错误答案。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了首个综合性机械图纸理解数据集MechVQA，该数据集通过半自动化的构建与质量控制流水线创建。MechVQA包含3.3k张高密度图像和21K个问答对，涵盖三个能力层次（识别、推理和判断）中的10个不同细粒度任务，为评估和提升MLLM在真实机械图纸上的理解能力提供了测试平台。在此基础上，我们进一步通过多阶段训练范式开发了MechVL模型，构建了强大的领域专业化基线。大量实验结果表明，MechVL在MechVQA总分上超越了最强闭源基线模型7.57个百分点，显著增强了机械图纸理解能力，并为在机械设计与检测场景中部署MLLM提供了可复用的基础。

</details>


### [24] [Function2Scene: 3D Indoor Scene Layout from Functional Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30819)
*Ruiqi Wang,Qimin Chen,Daniel Ritchie,Angel X. Chang,Manolis Savva,Kai Wang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Function2Scene 提出了一种从功能性自然语言描述（谁使用房间、需要做什么）生成3D室内布局的框架，通过迭代的检查-修复循环结合几何测量、LLM推理和VLM视觉评估，生成更符合人类使用需求的布局。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本驱动的室内场景合成方法大多基于物体中心提示（放什么家具），而非考虑空间如何被使用。真实室内设计应以满足 occupants 的活动和物理需求为评判标准，因此需要从功能规格出发生成布局。

Method: 解析 occupant 角色和活动，从包含17个标准（空间、人体工学、活动、环境）的分类体系中派生功能设计约束，通过工具增强的检查-修复循环（结合几何测量、LLM上下文推理、VLM视觉评估）进行迭代评估与优化，而非让LLM直接生成最终场景。

Result: 在30个专业室内设计案例上，Function2Scene 生成的布局比最近的 LLM 基线方法更好地满足功能需求，在94.3%的成对比较中被优选。

Conclusion: 该工作将文本驱动的室内场景合成从放置合理物体重新定义为设计支持人类使用的空间，证明了功能约束引导的迭代生成范式优于直接 LLM 生成。

Original Abstract: Most text-driven 3D indoor scene synthesis methods generate rooms from object-centric prompts, asking what furniture should be placed rather than how the space is used. Yet in real interior design, a layout is judged by how well it supports its occupants, e.g., their activities and physical needs. We introduce Function2Scene, a framework for generating 3D indoor layouts from functional specifications, i.e., natural-language design briefs describing who will use a room and what they need to do there. Given such a specification, our system parses occupant personas and activities, derives a customized set of functional design constraints from a taxonomy of 17 criteria spanning spatial, ergonomic, activity, and environmental considerations, and uses these constraints to guide layout generation. Rather than relying on an LLM to directly produce a final scene, Function2Scene performs iterative evaluation and refinement through a tool-augmented check-and-repair loop, combining geometric measurements, LLM-based contextual reasoning, and VLM-based visual assessment. Experiments on 30 professionally written interior-design cases show that Function2Scene produces layouts that better satisfy functional requirements than recent LLM-based scene synthesis baselines, with our results preferred in 94.3% of pairwise comparisons. Our work reframes text-driven indoor scene synthesis from placing plausible objects to designing spaces that support human use.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数文本驱动的3D室内场景合成方法从物体中心提示生成房间，询问应放置什么家具，而非空间应如何被使用。然而在真实室内设计中，布局的优劣取决于其对 occupants 的支持程度，例如他们的活动和生理需求。我们提出了 Function2Scene，一个从功能规格（即描述谁将使用房间以及他们需要在其中做什么的自然语言设计简报）生成3D室内布局的框架。给定这样的规格说明，我们的系统解析 occupant 角色和活动，从包含17个标准（涵盖空间、人体工学、活动及环境考量）的分类体系中推导出一组定制化的功能设计约束，并使用这些约束引导布局生成。Function2Scene 并非依赖 LLM 直接生成最终场景，而是通过一个工具增强的检查-修复循环执行迭代评估与优化，结合几何测量、基于LLM的上下文推理和基于VLM的视觉评估。在30个专业编写的室内设计案例上的实验表明，Function2Scene 生成的布局比最近的基于LLM的场景合成基线方法更好地满足功能需求，我们的结果在94.3%的成对比较中被优选。我们的工作将文本驱动的室内场景合成从放置合理物体重新定义为设计支持人类使用的空间。

</details>


### [25] [LegSegNet: A Public Deep Learning System for Lower Extremity CT Tissue Segmentation and Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30829)
*Yuwen Chen,Yaqian Chen,Roy Colglazier,Haoyu Dong,Hanxue Gu,Maciej A. Mazurowski,Kevin W. Southerland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LegSegNet是一个用于下肢CT组织分割和身体成分量化的深度学习系统，能够分割骨骼、骨骼肌、皮下脂肪组织和肌间/肌内脂肪组织，在900张测试切片上达到89.31的平均Dice分数，是首个公开可用的端到端下肢CT分析与量化系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有公开分割工具不适用于全面的下肢CT分析，特别是无法处理临床上重要的肌间/肌内脂肪组织分割；且大多只提供掩码预测而非端到端的量化系统，难以大规模提取体成分分析所需的测量指标。

Method: 使用1,302张手动标注的CT切片训练分割模型，经放射科医生审核标注质量；系统可分割骨骼、骨骼肌、皮下脂肪组织和肌间/肌内脂肪组织，并自动计算定量组织测量值。与CNN模型、Transformer模型和微调的基础模型等2D分割方法进行基准对比，并在外部公开CT数据集上评估泛化能力。

Result: 在900张保留测试集上达到89.31的平均Dice分数，优于所有对比的2D分割方法，包括CNN模型、Transformer模型和微调的基础模型，并在外部公开数据集上展现了良好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: LegSegNet是首个公开可用的端到端下肢CT组织分割与量化系统，为未来医学图像分析中的计算机视觉研究提供了实用的评估工具。

Original Abstract: Lower extremity computed tomography (CT) contains clinically relevant information for body composition analysis, sarcopenia assessment, and musculoskeletal disease monitoring, but extracting these measurements at scale requires accurate tissue segmentation and an automated quantification workflow. Existing public segmentation tools are not designed for comprehensive lower extremity CT analysis, particularly for clinically important inter/intramuscular adipose tissue, and most public methods only provide mask prediction rather than an end-to-end quantification system. To address this problem, we present LegSegNet, a deep learning system for lower extremity CT tissue segmentation and body composition quantification. Given an input CT scan, LegSegNet segments bone, skeletal muscle, subcutaneous adipose tissue, and inter/intramuscular adipose tissue. It then computes quantitative tissue measurements for downstream analysis. We developed the segmentation model using 1,302 manually annotated CT slices and evaluated it on 900 held-out test slices, with all annotations reviewed by radiologists. We benchmark LegSegNet against a broad set of 2D segmentation methods, including CNN-based models, transformer-based models, and finetuned foundation models, and further evaluate its generalization on an external public CT dataset. LegSegNet achieves the best overall segmentation performance, with an average Dice score of 89.31 on the held-out test set. To our knowledge, LegSegNet is the first publicly available end-to-end system for lower extremity CT tissue segmentation and quantification, providing a practical evaluation tool for future computer vision research in medical image analysis. The code and model weights are available at: https://github.com/mazurowski-lab/LegSegNet

中文摘要翻译: 下肢计算机断层扫描（CT）包含用于体成分分析、肌少症评估和肌肉骨骼疾病监测的临床相关信息，但要大规模提取这些测量指标，需要准确的组织分割和自动化量化工作流程。现有的公开分割工具并非为全面的下肢CT分析而设计，特别是对于临床上重要的肌间/肌内脂肪组织，且大多数公开方法仅提供掩码预测而非端到端的量化系统。为解决这一问题，我们提出了LegSegNet——一个用于下肢CT组织分割和体成分量化的深度学习系统。给定输入CT扫描，LegSegNet可分割骨骼、骨骼肌、皮下脂肪组织和肌间/肌内脂肪组织，随后计算定量组织测量值以供下游分析。我们使用1,302张手动标注的CT切片开发了分割模型，并在900张保留测试切片上进行了评估，所有标注均经放射科医生审核。我们将LegSegNet与广泛的2D分割方法进行基准对比，包括基于CNN的模型、基于Transformer的模型和微调的基础模型，并在外部公开CT数据集上进一步评估其泛化能力。LegSegNet在保留测试集上取得了最佳整体分割性能，平均Dice分数为89.31。据我们所知，LegSegNet是首个公开可用的用于下肢CT组织分割和量化的端到端系统，为未来医学图像分析中的计算机视觉研究提供了实用的评估工具。代码和模型权重可在以下网址获取：https://github.com/mazurowski-lab/LegSegNet

</details>


### [26] [Count Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30846)
*Mengqi Lei,Shuokun Cheng,Wei Bao,Shaoyi Du,Jun-Hai Yong,Siqi Li,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域文本引导的通用目标计数任务，构建了包含6个视觉领域、220K图像和15M实例的CLOC数据集，并设计了Count Anything模型，通过区域级稀疏计数器和像素级密集计数器的双粒度枚举机制与互补计数融合，在跨域开放场景下实现优于现有方法的计数精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有目标计数模型通常针对特定领域（如人群、车辆、细胞等）设计，难以跨类别、视觉域、目标尺度和密度分布泛化，且缺乏自然语言引导的通用计数框架。

Method: 提出Count Anything模型：(1) 采用离散实例点而非密度图，进行双粒度实例枚举；(2) 区域级稀疏计数器为大而稀疏的目标提供对象级锚点；(3) 像素级密集计数器通过密集点预测处理小、拥挤、弱边界目标；(4) 点为中心的监督策略支持异构标注学习；(5) 互补计数融合以无参数方式结合两种计数器。

Result: Count Anything在跨域开放场景下实现了强准确性和多域泛化，优于现有开放世界计数方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过构建统一的大规模跨域数据集CLOC和双粒度计数架构Count Anything，可以有效解决跨域目标计数中的泛化问题，推动从专用计数模型向通用计数模型的转变。

Original Abstract: Object counting remains fragmented across domain-specific datasets and task formulations, despite rapid progress in generalist vision models. Existing counting models are often tailored to scenarios such as crowds, vehicles, cells, crops, or remote-sensing objects, and thus struggle to generalize across categories, visual domains, object scales, and density distributions. In this paper, we study text-guided object counting across domains, where a model takes an image and a natural-language query as input and returns an instance-grounded set of target points whose cardinality gives the count. This formulation unifies category-conditioned counting with interpretable spatial localization. To support this setting, we construct CLOC, a Cross-domain Large-scale Object Counting dataset that reorganizes diverse public data sources into a unified benchmark. CLOC covers six visual domains: General Scene, Remote Sensing, Histopathology, Cellular Microscopy, Agriculture, and Microbiology, with about 220K images, 619 categories, and 15M object instances. Based on CLOC, we propose Count Anything, a generalist model for text-guided object counting. Unlike density-map-based methods, which dominate counting models, Count Anything adopts discrete instance points and performs dual-granularity instance enumeration. A Region-level Sparse Counter provides object-level anchors for large and sparse targets, while a Pixel-level Dense Counter handles small, crowded, and weakly bounded targets via dense point prediction. A point-centric supervision strategy enables learning from heterogeneous annotations, and Complementary Count Fusion combines both counters in a parameter-free manner. Extensive experiments show that Count Anything achieves strong accuracy and multi-domain generalization, outperforming existing open-world counting methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/Mengqi-Lei/count-anything.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管通用视觉模型取得了快速进展，目标计数仍然分散在特定领域的数据集和任务形式中。现有的计数模型通常针对人群、车辆、细胞、作物或遥感场景等特定场景设计，因此难以跨类别、视觉域、目标尺度和密度分布进行泛化。本文研究了跨域的文本引导目标计数，其中模型以图像和自然语言查询作为输入，返回一组基于实例的目标点，其基数即为计数结果。这种形式统一了类别条件计数与可解释的空间定位。为支撑该设定，我们构建了CLOC（跨域大规模目标计数数据集），将多样化的公开数据源重新组织为统一基准。CLOC覆盖六个视觉域：通用场景、遥感、组织病理学、细胞显微成像、农业和微生物学，包含约220K张图像、619个类别和1500万个目标实例。基于CLOC，我们提出Count Anything，一个用于文本引导目标计数的通用模型。与主导计数模型的密度图方法不同，Count Anything采用离散实例点并执行双粒度实例枚举。区域级稀疏计数器为大型稀疏目标提供对象级锚点，而像素级密集计数器通过密集点预测处理小型、拥挤和弱边界目标。点为中心的监督策略支持从异构标注中学习，互补计数融合以无参数方式结合两种计数器。大量实验表明，Count Anything实现了强准确性和多域泛化能力，优于现有开放世界计数方法。代码已开源：https://github.com/Mengqi-Lei/count-anything。

</details>


### [27] [Robust Dreamer: Deviation-Aware Latent Gaussian Memory for Action-Controlled AR Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30855)
*Hanlin Chen,Jiaxin Wei,Xibin Song,Yifu Wang,Steve Wang,Hongdong Li,Pan Ji,Gim Hee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Robust Dreamer，一个记忆增强的帧级动作控制图像到视频生成框架，通过Latent Gaussian Memory和Deviation Learning解决长自回归生成中的3D一致性和漂移问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D感知方法在长自回归生成中面临两大障碍：(1) Latent-RGB循环导致的信息损失，即生成潜变量反复解码为RGB再重新编码用于未来条件化；(2) 训练-推理差距，即无误差假设下清洁训练记忆与预测损坏推理记忆不匹配，导致灾难性漂移。

Method: (1) Latent Gaussian Memory：将扩散潜变量锚定到高斯基元上，通过潜空间高斯泼溅召回，提供密集、几何感知、视角对齐的条件化信号，避免VAE反复转换导致的累积退化。(2) Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive：通过一步近似合成自回归潜变量偏差，按自回归阶段和去噪时间戳存储，训练时注入历史记忆，使生成器暴露于真实损坏记忆状态并学会内部校正。

Result: 在ScanNet、DL3DV和OmniWorldGame数据集上实现最先进的长时序生成性能。

Conclusion: 通过设计3D记忆的表示方式（Latent Gaussian Memory）和鲁棒使用方法（Deviation Learning），有效缓解了长自回归图像到视频生成中的漂移问题，实现了更稳定的交互式世界模拟。

Original Abstract: Frame-wise action-controlled image-to-video generation is a promising paradigm for interactive world simulation, where each control signal should elicit an immediate visual response. However, maintaining visual fidelity and 3D consistency over long autoregressive rollouts remains challenging. Existing 3D-aware methods often suffer from catastrophic drift due to two impediments: information loss from \textit{Latent--RGB Cycling}, where generated latents are repeatedly decoded to RGB and re-encoded for future conditioning, and the training--inference gap induced by the \textit{error-free hypothesis}, where clean training memory fails to match prediction-corrupted inference memory. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{Robust Dreamer}, a memory-augmented framework built around how to design 3D memory and how to use it robustly. First, we introduce \textbf{Latent Gaussian Memory}, which anchors diffusion latents inherited from the generation process to Gaussian primitives and recalls them via latent-space Gaussian splatting. This provides dense, geometry-aware, view-aligned conditioning while avoiding accumulated degradation from repeated VAE conversion. Second, we propose \textbf{Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive}, which synthesizes rollout-induced latent deviations through a one-step approximation, stores them by autoregressive stage and denoising timestamp, and injects them into historical memory during training. This exposes the generator to realistic corrupted memory states and teaches internal correction before inference. Experiments on ScanNet, DL3DV, and OmniWorldGame demonstrate state-of-the-art long-horizon performance.

中文摘要翻译: 帧级动作控制的图像到视频生成是交互式世界模拟中一种有前景的范式，其中每个控制信号应引发即时的视觉响应。然而，在长时间自回归展开中保持视觉保真度和3D一致性仍然具有挑战性。现有3D感知方法常因两个障碍而遭受灾难性漂移：一是来自\textit{Latent-RGB循环}的信息损失，即生成的潜变量被反复解码为RGB并重新编码用于未来条件化；二是由\textit{无误差假设}引起的训练-推理差距，即清洁的训练记忆无法匹配预测损坏的推理记忆。为应对这些挑战，我们提出\textbf{Robust Dreamer}，一个围绕如何设计3D记忆以及如何鲁棒使用它而构建的记忆增强框架。首先，我们引入\textbf{Latent Gaussian Memory}，将生成过程中继承的扩散潜变量锚定到高斯基元上，并通过潜空间高斯泼溅进行召回。这提供了密集、几何感知、视角对齐的条件化信号，同时避免了反复VAE转换导致的累积退化。其次，我们提出\textbf{Deviation Learning with Dynamic Deviation Archive}，通过一步近似合成自回归诱导的潜变量偏差，按自回归阶段和去噪时间戳存储，并在训练时将其注入历史记忆中。这使得生成器暴露于真实损坏的记忆状态，并在推理前学会内部校正。在ScanNet、DL3DV和OmniWorldGame上的实验证明了其最先进的长时序性能。

</details>


### [28] [DSD-GS: Dynamic-Static Decomposition of Gaussian Splatting for Efficient and High-Fidelity Dynamic Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30863)
*Youngtae Han,Sung-hwan Han,Youngmin Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于前馈高斯泼溅编码器和光流模型的静态-动态分解框架，通过消除静态区域的冗余计算，在渲染质量、训练/渲染速度及存储效率上达到SOTA，在Neural 3D数据集上仅需10分钟训练、渲染速度超700 FPS。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态3DGS方法将所有高斯体素建模为动态组件导致计算效率低下；近期分解方法虽有所改进但仍存在重建质量下降和训练时间过长的问题。因此需要一种高效、高质量的动态场景重建方法。

Method: 提出基于前馈高斯泼溅编码器和光流模型的静态-动态分解框架，将场景拆分为静态和动态组件，仅对动态区域进行计算，消除静态区域的冗余计算；无需COLMAP预处理，支持确定性初始化。

Result: 在Neural 3D数据集上仅需10分钟训练，渲染速度超700 FPS（单张NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU，分辨率1352×1014），在渲染质量、训练/渲染速度和存储效率上均超越现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的静态-动态分解策略有效解决了动态场景重建的计算效率问题，实现了高效、高质量、可复现的动态场景重建与新视角合成。

Original Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction and novel view synthesis are fundamental to next-generation visual intelligence applications such as virtual reality, robotics, and digital twins. However, high-fidelity reconstruction of complex, time-varying scenes from arbitrary viewpoints remains a significant challenge. Existing dynamic 3DGS methods suffer from computational inefficiency, since they model all Gaussians as dynamic components. While recent decomposition-based approaches address this issue, they still struggle with degraded reconstruction quality and prolonged training time. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a novel dynamic reconstruction framework built upon an efficient static-dynamic decomposition strategy using a Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting encoder and an optical flow model. By eliminating redundant computations on static regions, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing baselines across rendering quality, training and rendering speed, and storage efficiency. Notably, on the Neural 3D dataset, our framework requires only 10 minutes for training and achieves a rendering speed of over 700 FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU at resolution of 1352x1014. Furthermore, our decomposition strategy eliminates the need for COLMAP preprocessing and enables deterministic initialization, thereby enhancing both efficiency and reproducibility.

中文摘要翻译: 动态场景重建和新视角合成是虚拟现实、机器人和数字孪生等下一代视觉智能应用的基础。然而，从任意视角对复杂、时变场景进行高保真重建仍然是一个重大挑战。现有的动态3DGS方法将所有高斯体素建模为动态组件，导致计算效率低下。虽然近期基于分解的方法试图解决这一问题，但仍面临重建质量下降和训练时间过长的问题。为缓解这些限制，我们提出了一种新颖的动态重建框架，该框架基于高效的前馈高斯泼溅编码器和光流模型构建了静态-动态分解策略。通过消除静态区域的冗余计算，我们的方法实现了最先进的性能，在渲染质量、训练和渲染速度以及存储效率方面均超越了现有基线方法。值得注意的是，在Neural 3D数据集上，我们的框架仅需10分钟训练，在单张NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU上以1352×1014分辨率可实现超过700 FPS的渲染速度。此外，我们的分解策略消除了对COLMAP预处理的需求，并支持确定性初始化，从而提升了效率和可复现性。

</details>


### [29] [GUI-C$^2$: Coarse-to-Fine GUI Grounding via Difficulty-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30884)
*Junlong Li,Chao Hao,Lap-Pui Chau,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对GUI智能体强化学习方法在数据和策略层面的局限性，提出GUI-D数据挖掘与难度评分流水线和GUI-C²粗到细精炼机制，实现了最先进的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GUI接地强化学习方法存在两个层面的局限：数据层面，所有训练样本被平等对待，忽略了不同难度样本对模型训练价值的差异，导致训练效率低下甚至崩溃；策略层面，现有框架难以平衡裁剪大区域以获取充分上下文和裁剪小区域以减少冗余之间的权衡，且复杂决策过程对小参数模型不友好且增加推理时间。

Method: 数据层面提出GUI-D，通过适当测试识别有价值的训练样本并为样本分配难度分数以指导后续训练权重。策略层面提出GUI-C²，采用区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部不确定性信号逐步缩小视野，自适应地为大目标保留上下文、为小目标提高精度，并引入改进感知的阶段奖励确保每次精炼都能真正提升接地效果，同时简化决策过程以减少额外推理时间。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GUI-D和GUI-C²有效解决了GUI接地中数据和策略层面的核心问题，实现了性能提升与推理效率的兼顾。

Original Abstract: Existing agentic reinforcement learning methods for GUI grounding have limitations at two levels. At the data level, current approaches typically treat all training samples equally, although their training value to the baseline model varies with difficulty. Overlooking this can greatly reduce training efficiency or even cause collapse. At the strategy level, existing frameworks struggle to balance the trade-off between cropping larger regions for sufficient context and smaller ones for reduced redundancy, a tension inherent to tool-augmented grounding agents. In addition, overly complex decision-making is difficult for small-parameter models and significantly increases inference time. To address these issues, at the data level, we propose GUI-D, a data mining and difficulty scoring pipeline that identifies the training-worthy samples by proper testing and assigns difficulty scores to guide subsequent training weights.
