At the strategy level, we propose GUI-C$^2$, which employs an area-gated coarse-to-fine refinement mechanism that progressively narrows the visual field via model-internal uncertainty signals, adaptively reserving context for large targets while amplifying precision for small ones, reinforced by improvement-aware stage rewards that ensure each refinement genuinely advances grounding. Meanwhile, we simplify the decision-making process to greatly reduce additional inference time. Finally, extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance. The code and data will be publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 现有面向GUI接地的智能体强化学习方法在数据和策略两个层面存在局限性。在数据层面，当前方法通常平等对待所有训练样本，然而不同难度的样本对基线模型的训练价值各不相同；忽视这一点会极大降低训练效率甚至导致模型崩溃。在策略层面，现有框架难以平衡裁剪更大区域以获取充分上下文与裁剪更小区域以减少冗余之间的权衡——这是工具增强型接地智能体固有的张力。此外，过度复杂的决策过程对小参数模型不友好，且显著增加了推理时间。为解决这些问题，在数据层面，我们提出GUI-D，一种数据挖掘与难度评分流水线，通过适当的测试识别有价值的训练样本，并分配难度分数以指导后续训练权重。在策略层面，我们提出GUI-C²，采用区域门控的粗到细精炼机制，通过模型内部的不确定性信号逐步缩小视野，自适应地为大目标保留上下文，同时为小目标放大精度，并由改进感知的阶段奖励增强，确保每次精炼都能真正推进接地效果。同时，我们简化了决策过程，大幅减少了额外推理时间。最后，大量实验表明，我们的方法达到了最先进的性能。代码和数据将公开提供。

</details>


### [30] [Foundation VAEs for 3D CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30893)
*Qi Chen,Shuhan Ding,Yu Gu,Nan Liu,Jiang Bian,Alan Yuille,Zongwei Zhou,Jingjing Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现，在自然图像和视频上大规模预训练的Foundation VAE（基础变分自编码器），无需微调即可直接作为CT重建、增强和生成的统一接口，在冻结编码器和解码器的情况下，实现解剖结构保持、噪声抑制，并显著提升下游分割和生成任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从头训练CT专用的VAE或进行大量微调需要巨大的计算和工程成本，且在异质扫描仪、协议和疾病条件下性能会退化；本文旨在探索能否利用已在自然图像上预训练的Foundation VAE，无需训练即可作为CT领域的统一表示接口。

Method: 冻结Foundation VAE的编码器和解码器，将其作为CT体积的重建、增强和生成接口；在潜在空间中训练条件潜在扩散模型（conditional latent diffusion model）进行CT生成。

Result: Foundation VAE重建的CT体积在保留解剖结构的同时抑制采集噪声；基于重建结果训练分割模型，胰腺肿瘤和肺肿瘤的表面精度（NSD）平均提升3.9%；条件潜在扩散模型在FVD上降低3.9%，CT CLIP分数提升36.2%，18种多疾病生成忠实度（AUC）提升2.76%。

Conclusion: Foundation VAE可作为可扩展的CT表示复用和忠实CT生成的实用接口，无需额外训练即可应用于CT重建、增强和生成任务。

Original Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) compress high resolution CT volumes into compact latents while preserving clinically relevant structure. However, training CT-specific VAEs from scratch or heavily fine-tuning them incurs substantial computational and engineering cost, and often degrades under heterogeneous scanners, protocols, and diseases. This paper makes a progressive stride toward training-free medical VAEs by leveraging a critical observation: a single Foundation VAE, pretrained at scale on natural images and videos, can serve as a unified interface for CT Reconstruction, Augmentation, and Generation. With both encoder and decoder frozen, the Foundation VAE reconstructs CT volumes with preserved anatomy while suppressing acquisition noise; training segmentation models on these reconstructions improves surface accuracy by 3.9% NSD on average for pancreatic tumor and lung tumor. Within the same Foundation VAE latent space, a conditional latent diffusion model achieves 3.9% lower average FVD with 36.2% higher CT CLIP score, and improves multi-disease generation faithfulness across 18 types by 2.76% AUC. These results demonstrate Foundation VAEs as a practical interface for scalable CT representation reuse and faithful CT generation. Our code and demo are available at https://github.com/qic999/Foundation-VAE.

中文摘要翻译: 变分自编码器（VAE）能够将高分辨率CT体积压缩为紧凑的潜在表示，同时保留临床相关的解剖结构。然而，从头训练CT专用VAE或进行大量微调会带来巨大的计算和工程成本，并且在不同扫描仪、协议和疾病条件下性能往往会退化。本文基于一个关键观察取得了向无需训练的医学VAE的渐进式进展：一个在自然图像和视频上大规模预训练的基础VAE（Foundation VAE），可以充当CT重建、增强和生成的统一接口。在编码器和解码器均被冻结的情况下，基础VAE在抑制采集噪声的同时重建CT体积并保留解剖结构；在这些重建结果上训练分割模型，胰腺肿瘤和肺肿瘤的表面精度（NSD）平均提升3.9%。在同一个基础VAE潜在空间内，条件潜在扩散模型实现了平均FVD降低3.9%，CT CLIP分数提升36.2%，并将18种类型多疾病生成忠实度（AUC）提升2.76%。这些结果表明，基础VAE可作为可扩展的CT表示复用和忠实CT生成的实用接口。我们的代码和演示可在 https://github.com/qic999/Foundation-VAE 获取。

</details>


### [31] [SteerFace: Debiasing Synthetic Face Generation via Adaptive Residue Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30894)
*Yuxi Mi,Qiuyang Yuan,Jianqing Xu,Yichun Zhou,Xuan Zhao,Jun Wang,Rizen Guo,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerFace框架，通过向身份嵌入添加随机正交方向扰动来抑制生成人脸图像中的视觉倾向（不真实视觉属性偏多），从而缩小合成数据与真实数据在面部识别任务中的差距。实验表明该方法有效缓解了视觉倾向，提升了下游识别性能，并具有良好的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型生成的合成人脸图像虽逼真且身份保持良好，但在下游识别任务中与真实数据仍存在显著的合成-真实差距。本文发现视觉倾向（synthetic visual tendency）是此前未被充分探索的局限，即合成数据中存在不切实际的视觉属性偏多现象，偏离了真实数据分布。该现象源于生成器对身份嵌入的条件化过程中，共现的残留视觉线索被无意吸收进身份语义中。

Method: 提出SteerFace训练框架，通过对身份嵌入施加向嵌入超球面上随机正交方向的扰动，作为身份保持的正则化项，惩罚生成器对非身份成分的依赖。进一步引入自适应策略，学习具有样本偏好的扰动强度，同时保持整体统计量的合理性。

Result: 大量实验表明，SteerFace有效缓解了视觉倾向，在合成人脸用于下游识别任务上优于此前方法，且在不同训练数据集和生成流程上均具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SteerFace通过简单的身份嵌入扰动正则化，有效解决了合成人脸数据中的视觉倾向问题，缩小了合成-真实差距，为使用合成数据提升面部识别性能提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: The shortage of legally compliant data for face recognition training has sparked growing interest in using synthetic data as an alternative. While recent diffusion-based methods enable the generation of photorealistic face images with strong identity adherence and data diversity, their downstream recognition performance still exhibits a significant synthetic-real gap. This paper identifies visual tendency as a previously underexplored limitation, whereby synthetic data exhibit an unrealistic prevalence of visual attributes and thus deviate from the real-data distribution. Visual tendency can be attributed to the generator's conditioning on identity embeddings, through which co-occurring residual visual cues are unintentionally absorbed into learned identity semantics. To discourage the generator from exploiting such visual cues, this paper proposes SteerFace, a simple and efficient training framework that perturbs identity embeddings by steering them toward random orthogonal directions on the embedding hypersphere. The perturbation serves as an identity-preserving regularizer that penalizes the generator's reliance on non-identity components, as supported by theoretical analysis. This paper further introduces an adaptive strategy that learns perturbation strengths with both sample-wise preference and favorable overall statistics. Extensive experiments show that SteerFace effectively mitigates visual tendency, outperforms prior methods in downstream face recognition, and generalizes well across different training datasets and generation pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 用于人脸识别训练的合规数据短缺，引发了人们对使用合成数据作为替代方案的日益关注。虽然近期基于扩散的方法能够生成具有强身份保持性和数据多样性的逼真人脸图像，但其下游识别性能仍存在显著的合成-真实差距。本文识别出视觉倾向（visual tendency）这一此前未被充分探索的局限性，即合成数据表现出不切实际的视觉属性偏多现象，从而偏离了真实数据分布。视觉倾向可归因于生成器对身份嵌入的条件化——共现的残留视觉线索被无意地吸收进学习到的身份语义中。为抑制生成器利用此类视觉线索，本文提出SteerFace，一种简单高效的训练框架，通过将身份嵌入向嵌入超球面上的随机正交方向扰动来施加干扰。该扰动作为一种身份保持的正则化项，惩罚生成器对非身份成分的依赖，理论分析对此提供了支持。本文进一步引入自适应策略，学习具有样本偏好且整体统计量有利的扰动强度。大量实验表明，SteerFace有效缓解了视觉倾向，在下游人脸识别任务中优于此前方法，并在不同训练数据集和生成流程上均具有良好的泛化能力。

</details>


### [32] [MergeTok: Unified Continuous and Discrete Visual Tokenization via Token Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30904)
*Luyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Zedong Wang,Qingsong Xie,Cheng Tan,Anna Wang,Yanhao Zhang,Chen Chen,Haonan Lu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MergeTok是一种统一视觉分词器，通过编码器-解码器架构联合优化连续VAE和离散VQ分词器，利用Token合并技术作为语义桥梁，在ImageNet-256上实现了优于VAE和VQ模型的低rFID重建与生成性能，并兼容自回归和扩散生成器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉分词器分为连续VAE（高保真重建但潜在表示稠密纠缠、不利于语义控制）和离散VQ模型（支持自回归生成但存在梯度稀疏、训练不稳定和码本崩溃问题），两者各有局限，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 提出MergeTok，在编码器-解码器架构中联合优化连续VAE和离散VQ分支，通过编码时聚类相似Token建立结构先验，提供双重监督信号：(i)在VAE分支施加合并Token的语义对齐，正则化潜在空间使其解缠并语义感知；(ii)推导分组约束，促进组内多样性和组间排他性以稳定VQ训练。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上，MergeTok在匹配的Token预算下实现了显著低于强VAE和VQ模型的rFID，重建和生成性能均具有竞争力，且产生的语义化Token表示兼容自回归和扩散生成器。

Conclusion: 单一架构可以使视觉分词器同时具备鲁棒的语义组织和适合生成器的离散性，统一了连续与离散两种分词范式的优势。

Original Abstract: Most visual tokenizers for image generation are bifurcated into two families with complementary limitations: continuous VAEs offer high-fidelity reconstruction but suffer from dense, entangled latents that are poorly suited for semantic control, whereas discrete VQ-based models enable autoregressive generation yet struggle with gradient sparsity, unstable training, and codebook collapse. In this work, we introduce MergeTok, a unified tokenizer that jointly optimizes continuous (VAE) and discrete (VQ) tokenizers within a encoder-decoder architecture, leveraging token merging techniques as a semantic bridge. By clustering similar tokens during encoding, MergeTok establishes a structural prior that provides dual supervision signals: (i) it imposes merged-token semantic alignment in the VAE branch, regularizing its latent space toward disentangled, semantic-aware representations; (ii) it derives group-wise constraints, promoting intra-group diversity and inter-group exclusivity that stabilize VQ training. MergeTok shows competitive reconstruction and generation performance on ImageNet-256, with substantially lower rFID than strong VAE and VQ models under matched token budgets, while producing semantically-organized token representations compatible with both autoregressive and diffusion generators. This shows that a single architecture can endow visual tokenizers with robust semantic organization and generator-friendly discreteness.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数用于图像生成的视觉分词器分为两个具有互补局限性的家族：连续VAE提供高保真重建，但存在稠密且纠缠的潜在表示，难以进行语义控制；而离散VQ模型支持自回归生成，却面临梯度稀疏、训练不稳定和码本崩溃的问题。在这项工作中，我们引入了MergeTok，一种统一的分词器，它在编码器-解码器架构中联合优化连续（VAE）和离散（VQ）分词器，利用Token合并技术作为语义桥梁。通过编码时聚类相似Token，MergeTok建立了一个结构先验，提供双重监督信号：(i)在VAE分支施加合并Token的语义对齐，正则化其潜在空间朝向解缠且语义感知的表征；(ii)推导分组约束，促进组内多样性和组间排他性以稳定VQ训练。MergeTok在ImageNet-256上展现出具有竞争力的重建和生成性能，在匹配的Token预算下，其rFID显著低于强VAE和VQ模型，同时产生语义组织的Token表征，兼容自回归和扩散生成器。这表明，单一架构即可赋予视觉分词器鲁棒的语义组织和适合生成器的离散性。

</details>


### [33] [What Makes LVLMs Hallucinate Less? Unveiling the Architectural Factors Behind Hallucination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30911)
*Yusheng He,Jizhe Zhou,Xia Du,Zheng Lin,Jun Luo,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次从架构设计层面系统探索了大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的幻觉问题，将架构分解为语言基础、视觉表示和语义对齐三个维度，并将幻觉分为共现、相似性和不确定性三种类型，提出了可精细控制幻觉场景的基准CoSimUE。实验揭示了参数规模扩大对减少幻觉效果有限，而增强视觉编码器、分辨率、语言基础及对齐策略可分别缓解不同类型的幻觉，跨维度联合提升效果最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作大多聚焦于改进模型内部组件来减少幻觉，但作者认为幻觉从根本上源于模型架构设计，因此有必要系统探究架构设计选择与幻觉行为之间的映射关系，为开发更可靠的LVLM提供指导。

Method: 将LVLM架构设计分解为语言基础（LF）、视觉表示（VR）和语义对齐（SA）三个维度，将幻觉分为共现、相似性和不确定性三种类型；提出CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动创建细粒度幻觉场景，实现设计选择与幻觉行为之间的映射。

Result: 1) 广泛强调的参数规模扩大对所有三种幻觉的减少效果有限；2) 更大、训练更好的语言基础可减少共现幻觉；3) 更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率可缓解相似性错误；4) 有效的对齐策略可缓解不确定性幻觉；5) 联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量可获得最全面的改进。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统探索了架构级设计如何影响LVLM的幻觉鲁棒性，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导。

Original Abstract: Hallucination remains one of the key challenges undermining the reliability of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs). But what makes an LVLM hallucinate less? Many existing efforts focus on improving internal components of the model. We argue that hallucination fundamentally stems from how the model architecture is designed. To investigate this, we factor the architecture design into three dimensions: Linguistic Foundation (LF), Visual Representation (VR), and Semantic Alignment (SA), and categorize hallucinations into Co-occurrence, Similarity, and previously overlooked Uncertainty types. Building on this formulation, we propose CoSimUE, a benchmark that creates fine-grained hallucination scenarios through controlled textual perturbations and random perturbations, enabling mapping between design choices and hallucination behaviors. Experiments across 7 design aspects show that: 1) the widely emphasized scaling of model parameters has only limited impact on reducing all three types of hallucinations; 2) larger and better-trained language foundations can reduce co-occurrence hallucinations; 3) stronger visual encoders and higher resolutions mitigate similarity errors; 4) effective alignment strategies alleviate uncertainty hallucinations. 5) Furthermore, cross-dimensional analysis reveals that jointly enhancing visual fidelity and alignment quality yields the most comprehensive improvements. This study provides the first systematic exploration linking architecture-level design to hallucination robustness, offering practical guidance for developing reliable and efficient LVLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 幻觉仍然是影响大视觉语言模型（LVLM）可靠性的关键挑战之一。但什么因素能让LVLM产生更少的幻觉？许多现有工作专注于改进模型的内部组件。我们认为幻觉从根本上源于模型架构的设计方式。为了探究这一点，我们将架构设计分解为三个维度：语言基础（LF）、视觉表示（VR）和语义对齐（SA），并将幻觉分为共现（Co-occurrence）、相似性（Similarity）以及以往被忽视的不确定性（Uncertainty）类型。基于这一框架，我们提出了CoSimUE基准，通过受控文本扰动和随机扰动创建细粒度的幻觉场景，从而实现设计选择与幻觉行为之间的映射。跨7个设计方面的实验表明：1）被广泛强调的模型参数规模扩大对所有三种幻觉的减少效果有限；2）更大、训练更好的语言基础可以减少共现幻觉；3）更强的视觉编码器和更高分辨率可缓解相似性错误；4）有效的对齐策略可缓解不确定性幻觉；5）进一步地，跨维度分析表明，联合增强视觉保真度和对齐质量可获得最全面的改进。本研究首次系统探索了架构级设计与幻觉鲁棒性之间的关系，为开发可靠高效的LVLM提供了实用指导。

</details>


### [34] [Attend to Evidence: Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention Supervision for Multimodal RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30912)
*Ruina Hu,Chen Wang,Lai Wei,Jionghao Bai,Bin Yu,Weiran Huang,Kai Wang,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EASE方法，通过视觉证据过程监督增强多模态强化学习，在RL训练中利用标注证据区域引导视觉注意力，显著提升VLM在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理等基准上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅基于最终答案提供结果奖励，无法区分哪些图像区域支撑了答案。对于需要视觉接地的问题，模型可能通过语言先验捷径或幸运猜测获得正确答案，而缺乏真正的视觉证据支持。

Method: EASE将标注的证据区域转化为平滑的视觉令牌目标，在RL训练中仅对高奖励轨迹施加响应到图像的注意力引导。标注仅在训练时作为特权标签使用，推理时无需额外标注。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B上，EASE相比DAPO在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准上平均提升2.5到3.1分。诊断和消融实验表明EASE更好地对齐了视觉注意力与标注证据区域。

Conclusion: EASE通过视觉证据过程监督有效增强了多模态RLVR，使模型不仅关注答案正确性，还能将注意力引导到支撑答案的视觉证据区域，从而提升模型的视觉接地能力和推理可靠性。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language models (VLMs) by optimizing outcome rewards derived from final answers. However, such outcome-only rewards do not tell the model which image regions justify an answer. For questions that require visual grounding, these rewards cannot distinguish responses supported by relevant visual evidence from those produced by language-prior shortcuts or lucky guesses. We introduce EASE (Evidence-Anchored Spatial Attention), which augments multimodal RLVR with visual-evidence process supervision. EASE converts annotated evidence regions into a smoothed visual-token target and uses it to guide response-to-image attention during RL training, but only on high-reward trajectories. The annotations are used solely as privileged training labels, while inference requires only the original image and question. Across Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-4B, and Qwen3-VL-8B, EASE raises average scores over DAPO by 2.5 to 3.1 points on perception, hallucination, visual math, and multimodal reasoning benchmarks. Diagnostics and ablations show that EASE better aligns visual attention with annotated evidence regions.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）通过优化源自最终答案的结果奖励来改进视觉语言模型（VLM）。然而，这种仅基于结果的奖励无法告知模型哪些图像区域证明了答案的正确性。对于需要视觉接地的问题，这些奖励无法区分那些由相关视觉证据支持的响应与通过语言先验捷径或幸运猜测产生的响应。我们提出EASE（证据锚定空间注意力），该方法以视觉证据过程监督来增强多模态RLVR。EASE将标注的证据区域转化为平滑的视觉令牌目标，并在强化学习训练期间仅对高奖励轨迹使用该目标来引导响应到图像的注意力。标注仅作为特权训练标签使用，而推理时仅需要原始图像和问题。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-4B和Qwen3-VL-8B上，EASE在感知、幻觉、视觉数学和多模态推理基准上相比DAPO平均提升2.5到3.1分。诊断和消融实验表明，EASE能够更好地将视觉注意力与标注的证据区域对齐。

</details>


### [35] [DiTTo: Scalable Order-aware All-in-One Image Restoration Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30915)
*Seungho Choi,Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiTTo框架，通过DiTTo模拟器（将ORTD构建复杂度从O((N^D)^2)降至O(N^D)）和顺序感知对齐训练（ORA），实现了高效、可扩展的智能体图像修复，支持即插即用地添加新恢复专家，在MiO-100上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于智能体的图像修复方法存在两个瓶颈：(1) 构建最优恢复动作轨迹数据集（ORTD）需要O((N^D)^2)次专家调用，效率极低；(2) 智能体训练与固定的专家池耦合，无法在不全面重新训练的情况下扩展新的恢复专家。

Method: 提出DiTTo框架，包含两个组件：(1) DiTTo模拟器，结合∪S-IR进行单步恢复动作模拟和AiO-IQA进行每步质量预测，将ORTD构建复杂度降至O(N^D)；(2) DiTTo智能体，在模拟器生成的ORTD上进行SFT训练，再通过顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA）沿独立轴对齐退化识别、恢复动作排序和输出格式，支持即插即用地扩展新恢复专家（仅需更新轻量级ORA阶段）。

Result: 在最多五种并发退化的MiO-100评估集上，DiTTo智能体在先前基于智能体的图像修复方法中达到了最先进的多退化恢复质量。

Conclusion: DiTTo框架通过模拟器大幅降低数据构建成本，并通过ORA实现可插拔扩展，有效解决了现有智能体图像修复方法的效率和可扩展性瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Real-world images rarely suffer from a single degradation, and the order in which degradations are removed substantially affects the final restoration quality, motivating agent-based image restoration (IR), where a vision-language model schedules a pool of pre-built restoration-experts. However, existing training-based agents require $\mathcal{O}((N^{\mathbf{D}})^{2})$ restoration-expert calls per image to construct the Optimal Restoration-action Trajectory Dataset (ORTD), where $N^{\mathbf{D}}$ denotes the number of degradation types in the universe $\mathbf{D}$, and couple agent training to a fixed restoration-expert pool, preventing extension to newly introduced restoration-experts without full retraining. To overcome these efficiency and extensibility bottlenecks, we propose \textbf{DiTTo}, a novel order-aware image restoration agent framework consisting of the DiTTo Simulator and the DiTTo Agent. The DiTTo Simulator combines $\cup$S-IR for single-step restoration-action simulation and AiO-IQA for per-action quality prediction, reducing ORTD construction to $\mathcal{O}(N^{\mathbf{D}})$ simulator calls per image; the DiTTo Agent is trained by SFT on the simulator-generated ORTD, followed by \textbf{Order-aware Restoration Alignment (ORA)} that aligns degradation identification, restoration-action-ordering, and output format along independent axes. This enables \textbf{plug-and-play scalable extensibility}: adding a new restoration-expert requires updating only the lightweight ORA stage. On the MiO-100 evaluation set with up to five concurrent degradations, our DiTTo Agent achieves state-of-the-art multi-degradation restoration quality among previous agent-based IR methods.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界的图像很少只遭受单一退化，而退化移除的顺序会显著影响最终恢复质量，这推动了基于智能体的图像修复（IR）的发展——其中视觉语言模型调度一组预构建的恢复专家。然而，现有的基于训练的智能体需要每张图像调用O((N^D)^2)次恢复专家来构建最优恢复动作轨迹数据集（ORTD），其中N^D表示退化全集D中的退化类型数量，并且将智能体训练与固定的恢复专家池耦合，导致无法在不全面重新训练的情况下扩展到新引入的恢复专家。为了克服这些效率和可扩展性瓶颈，我们提出DiTTo，一种新颖的顺序感知图像修复智能体框架，包括DiTTo模拟器和DiTTo智能体。DiTTo模拟器结合∪S-IR进行单步恢复动作模拟和AiO-IQA进行逐动作质量预测，将ORTD构建降至每张图像O(N^D)次模拟器调用；DiTTo智能体通过在模拟器生成的ORTD上进行SFT训练，然后进行顺序感知恢复对齐（ORA），该对齐沿独立轴对齐退化识别、恢复动作排序和输出格式。这实现了即插即用的可扩展扩展性：添加新的恢复专家只需更新轻量级ORA阶段。在最多五种并发退化的MiO-100评估集上，我们的DiTTo智能体在先前基于智能体的图像修复方法中达到了最先进的多退化恢复质量。

</details>


### [36] [MultiAct: Text-to-Motion Generation from Composite Text via Tailored Attention Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30925)
*Nathan Sala,Ofir Abramovich,Ariel Shamir,Daniel Cohen-Or,Andreas Aristidou,Sigal Raab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiAct 是一个无需重新训练、仅在推理阶段运行的组合文本到动作合成框架，通过自适应增强被忽略提示组件的交叉注意力分数，解决现有模型在处理多动作复合描述时的语义坍缩问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成模型在处理同时发生的多动作复合提示时表现脆弱，往往只生成主体动作而忽略其余动作，导致生成的动作不完整或语义模糊。

Method: 提出 MultiAct，一种无需配对数据、仅在推理阶段运行的 compositional 文本到动作合成框架。该方法通过自适应放大与未被充分表示的提示组件相关的交叉注意力分数来对抗语义坍缩，并引入一个轻量级辅助决策方案来自动选择最有效的注意力增强参数（如目标 token 和层）。

Result: 大量定量和定性评估表明，MultiAct 在复合提示上持续优于现有基线，实现了更高的语义覆盖率，同时保持了动作的真实性。

Conclusion: MultiAct 无需重新训练或修改模型架构，即可有效提升预训练动作生成器处理复合提示的能力，为组合文本到动作合成提供了一种高效且通用的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Text-to-motion generation has progressed rapidly in recent years, offering an expressive interface for animation and human-computer interaction. However, current models remain brittle when handling prompts that describe multiple actions occurring at the same time. Rather than realizing all components of a composite description, models frequently prioritize a single dominant action and neglect the rest, leading to incomplete or ambiguous motion. We present MultiAct, an unpaired, inference-time framework for compositional text-to-motion synthesis that operates directly on pretrained motion generators without retraining or architectural modification. Our method counteracts semantic collapse by adaptively amplifying cross-attention scores associated with underrepresented prompt components. We note that effective modulation depends on prompt-specific choices, such as which tokens and layers to target, and introduce a lightweight auxiliary decision scheme that determines the most effective attention-strengthening parametrization. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that MultiAct consistently outperforms existing baselines on composite prompts, achieving improved semantic coverage while preserving motion realism. Project page: https://natsala13.github.io/multiact.github.io.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，文本到动作生成技术发展迅速，为动画和人机交互提供了富有表现力的接口。然而，当前模型在处理描述同时发生的多个动作的提示时仍然脆弱。模型往往优先生成单一主导动作而忽略其余动作，而不是完整地实现复合描述中的所有成分，导致生成的动作不完整或语义模糊。我们提出了 MultiAct，一个无需配对数据、仅在推理阶段运行的组合文本到动作合成框架，它直接作用于预训练的动作生成器上，无需重新训练或修改架构。我们的方法通过自适应放大与被低估的提示组件相关的交叉注意力分数来对抗语义坍缩。我们注意到有效的调制取决于提示特定的选择，例如要定位哪些 token 和层，并引入了一个轻量级辅助决策方案，以确定最有效的注意力增强参数化。大量定量和定性评估表明，MultiAct 在复合提示上持续优于现有基线，在保持动作真实性的同时实现了更高的语义覆盖率。项目页面：https://natsala13.github.io/multiact.github.io。

</details>


### [37] [Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30968)
*WenZhang Wei,Zhipeng Gui,Dehua Peng,Tiandi Ye,Huayi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出变分适配器（VACSR），将跨模态相似度表示重构成变分推断问题，以缓解因细粒度语义标注稀缺导致的虚假负样本和二值化压缩问题，在图像-文本检索、域泛化和基类到新类泛化任务上验证了有效性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像-文本匹配或分类数据缺乏细粒度跨模态匹配标注，迫使连续相似度空间被压缩成二分类边界，导致虚假负样本并损害跨模态任务泛化性能；先前方法虽尝试建模模态内歧义性，却忽略了固有标注缺陷，造成次优的不确定性分配。

Method: 提出变分适配器（Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation, VACSR），将存在细粒度语义稀缺的图像-文本匹配重构成变分推断问题，构建跨模态相似度的隐空间，并利用正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合。

Result: 在图像-文本检索、域泛化和基类到新类泛化三个任务上的实验表明，所提方法有效且具备鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的VACSR通过变分推断框架有效解决了跨模态相似度表示中的二值化压缩和虚假负样本问题，在多个跨模态任务上展现出优越的泛化性能。

Original Abstract: The core of vision-language models lies in measuring cross-modal similarity within a unified representation space. However, most image-text matching or multi-class image classification datasets lack fine-grained cross-modal matching annotations, forcing the continuous similarity space into binary classification boundaries. This compression induces false negative samples and significantly impairs the generalization performance of cross-modal tasks. While prior research has attempted to mitigate this by modeling intra-modal ambiguity, it often overlooks inherent annotation flaws, leading to suboptimal uncertainty allocation. To address these challenges, we propose a Variational Adapter for Cross-modal Similarity Representation (VACSR). This approach reformulates image-text matching with fine-grained semantic scarcity as a variational inference problem. It constructs a latent space for cross-modal similarity and uses regularization techniques to mitigate overfitting to binary annotations. Experiments on image-text retrieval, domain generalization, and base-to-novel generalization demonstrate the proposed method's effectiveness and robust generalization ability.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型的核心在于在统一表示空间中度量跨模态相似性。然而，大多数图像-文本匹配或多类图像分类数据集缺乏细粒度的跨模态匹配标注，迫使连续的相似度空间被压缩到二分类边界中。这种压缩引入了虚假负样本，并显著损害了跨模态任务的泛化性能。虽然先前的研究试图通过建模模态内歧义性来缓解这一问题，但通常忽略了固有的标注缺陷，导致不确定性分配次优。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种用于跨模态相似性表示的变分适配器（VACSR）。该方法将存在细粒度语义稀缺的图像-文本匹配重新表述为变分推断问题，构建跨模态相似性的隐空间，并利用正则化技术缓解对二值标注的过拟合。在图像-文本检索、域泛化和基类到新类泛化上的实验表明，所提方法具有有效性和鲁棒的泛化能力。

</details>


### [38] [Omni-Supervised Motion Editing: Balancing Change and Invariance through Positive-Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30969)
*Zhenwu Shi,Jingyu Gong,Peiwei Wang,Xingzan Wang,Tianwen Qian,Wenxi Li,Yuan Fang,Jiao Xie,Lizhuang Ma,Shaohui Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniME，一个全监督正负学习框架，通过回顾性特征监督、运动保持机制和三元组语义对齐三个互补组件，在文本驱动的人体运动编辑中平衡修改与保留，实现最先进的编辑对齐性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法依赖启发式相似性线索或粗糙的全局条件，导致运动失真和语义对齐不佳，核心挑战在于如何在精确编辑目标区域的同时保持未编辑部分的不变性。

Method: 提出OmniME全监督正负学习框架，包含三个互补组件：(1)回顾性特征监督——跨Transformer层强制粗到细的一致性；(2)运动保持机制——根据源-目标相似性关注细微变化；(3)三元组语义对齐——增强文本-运动对应关系。三者形成统一监督范式以平衡变化与不变性。

Result: 在MotionFix和STANCE Adjustment数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniME在编辑对齐方面达到了最先进的性能，验证了统一学习框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的OmniME框架通过正负学习范式有效解决了文本驱动人体运动编辑中变化与不变性的平衡问题，代码和模型已开源。

Original Abstract: Text-based human motion editing aims to modify existing motion sequences according to natural language instructions while maintaining the consistency of the original motion. Existing diffusion-based approaches often rely on heuristic similarity cues or coarse global conditioning, leading to motion distortion and suboptimal semantic alignment. The key challenge lies in balancing change (i.e. precisely editing target regions) and invariance (i.e. preserving unedited parts). To handle such challenge, we propose an Omni-Supervised Positive-Negative Learning framework, named OmniME. Our method integrates three complementary components: (1) retrospective feature supervision that enforces coarse-to-fine consistency across transformer layers,(2) motion preservation mechanism that focuses on subtle variations according to the source-target similarity, and (3) triplet-based semantic alignment that strengthens text-motion correspondence. Together, these components form a unified supervision paradigm that balances change and invariance. Extensive experiments on the MotionFix and STANCE Adjustment datasets demonstrate that OmniME achieves state-of-the-art performance in editing alignment, validating the effectiveness of our unified learning framework. Our source codes and models have been released at: https://github.com/rocket-ycyer/OmniME.git

中文摘要翻译: 基于文本的人体运动编辑旨在根据自然语言指令修改现有运动序列，同时保持原始运动的一致性。现有的基于扩散的方法往往依赖启发式相似性线索或粗糙的全局条件，导致运动失真和次优的语义对齐。核心挑战在于平衡变化（即精确编辑目标区域）与不变性（即保留未编辑部分）。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种全监督的正负学习框架，命名为OmniME。我们的方法整合了三个互补组件：(1)回顾性特征监督，跨Transformer层强制执行粗到细的一致性；(2)运动保持机制，根据源-目标相似性关注细微变化；(3)三元组语义对齐，增强文本-运动对应关系。这些组件共同构成一个统一的监督范式，以平衡变化与不变性。在MotionFix和STANCE Adjustment数据集上的大量实验表明，OmniME在编辑对齐方面达到了最先进的性能，验证了我们的统一学习框架的有效性。我们的源代码和模型已在https://github.com/rocket-ycyer/OmniME.git开源。

</details>


### [39] [Can BEV Perception Gracefully Degrade under Sensor Failures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30983)
*Haifa Zhang,Yijing Wang,Haoyu Wang,Zheng Li,Zhiqiang Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Grace-BEV，一种轻量级即插即用框架，通过对多模态BEV融合进行主动可靠性评估，实现传感器损坏场景下的优雅降级，在LiDAR完全失效时将mAP从0.0%恢复至34.7%，并提升干净数据精度最高1.4%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶多模态BEV感知系统的融合机制对传感器损坏高度脆弱，静态融合方式在模态缺失或损坏时会导致性能急剧下降，亟需一种能够主动评估模态可靠性并实现优雅降级的融合方法。

Method: (1) 提出Grace-BEV框架，包含TrustGate Router用于显式评估各模态可信度，以及FailSafe Fusion Block用于动态重校准特征融合；(2) 设计三阶段训练策略（Three-Phase Training）配合模态丢弃（Modality Dropout），防止模态主导并促进不平衡输入下的跨模态均衡学习。

Result: 在nuScenes-R和nuScenes-C数据集上，Grace-BEV在各种损坏场景下保持鲁棒性能。在LiDAR完全失效导致基线模型mAP降至0.0%时，Grace-BEV恢复至34.7% mAP；同时提升干净数据精度最高1.4%，实现鲁棒性与效率的强平衡。

Conclusion: 通过对多模态融合引入主动可靠性感知机制，可以显著提升自动驾驶BEV感知系统在传感器损坏情况下的鲁棒性，实现优雅的性能降级而非完全崩溃，且不引入过多计算开销。

Original Abstract: Despite the remarkable success of multi-modal bird's-eye view (BEV) perception in autonomous driving, current systems exhibit a critical vulnerability: existing fusion mechanisms are highly brittle to sensor corruptions, often causing catastrophic performance degradation. This vulnerability largely stems from the fact that standard fusion frameworks typically integrate multi-modal representations in a static manner, leading to a precipitous performance collapse under missing or corrupted modalities. In contrast, we show that graceful degradation is achievable through active modality reliability assessment. To this end, we present Grace-BEV, a lightweight and plug-and-play framework that enforces active reliability awareness during multi-modal fusion. Instead of relying on computationally expensive cross-modal interactions, Grace-BEV leverages the aligned BEV space to explicitly assess modality trustworthiness via a TrustGate Router and dynamically recalibrate feature integration using the FailSafe Fusion Block. Furthermore, we devise a Three-Phase Training strategy with Modality Dropout to prevent modality dominance and encourage balanced cross-modal learning under unreliable inputs. Extensive experiments on nuScenes-R and nuScenes-C show that Grace-BEV maintains robust performance across diverse corruption settings. Notably, under catastrophic LiDAR failures where standard baselines collapse to 0.0% mean Average Precision (mAP), Grace-BEV restores performance to as high as 34.7% mAP. Moreover, it improves clean accuracy by up to 1.4%, achieving a strong trade-off between robustness and efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态鸟瞰图（BEV）感知在自动驾驶中取得了显著成功，但现有系统存在一个关键漏洞：当前的融合机制对传感器损坏高度脆弱，常常导致灾难性的性能下降。这一漏洞主要源于标准融合框架通常以静态方式集成多模态表示，导致在模态缺失或损坏时性能急剧崩溃。相反，我们证明通过主动的模态可靠性评估可以实现优雅降级。为此，我们提出Grace-BEV，一个轻量级且即插即用的框架，在多模态融合过程中强制执行主动可靠性感知。Grace-BEV不依赖计算昂贵的跨模态交互，而是利用对齐的BEV空间，通过TrustGate Router显式评估模态可信度，并利用FailSafe Fusion Block动态重校准特征融合。此外，我们设计了三阶段训练策略（Three-Phase Training）配合模态丢弃（Modality Dropout），以防止模态主导并鼓励在不可靠输入下的均衡跨模态学习。在nuScenes-R和nuScenes-C上的大量实验表明，Grace-BEV在各种损坏场景下均保持鲁棒性能。值得注意的是，在灾难性的LiDAR故障下，当标准基线模型平均精度（mAP）降至0.0%时，Grace-BEV将性能恢复至高达34.7% mAP。此外，它还提升了干净数据精度最高1.4%，实现了鲁棒性与效率之间的强平衡。

</details>


### [40] [Generating Reports or Repeating Templates? Measuring and Mitigating Template Collapse in 3D CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30984)
*Tom Maye-Lasserre,Yitong Li,Bailiang Jian,Morteza Ghahremani,Benedikt Wiestler,Christian Wachinger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别了3D医学视觉-语言模型中的“模板坍塌”问题（即模型倾向生成通用模板而忽略罕见但关键的病理发现），并提出CLarGen解耦框架——将“检测什么”（临床检测）与“如何表述”（语言合成）分离，通过潜在查询变压器、病理引导检索和医学语言模型显著提升了临床准确性和输出多样性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代3D医学视觉-语言模型虽然能生成流畅的放射学风格文本，但在病理检测和输出多样性方面表现极差，倾向于生成通用模板，低估了罕见但关键的发现。这种“模板坍塌”源于3D医学成像的独特约束：数据有限、标签严重不平衡、体素编码器信号弱，导致文本生成目标鼓励捷径学习和弱化接地性的报告。

Method: 提出CLarGen解耦框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) 潜在查询变压器（Latent Query Transformer）用于多标签病理检测；(ii) 病理引导检索（pathology-guided retrieval）获取临床匹配的范例；(iii) 医学语言模型基于检测到的发现和检索到的上下文合成最终报告。该框架将“说什么”（临床检测）与“怎么说”（语言合成）分离。

Result: 在3D CT报告生成基线方法上，CLarGen缓解了模板坍塌，临床准确性显著提升：macro-F1从0.189提升至0.487，CRG从0.368提升至0.472，同时保持流畅的报告生成。

Conclusion: 明确的、可测量的临床接地性对于抵抗模板坍塌的3D CT报告生成至关重要。

Original Abstract: Modern 3D medical vision-language models (VLMs) can generate fluent radiology-style text while exhibit critically low pathology detection and output diversity, collapsing to generic templates that under-report rare yet critical findings. We identify this failure mode as Template Collapse. This failure stems from the unique constraints of 3D medical imaging, e.g., limited data, severe label imbalance, and weak signals from volumetric encoders. Under these constraints, text-generation objectives encourage shortcut learning and fluent but weakly grounded reports. We systematically diagnose the Template Collapse through clinical fidelity, output diversity, normal-template bias, and rare-finding survival. To mitigate it, we propose CLarGen, a decoupled framework that separates what to say (clinical detection) from how to say it (language synthesis). CLarGen uses (i) a Latent Query Transformer for multi-label pathology detection, (ii) pathology-guided retrieval for clinically matched exemplars, and (iii) a medical language model to synthesize the final report from detected findings and retrieved context. Across state-of-the-art 3D CT report generation baselines, CLarGen mitigates Template Collapse and substantially improves clinical accuracy (macro-F1 0.487 vs. 0.189; CRG 0.472 vs. 0.368) while maintaining fluent reporting. Our results suggest that explicit, measurable clinical grounding is essential for template-collapse-resistant 3D CT report generation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 现代3D医学视觉-语言模型能够生成流畅的放射学风格文本，但在病理检测和输出多样性方面表现出严重不足，趋向于生成通用模板，从而低估了罕见但关键的发现。我们将这种失效模式定义为“模板坍塌”。该问题源于3D医学成像的独特约束，例如数据有限、标签严重不平衡以及体素编码器的弱信号。在这些约束下，文本生成目标鼓励了捷径学习和流畅但接地性较弱的报告。我们通过临床保真度、输出多样性、正常模板偏差和罕见发现存活率等方面系统性地诊断了模板坍塌。为了缓解这一问题，我们提出了CLarGen——一个解耦框架，将“说什么”（临床检测）与“怎么说”（语言合成）分离开来。CLarGen使用：(i) 潜在查询变压器进行多标签病理检测；(ii) 病理引导检索以获取临床匹配的范例；(iii) 医学语言模型根据检测到的发现和检索到的上下文合成最终报告。在多种先进的3D CT报告生成基线方法中，CLarGen缓解了模板坍塌，并显著提高了临床准确性（macro-F1 0.487 vs. 0.189；CRG 0.472 vs. 0.368），同时保持了流畅的报告生成。我们的结果表明，明确且可测量的临床接地性对于抗模板坍塌的3D CT报告生成至关重要。代码将在论文接收后开源。

</details>


### [41] [Benchmarking Single-Step Inpainting Methods for Multi-Object 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30987)
*Finn Dröge,Cecilia Curreli,Abhishek Saroha,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在3D高斯泼溅场景中移除物体并进行修复的方法，发现基于重建的2D修复器在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型，从头初始化场景比微调现有场景效果更好，并提出了一个新的多物体360°数据集用于评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 针对3DGS场景中物体移除和修复面临的跨视角3D一致性问题，以及现有360°数据集缺乏真实标注和遮挡场景的问题，探索不同2D修复器在3D域中的适用性并建立有效基线。

Method: 比较了基于重建的修复器和生成式扩散模型的3D一致性表现，将2D修复器集成到不同的单步方法中用于创建和微调3DGS场景，并提出先移除物体再进行修复的流程。

Result: 基于重建的修复器在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型；从头初始化场景比微调现有场景产生更高质量的结果；先移除物体再修复的策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 在3DGS场景中，使用基于重建的2D修复器、从头初始化场景、并遵循先移除后修复的流程，可以获得更好的3D一致性和修复质量。同时，新提出的多物体遮挡数据集为未来研究提供了有价值的基准。

Original Abstract: The tasks of object removal and inpainting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scenes face challenges such as 3D consistency across camera views. In comparing 2D inpainters and their suitability for the 3D domain, we find that reconstruction-based inpainters outperform generative diffusion models in 3D consistency. Integrating these 2D inpainters into different single-step methods for creating and finetuning 3DGS scenes, our results indicate that initializing the scene from scratch produces higher quality results than finetuning the existing scene. Using a state-of-the-art generative 2D inpainter, we create a straightforward baseline to underline the importance of object removal before inpainting in the 3D setting. Since 360° datasets rarely include real-world ground truths, and challenging occlusion scenarios are equally sparse, we introduce a novel multi-object scene with recorded ground truth data and many views with object occlusions.

中文摘要翻译: 在3D高斯泼溅场景中执行物体移除和修复任务面临着跨视角3D一致性的挑战。通过比较不同2D修复器在3D域中的适用性，我们发现基于重建的修复器在3D一致性上优于生成式扩散模型。将这些2D修复器集成到创建和微调3DGS场景的不同单步方法中，我们的结果表明从头初始化场景比微调现有场景产生更高质量的结果。利用最先进的生成式2D修复器，我们建立了一个直观的基线，强调了在3D设置中先移除物体再进行修复的重要性。由于360°数据集很少包含真实世界的标注数据，且具有挑战性的遮挡场景同样稀缺，我们引入了一个新颖的多物体场景，其中包含记录的真实标注数据和大量带有物体遮挡的视角。

</details>


### [42] [PEEK: Picking Essential frames via Efficient Knowledge distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31029)
*Killian Steunou,Anas Filali Razzouki,Khalil Guetari,Mounîm A. El-Yacoubi,Yannis Tevissen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PEEK是一种高效的动态帧采样方法，通过从强教师模型蒸馏帧相关性排序到轻量级时序模型，在低帧预算下显著优于现有方法，且计算开销极小（仅增加5.2%的推理时间）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频语言模型只能处理有限帧数，均匀采样忽略视觉内容，而自适应采样虽能选取信息帧但计算成本高昂，亟需一种既准确又高效的动态帧选择方法。

Method: PEEK首先利用强教师模型（如VLM）生成基于字幕条件的帧相关性排序，然后将排序知识蒸馏到仅依赖视觉内容的轻量级时序模型中，实现高效的动态帧采样。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions和MSR-VTT上，PEEK在所有下游视觉语言模型中优于现有方法，尤其在低帧预算下表现最佳（ActivityNet Captions上16个配置中赢得14个）。计算开销仅增加5.2%，远低于CSTA（65.4%）和MaxInfo（211.9%）。零样本评估在低帧预算下迁移效果最优。

Conclusion: PEEK在低帧预算场景下兼具高精度和高效率，为视频字幕任务中的自适应帧采样提供了实用且轻量的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Video-language models can process only a limited number of frames, making frame selection a key bottleneck for efficient video captioning. Most captioning pipelines still rely on uniform sampling, which is computationally cheap but agnostic to visual content. Adaptive frame sampling has recently emerged as a promising approach for selecting the most informative frames from a video; however, existing methods remain computationally expensive. We introduce PEEK, an efficient dynamic frame sampling method that distills caption-conditioned frame relevance rankings from a stronger teacher model into a lightweight temporal model that operates only on visual content. We find that, overall, on ActivityNet Captions and MSR-VTT, our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all evaluated downstream vision language models, especially when only one or two frames are selected for captioning, obtaining the best CIDEr for most frame budgets. On ActivityNet Captions, PEEK is particularly strong, winning 14 out of 16 configurations. Zero-shot evaluation on MSR-VTT shows that our model transfers best at low frame budgets, while results at four and eight frames are more mixed as temporal coverage and visual diversity become increasingly competitive. Compared with recent adaptive baselines, PEEK is both more accurate in the low-budget regime and more efficient: it adds only $5.2\%$ to the captioning time, compared with $65.4\%$ for CSTA and $211.9\%$ for MaxInfo. We release our code and pre-trained checkpoint at https://github.com/momentslab/peek.

中文摘要翻译: 视频语言模型只能处理有限数量的帧，这使得帧选择成为高效视频字幕生成的关键瓶颈。大多数字幕生成流程仍依赖均匀采样，该方法计算成本低但忽视视觉内容。自适应帧采样最近作为一种从视频中选择最具信息量帧的有前景方法出现，然而现有方法计算成本仍然高昂。我们提出PEEK，一种高效的动态帧采样方法，它将基于字幕条件的帧相关性排序从更强的教师模型蒸馏到一个仅处理视觉内容的轻量级时序模型中。我们发现，总体而言，在ActivityNet Captions和MSR-VTT上，我们的方法在所有评估的下游视觉语言模型中均优于现有方法，尤其是在仅选择一两帧用于字幕生成时，在大多数帧预算下获得了最佳CIDEr分数。在ActivityNet Captions上，PEEK表现尤为突出，在16个配置中赢得了14个。在MSR-VTT上的零样本评估表明，我们的模型在低帧预算下迁移效果最佳，而在四帧和八帧时的结果则更趋混合，因为此时时序覆盖和视觉多样性逐渐成为竞争因素。与最近的自适应基线方法相比，PEEK在低预算场景下既更准确也更高效：它仅增加5.2%的字幕生成时间，而CSTA增加65.4%，MaxInfo增加211.9%。我们在https://github.com/momentslab/peek上发布我们的代码和预训练检查点。

</details>


### [43] [GGT-100K: Generative Ground Truth for Generalizable Real-World Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31039)
*Xiangtao Kong,Jixin Zhao,Lingchen Sun,Rongyuan Wu,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生成式真值（GGT）方法，利用多模态基础模型（MFM）从真实低质量图像合成高质量目标，构建GGT-100K配对数据集，显著提升多种图像恢复模型的真实世界泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实图像恢复任务受限于高质量配对训练数据的稀缺性：合成数据集无法模拟真实退化，而真实配对数据采集成本高昂，导致现有模型在真实场景中泛化能力有限。

Method: 系统评估9种先进MFM（含Nano-Banana-2、GPT-Image-2）在不同场景和退化类型图像上的性能，选取Nano-Banana-2结合VLM自适应提示作为生成式真值合成工具，构建包含多阶段质量控制的GGT合成流程，最终产出GGT-100K数据集（103,707训练对+500测试对），覆盖多样场景和复杂真实退化。

Result: Nano-Banana-2+VL M自适应提示在感知真实性和内容保真度上表现最优；GGT-100K数据集一致性地提升了多种图像恢复模型的真实世界泛化能力，对生成式IR模型的微调效果尤为显著。

Conclusion: 多模态基础模型可作为面向图像恢复的数据生成实用工具，GGT-100K是扩展真实世界图像恢复模型泛化边界的有用资源。

Original Abstract: Real-world image restoration (IR) is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high-quality paired training data. Synthetic datasets are abundant but often fail to model real-world degradations, while real-world paired datasets are expensive and difficult to capture. As a result, IR models trained on these datasets show limited generalization in real-world scenarios. In this work, we propose Generative Ground Truth (GGT) by using generative multimodal foundation models (MFMs) to produce high-quality (HQ) targets from real-world low-quality (LQ) images. We first conduct a systematic evaluation of nine state-of-the-art MFMs, including Nano-Banana-2 and GPT-Image-2, on images of various scenes and degradation types. The results demonstrate that Nano-Banana-2 with VLM-based adaptive prompting shows the highest capability to synthesize perceptually realistic and content-faithful HQ targets, which can serve as the GGT for the LQ input. We then employ Nano-Banana-2 to build a GGT synthesis pipeline, which involves multi-stage quality control to ensure data reliability, and construct GGT-100K, an LQ-HQ paired dataset comprising 103,707 training pairs and covering diverse scenes and complex real-world degradations. A test set of 500 image pairs is also established. Extensive experiments show that GGT-100K consistently improves the real-world generalization of a wide range of IR models, with particularly strong benefits for finetuning generative models for IR tasks. Our results suggest that MFMs can serve as practical tools for restoration-oriented data generation, and GGT-100K is a useful resource to expand the generalization boundaries of real-world IR models.

中文摘要翻译: 真实世界的图像恢复（IR）受限于高质量配对训练数据的稀缺性。合成数据集虽然丰富，但通常无法模拟真实世界的退化，而真实世界的配对数据集则成本高昂且难以采集。因此，基于这些数据集训练的IR模型在真实场景中的泛化能力有限。在这项工作中，我们提出生成式真值（GGT）方法，利用生成式多模态基础模型（MFM）从真实世界的低质量（LQ）图像中生成高质量（HQ）目标。我们首先对包括Nano-Banana-2和GPT-Image-2在内的九种最先进的MFM进行了系统评估，涵盖了多种场景和退化类型的图像。结果表明，Nano-Banana-2结合基于VLM的自适应提示在合成感知真实且内容忠实的高质量目标方面展现出最强能力，可作为LQ输入的GGT。随后，我们采用Nano-Banana-2构建了GGT合成流水线，该流水线包含多阶段质量控制以确保数据可靠性，并构建了GGT-100K——一个包含103,707个训练对、覆盖多样场景和复杂真实退化的LQ-HQ配对数据集。此外，还建立了包含500个图像对的测试集。大量实验表明，GGT-100K能够一致性地提升多种IR模型在真实世界的泛化能力，对微调用于IR任务的生成式模型尤为有益。我们的结果表明，MFM可以作为面向图像恢复的数据生成的实用工具，而GGT-100K是拓展真实世界IR模型泛化边界的有用资源。

</details>


### [44] [Does Visual Information Play a Decisive Role in Vision-Language-Action Model Driving Behavior?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31041)
*Jingtao He,Hongliang Lu,Xiaoyun Qiu,Yixuan Wang,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构化多级视觉扰动框架，系统分析VLA驾驶模型中视觉信息与驾驶行为之间的依赖关系，揭示了评估方式依赖的依赖模式和不同抽象层级上不均匀的视觉基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型在感知与规划联合建模上表现优异，但视觉信息如何影响驾驶行为尚不清楚，且现有评估协议仅关注聚合性能指标，缺乏结构化、可操作的诊断方法来量化视觉-行为依赖关系。

Method: 提出结构化多级视觉扰动框架，沿三个正交维度（通道级退化、信息级破坏、结构级修改）组织受控视觉扰动，应用于VLA驾驶系统，在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种场景下评估行为响应。

Result: 实验揭示了评估方式依赖的依赖模式，以及不同抽象层级上不均匀的视觉基础。

Conclusion: 需要更结构化的分析和更严谨的VLA驾驶模型设计以理解视觉信息如何塑造行为，并开发更安全、更鲁棒的系统。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capability in autonomous driving, highlighting the potential of unified multimodal architectures for jointly modeling perception and planning. However, how current VLA-based driving behavior is grounded in visual information remains poorly understood. Existing evaluation protocols mainly focus on aggregate performance metrics, lacking structured and practical diagnostics to quantify visual-behavior dependency. In this work, we introduce a structured multi-level visual perturbation framework to analyze visual-behavior dependency in VLA-based driving models systematically. The framework organizes controlled visual perturbations along three complementary dimensions: channellevel degradation, information-level disruption, and structurelevel modification. We apply it to VLA-based driving systems and evaluate behavioral responses under both open-loop trajectory prediction and interactive closed-loop safety evaluation. Experimental results reveal evaluation-dependent dependency patterns and uneven visual grounding across abstraction levels. These findings call for more structured analyses and principled design of VLA driving models to better understand how visual information shapes behavior and develop safer, more robust systems.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作模型在自动驾驶领域展现了令人期待的能力，突显了统一多模态架构在联合建模感知与规划方面的潜力。然而，当前基于VLA的驾驶行为如何根植于视觉信息尚不明确。现有评估协议主要关注聚合性能指标，缺乏结构化且可操作的诊断方法来量化视觉-行为依赖关系。本文提出一个结构化的多级视觉扰动框架，用于系统分析VLA驾驶模型中的视觉-行为依赖关系。该框架沿三个互补维度组织受控视觉扰动：通道级退化、信息级破坏和结构级修改。我们将其应用于VLA驾驶系统，并在开环轨迹预测和交互式闭环安全评估两种条件下评估行为响应。实验结果表明，依赖模式与评估方式相关，且不同抽象层级的视觉基础不均匀。这些发现呼吁对VLA驾驶模型进行更结构化的分析和更严谨的设计，以更好地理解视觉信息如何塑造行为，并开发更安全、更鲁棒的系统。

</details>


### [45] [LVSA: Training-Free Sparse Attention for Long Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31057)
*Gael Glorian,Ioannis Lamprou,Zhen Zhang,Yujie Yuan,Hongsheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LVSA，一种无需训练、与模型无关的块稀疏注意力机制，通过结合结构化窗口模式与旋转全局锚点，消除固定网格偏差，在长视频扩散推理中实现2.98–3.33倍加速，并支持单GPU生成超训练长度视频，同时保持或提升生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 密集自注意力是长视频扩散推理的计算和质量瓶颈：计算量随序列长度二次增长，且超出训练长度时模型产生近静态的“冻结”重复视频。现有方法要么需要重训练成本过高，要么无法兼顾性能与质量的可扩展性。

Method: LVSA是一种无需训练、与模型无关的块稀疏注意力机制，针对视频扩散Transformer设计。它结合了结构化窗口模式（关注局部时序连贯性）与旋转全局锚点（捕捉长程依赖），从而消除了导致长程时序伪影的固定网格偏差。结合FlashInfer内核实现高效计算。

Result: 在Wan 2.1 1.3B上6倍长度时计算量减少3.17倍，Wan 2.1 14B上6倍长度时减少2.98倍，HunyuanVideo 1.5上1.5倍长度时减少3.33倍。支持HunyuanVideo 1.5在单GPU上生成2倍训练长度的视频（原本会OOM）。相比RIFLEx加速2.41倍，相比UltraViCo加速3.27倍。在NPU上也实现2.71–3.24倍加速。在训练长度上质量中性，在超长长度上质量正向。

Conclusion: LVSA是一种高效、可扩展的稀疏注意力方案，在不牺牲质量的前提下显著降低长视频扩散推理的计算开销，且无需重新训练模型，适用于多种硬件平台。

Original Abstract: Dense self-attention is the compute and quality bottleneck of long-video diffusion inference: cost grows quadratically with the sequence length, and beyond the training horizon the model converges to near-static output, that is, "frozen" repetitive video. State of the art approaches are either too costly, e.g., they require retraining, or fail to satisfy both performance and quality objectives in a scalable manner. To this end, we introduce Long Video Sparse Attention (LVSA), a training-free model-agnostic block-sparse attention for video diffusion transformers that combines a structured window pattern with rotating global anchors, thus removing the fixed-grid bias which causes long-range temporal artifacts. LVSA, combined with a FlashInfer kernel, reduces compute up to 3.17x on Wan 2.1 1.3B at a 6x horizon, 2.98x on Wan 2.1 14B at a 6x horizon, and 3.33x on HunyuanVideo 1.5 at a 1.5x horizon, compared to dense attention. Beyond reducing compute, LVSA enables HunyuanVideo 1.5 generation at a 2x horizon, which is otherwise out-of-memory on a single GPU. Moreover, LVSA provides speedups up to 2.41x compared to RIFLEx and 3.27x compared to UltraViCo on Wan 2.1 1.3B. To demonstrate applicability across diverse platforms, we apply LVSA on NPUs and achieve speedups up to 2.71x on Wan 2.2 A14B and 3.24x on Wan 2.1 1.3B compared to dense attention. To evaluate quality in a fair way, we introduce VQeval, a tool properly scoring loopy video failures, which instead are rewarded in state of the art evaluators like VBench-Long. LVSA is quality-neutral for generation at training horizon length and quality-positive at extended lengths.

中文摘要翻译: 密集自注意力是长视频扩散推理中的计算与质量瓶颈：计算成本随序列长度呈二次增长，且超出训练范围时模型会收敛到近乎静态的输出，即“冻结”的重复视频。当前最先进的方法要么成本过高（例如需要重新训练），要么无法以可扩展的方式同时满足性能和质量目标。为此，我们提出了长视频稀疏注意力（LVSA），一种无需训练、与模型无关的块稀疏注意力机制，专为视频扩散Transformer设计。它结合了结构化窗口模式与旋转全局锚点，从而消除了导致长程时序伪影的固定网格偏差。LVSA结合FlashInfer内核，与密集注意力相比，在Wan 2.1 1.3B的6倍长度上计算量减少3.17倍，在Wan 2.1 14B的6倍长度上减少2.98倍，在HunyuanVideo 1.5的1.5倍长度上减少3.33倍。除减少计算量外，LVSA使得HunyuanVideo 1.5可以在单GPU上生成2倍训练长度的视频，而这原本会导致显存溢出。此外，在Wan 2.1 1.3B上，LVSA相比RIFLEx提供高达2.41倍的加速，相比UltraViCo提供高达3.27倍的加速。为展示跨不同平台的适用性，我们将LVSA应用于NPU，并在Wan 2.2 A14B上实现高达2.71倍、在Wan 2.1 1.3B上实现高达3.24倍的加速（相比密集注意力）。为公平评估质量，我们引入了VQeval，一种能够正确评分循环视频失败的工具——而这种失败在VBench-Long等现有评估工具中反而会被奖励。LVSA在训练长度上的生成质量是中性的，在扩展长度上则质量正向提升。

</details>


### [46] [HQ-JEPA: Hybrid Quantum Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Cross-Modal Remote Sensing Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31068)
*Md Aminur Hossain,Ayush V. Patel,Sanjay K. Singh,Biplab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HQ-JEPA是一种混合量子-经典联合嵌入预测架构，用于跨模态遥感表示学习，通过在潜空间中进行掩码预测、跨模态对齐、高斯正则化和量子保真度相似性损失，在GeoBench分类与分割任务上取得优于现有基线的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 遥感领域跨模态（Sentinel-1与Sentinel-2）表示学习面临模态差异大、标注数据稀缺等问题，现有方法多依赖像素级重构，难以学到高质量的语义表示。作者希望引入量子相似性度量与预测式自监督学习来提升跨模态遥感表示的质量。

Method: HQ-JEPA包含四个互补目标：(1) 潜层令牌预测（JEPA风格掩码预测）；(2) 跨模态令牌对齐；(3) 融合潜空间的SIGReg高斯正则化；(4) 基于可微SWAP测试的保真度量子相似性（FQS）损失。模型在潜空间中直接学习语义表示，利用量子态重叠相似度作为正则化信号。

Result: 在GeoBench的分类和分割任务上，经过线性探测和微调评估，HQ-JEPA在多个设置下达到了优于或持平于强自监督基线及遥感基础模型基线的性能，验证了预测式自监督、跨模态几何正则化与量子保真度表示学习的有效性。

Conclusion: 整合预测式自监督学习、跨模态几何正则化和量子保真度相似性学习可以有效提升遥感跨模态表示的质量，HQ-JEPA为遥感基础模型提供了一种新的混合量子-经典范式。

Original Abstract: We introduce HQ-JEPA, a hybrid quantum-classical joint-embedding predictive architecture for cross-modal remote sensing representation learning. The proposed framework extends JEPA-style masked latent prediction to paired Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 imagery by predicting masked target representations from visible context regions while aligning heterogeneous modality features in a shared embedding space. To improve representation quality, HQ-JEPA combines four complementary objectives: latent token prediction, cross-modal token alignment, SIGReg-based Gaussian regularization in the fused latent space, and a differentiable SWAP-test-based Fidelity Quantum Similarity (FQS) loss. Unlike pixel reconstruction methods, HQ-JEPA learns semantic representations directly in latent space and uses quantum state-overlap-based similarity as an additional regularization signal. We evaluate the pretrained encoder on GeoBench classification and segmentation tasks under linear probing and fine-tuning settings. Results show that HQ-JEPA achieves competitive and often superior performance over strong self-supervised and remote sensing foundation-model baselines, demonstrating the benefit of integrating predictive self-supervision, cross-modal geometric regularization, and quantum fidelity-based representation learning for remote sensing applications.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了HQ-JEPA，一种用于跨模态遥感表示学习的混合量子-经典联合嵌入预测架构。该框架将JEPA风格的掩码潜层预测扩展到配对的Sentinel-1和Sentinel-2图像，通过从可见上下文区域预测被掩码的目标表示，同时在共享嵌入空间中对齐异质模态特征。为了提升表示质量，HQ-JEPA结合了四个互补目标：潜层令牌预测、跨模态令牌对齐、融合潜空间中的SIGReg高斯正则化，以及基于可微SWAP测试的保真度量子相似性（FQS）损失。与像素重构方法不同，HQ-JEPA直接在潜空间中学习语义表示，并利用量子态重叠相似度作为额外的正则化信号。我们在GeoBench分类与分割任务上，在线性探测和微调设置下评估了预训练编码器。结果表明，HQ-JEPA在多个场景下取得了优于或持平于强自监督基线和遥感基础模型基线的性能，验证了将预测式自监督、跨模态几何正则化与量子保真度表示学习相结合对遥感应用的有效性。

</details>


### [47] [Towards Effective Long-Video Event Prediction via Multi-Level Event Semantics Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31069)
*Bo Peng,YuanJie Lyu,PengGang Qin,Tong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA，一种多层级事件语义挖掘框架，用于长视频事件预测。VISTA通过角色中心视觉提示提取细节、知识增强迭代检索构建事件链、以及类人提出-检索策略生成多样化预测，在真实数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦文本或短视频事件预测，长视频因多模态上下文复杂、叙事结构丰富而未被充分探索；同时，现有的长视频语言模型虽在问答和摘要上表现良好，但无法精确提取事件细节并进行细粒度事件发展分析，难以泛化到事件预测任务。

Method: VISTA框架包含三部分：(1) 角色中心视觉提示，精确提取事件相关的视觉细节，增强细节级语义；(2) 知识增强迭代检索策略，引导大语言模型逐步构建逻辑一致的事件链，提升事件级叙事；(3) 类人提出-检索策略，生成多样化未来事件提议并整合多层级线索，产生鲁棒且准确的预测。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验验证了VISTA在长视频事件预测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: VISTA通过多层级语义挖掘有效解决了长视频事件预测中的细节提取和叙事分析难题，为长视频内容理解与预测提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Accurately predicting future events is fundamental to content understanding and decision-making across various domains. While prior research has primarily focused on text or short-video scenarios, long-video event prediction, characterized by vast multimodal context and more complex narratives, remains underexplored. Meanwhile, although recent Long-Video Language Models (LVLMs), built on Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), have shown promise in long-video question answering and summarization, they struggle to generalize to event prediction, as they can neither precisely extract event-related details nor perform fine-grained analysis of event development. To address this gap, we propose VISTA, a multi-level event semantics mining framework for long-video event prediction. Initially, VISTA applies a character-centric visual prompt to precisely extract event-related visual details, enhancing detail-level semantics; subsequently, it employs a knowledge-enhanced iterative retrieval strategy, guiding the LLM to progressively construct logically coherent event chains, thereby improving event-level narratives; ultimately, VISTA adopts a human-like propose-then-retrieve strategy to generate diverse future-oriented proposals and integrate multi-level clues, producing robust and accurate predictions. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of VISTA for long-video event prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 准确预测未来事件是跨领域内容理解和决策制定的基础。虽然先前研究主要聚焦于文本或短视频场景，但以海量多模态上下文和更复杂叙事为特征的长视频事件预测仍未被充分探索。与此同时，尽管基于大语言模型和视觉语言模型构建的近期长视频语言模型在长视频问答和摘要方面展现出潜力，但它们难以泛化到事件预测任务，因为它们既无法精确提取事件相关细节，也无法对事件发展进行细粒度分析。为弥补这一空白，我们提出VISTA，一种用于长视频事件预测的多层级事件语义挖掘框架。首先，VISTA应用角色中心视觉提示精确提取事件相关的视觉细节，增强细节级语义；其次，它采用知识增强的迭代检索策略，引导大语言模型逐步构建逻辑一致的事件链，从而提升事件级叙事；最后，VISTA采用类人的提出-检索策略生成多样化的未来事件提议并整合多层级线索，产生鲁棒且准确的预测。在真实世界数据集上的大量实验验证了VISTA在长视频事件预测中的有效性。

</details>


### [48] [Task-Focused Memorization for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31075)
*Tao Zou,Yichen He,Tian Qiu,Yuan Lin,Hang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TaskMem框架，将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆化策略，通过强化学习使多模态智能体动态聚焦与任务相关的内容，在三个流式基准上VQA准确率提升5.3%-7.0%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态智能体（如具身智能体）持续感知、推理和行动，面临无限的多模态观测流，核心挑战不在于记忆模块设计或准确度，而在于**决定记忆什么内容**。现有方法缺乏对任务相关性的动态关注，需要一种可学习的记忆化策略来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出TaskMem框架，采用两阶段训练范式：(1) 第一阶段在基本保真度要求下优化记忆质量，学习如何记忆；(2) 第二阶段在部署后，通过适配器微调基础多模态大语言模型，利用环境中的近期任务定义奖励模型，引导记忆化策略聚焦任务相关的内容。同时将VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo改造为流式基准测试。

Result: 基于Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B，TaskMem在VideoMME、EgoLife、EgoTempo三个流式基准上分别提升VQA准确率6.3%、7.0%和5.3%。

Conclusion: 将记忆生成建模为可学习的记忆化策略比依赖固定规则更有效，强化学习框架能使智能体动态适应任务需求，显著提升多模态智能体在持续学习场景中的记忆质量和任务性能。

Original Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for multimodal agents to build coherent experience, accumulate world knowledge, and achieve continual learning. However, constructing effective memory goes beyond memory module design and basic requirements such as accuracy and fidelity; the key challenge lies in determining what to memorize. Multimodal agents, such as embodied agents, continuously perceive, reason, and act in real or virtual environments, receiving an unbounded stream of multimodal observations. From this combinatorial explosion of information, an agent must selectively retain content that is relevant to its role in the environment and valuable for future tasks. To bridge this gap, we frame memory generation as a learnable memorization policy and introduce TaskMem (Task-focused Memorization Policy Learning), a reinforcement-learning-based framework that enables the policy to dynamically adjust its focus to the demands of real tasks encountered in the environment. TaskMem adopts a two-phase training paradigm: Phase One learns how to memorize by optimizing memory quality under fundamental fidelity requirements; Phase Two occurs after deployment, where the agent learns what to memorize by tuning an adapter on its base MLLM, using recent environment tasks to define a reward model that guides the memorization policy toward task-relevant content. To evaluate our approach, we reformulate VideoMME, EgoLife, and EgoTempo into streaming benchmarks that simulate a realistic setting in which an agent processes streaming observations and handles tasks arriving online. To isolate memory assessment, the questions must be answered using only the agent's memory, without access to raw video. Built on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B, TaskMem improves VQA accuracy by 6.3%, 7.0%, and 5.3% on these benchmarks, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 长时记忆对于多模态智能体构建连贯经验、积累世界知识以及实现持续学习至关重要。然而，构建有效的记忆不仅仅涉及记忆模块设计和准确性与保真度等基本要求；关键挑战在于决定记忆什么内容。多模态智能体（如具身智能体）在真实或虚拟环境中持续感知、推理和行动，接收无限的多模态观测流。面对这种信息组合爆炸，智能体必须选择性地保留与其在环境中的角色相关且对未来任务有价值的内容。为弥补这一差距，我们将记忆生成建模为一个可学习的记忆化策略，并提出TaskMem（任务聚焦的记忆化策略学习），这是一个基于强化学习的框架，使策略能够动态调整其焦点以适应环境中实际任务的需求。TaskMem采用两阶段训练范式：第一阶段通过优化基本保真度要求下的记忆质量来学习如何记忆；第二阶段在部署后发生，智能体通过在其基础多模态大语言模型上微调一个适配器来学习记忆什么，利用近期环境任务定义奖励模型，引导记忆化策略朝向任务相关的内容。为评估我们的方法，我们将VideoMME、EgoLife和EgoTempo改造为流式基准测试，模拟智能体处理流式观测并处理在线到达任务的现实场景。为隔离记忆评估，问题必须仅使用智能体的记忆来回答，而不允许访问原始视频。基于Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B，TaskMem在这些基准测试上分别提升了6.3%、7.0%和5.3%的VQA准确率。

</details>


### [49] [Cross-Modal Clinical Knowledge Integration for Mammography Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31093)
*Jiayi Zhu,Fuxiang Huang,Yu Xie,Xi Wang,Zhixuan Chen,Yuan Guo,Qingcong Kong,Zhenhui Li,Qiong Luo,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MammoRG是一个模拟放射科医生临床报告工作流的乳腺X线摄影报告生成框架，通过两阶段训练（先验知识融合+术语感知微调）和专用报告解析工具MammoRGTool，在多个数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动乳腺X线摄影报告生成方法主要关注直接的视觉到文本映射，忽略了放射科医生在实际临床实践中遵循的结构化临床推理过程（如BI-RADS指南），导致生成的报告缺乏临床一致性和诊断准确性。

Method: MammoRG采用两阶段训练框架：(1) 第一阶段通过分类监督从患者四视图乳腺X线图像中学习整合临床相关先验知识；(2) 第二阶段引入术语感知监督微调策略，将乳腺X线摄影特有临床术语建模为原子语义单元，生成具有更好临床一致性的高质量报告。同时开发了MammoRGTool专用报告解析工具，从自由文本报告中提取结构化临床信息进行临床效能评估。

Result: 在内部、外部1、外部2和VinDr-Mammo四个数据集上，MammoRG在诊断相关的BI-RADS F1指标上分别超越第二名模型2.73%、2.04%、1.90%和3.27%，在多项临床效能指标上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式模拟放射科医生的临床报告工作流并遵循BI-RADS指南，MammoRG有效提升了乳腺X线摄影报告生成的临床一致性和诊断准确性，为自动化医学报告生成提供了更具临床价值的技术路径。

Original Abstract: Breast cancer is a major global health concern, and mammography screening plays a central role in early detection. The large volume of screening examinations creates a substantial workload for radiologists, making accurate and consistent report generation a critical clinical challenge. Existing automated mammography report generation methods primarily focus on direct visual-to-text mapping, while overlooking the structured clinical reasoning process followed by radiologists in real-world practice. To address this limitation, we propose MammoRG, a mammography report generation framework that explicitly simulates the clinical reporting workflow by following the BI-RADS guideline and incorporating prior clinical knowledge to produce diagnostic reports. Specifically, MammoRG adopts a two-stage training framework. In the first stage, the model learns to integrate clinically relevant prior knowledge from a patient's four-view mammograms through classification-based supervision. In the second stage, a terminology-aware supervised fine-tuning strategy is introduced to model mammography-specific clinical terms as atomic semantic units, enabling the generation of high-quality reports with improved clinical consistency. To facilitate clinical efficacy evaluation of generated reports, we further develop MammoRGTool, a dedicated mammography report parsing tool that extracts structured clinical information from free-text reports. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MammoRG consistently outperforms existing methods across multiple clinical efficacy metrics, particularly in diagnosis-related BI-RADS F1, where it surpasses the second-best model by 2.73%, 2.04%, 1.90%, and 3.27% on the internal, external 1, external 2, and VinDr-Mammo datasets, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 乳腺癌是一个重大的全球健康问题，乳腺X线摄影筛查在早期检测中发挥着核心作用。大量的筛查检查给放射科医生带来了沉重的工作负担，使得准确且一致的报告生成成为一项关键的临床挑战。现有的自动化乳腺X线摄影报告生成方法主要关注直接的视觉到文本映射，而忽略了放射科医生在实际临床实践中遵循的结构化临床推理过程。为解决这一局限性，我们提出了MammoRG——一个乳腺X线摄影报告生成框架，该框架通过遵循BI-RADS指南并融合先验临床知识来显式模拟临床报告工作流，从而生成诊断报告。具体而言，MammoRG采用两阶段训练框架。在第一阶段，模型通过基于分类的监督学习，从患者的四视图乳腺X线图像中学习整合临床相关的先验知识。在第二阶段，引入术语感知监督微调策略，将乳腺X线摄影特有的临床术语建模为原子语义单元，从而生成具有更好临床一致性的高质量报告。为便于对生成报告进行临床效能评估，我们进一步开发了MammoRGTool，这是一个专用的乳腺X线摄影报告解析工具，可从自由文本报告中提取结构化的临床信息。大量实验表明，MammoRG在多项临床效能指标上持续优于现有方法，特别是在诊断相关的BI-RADS F1指标上，在内部、外部1、外部2和VinDr-Mammo数据集上分别超越第二名模型2.73%、2.04%、1.90%和3.27%。

</details>


### [50] [Redefining Instance Matching: A Unified Framework for Part-Aware Matching in Panoptic Segmentation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31094)
*Erik Großkopf,Soumya Snigdha Kundu,Hendrik Möller,Nicolas Münster,Mehdi Astaraki,Paula Tamara Buzduga,Kerstin Ritter,Benedikt Wiestler,Jan Kirschke,Jonathan Shapey,Tom Vercauteren,Florian Kofler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地将全景质量（PQ）度量中的片段匹配问题重新定义为带约束的二分图分配问题，阐明了四种匹配策略（一对一、多对一、一对多、多对多）及其适用场景，并基于顶点计数重新定义了TP、FN、FP，同时扩展到了部分感知的全景分割评估，提供了一个统一的开源工具包。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始PQ度量依赖于IoU阈值超过0.5时的一对一匹配，但当IoU阈值低于0.5时存在多种匹配策略且该问题空间未被充分探索。本文旨在系统性地阐明这一匹配空间，并为模糊情况下的全景分割评估提供更严谨的框架。

Method: 将片段匹配重新形式化为带约束的二分图分配问题，通过独立限制预测侧和真值侧的度数，推导出四种匹配策略；提出基于顶点（而非匹配边）的TP、FN、FP计数方法；将该框架自然扩展至部分感知的全景分割评估，并在生物医学数据上进行了探索。

Result: 通过可配置的案例研究，报告了不同IoU阈值与匹配策略组合在实际场景中的表现；框架可自然扩展至部分感知全景分割；发布了基于Panoptica的统一开源工具包，支持Voronoi区域分析、部分感知评估和阈值下面积（AUTC）计算。

Conclusion: PQ框架下前三类匹配策略（一对一、多对一、一对多）均有明确定义，而多对多策略超出其范围；这些策略在实例碎片化、相邻目标难以区分或标注噪声大时尤为相关；所提出的顶点计数方法和部分感知扩展增强了全景分割评估的灵活性与适用性。

Original Abstract: The Panoptic Quality (PQ) metric is the standard for jointly evaluating instance and semantic segmentation. However, its original definition relies on a One-to-One matching between predicted and ground truth segments, which is only straightforward when the IoU threshold exceeds 0.5. Below 0.5, multiple matching strategies emerge in a poorly explored problem space. We systematically elucidate this space by recasting segment matching as a constrained bipartite assignment problem. Independently bounding the prediction- and ground-truth-side degrees yields four matching strategies: One-to-One, Many-to-One, One-to-Many, and Many-to-Many. We show that the first three are well-defined within the PQ framework, while Many-to-Many falls outside it. These strategies become relevant when instances are fragmented, adjacent objects are difficult to delineate, or annotations are noisy. Central to our framework is a vertex-based accounting of TP, FN, and FP, anchored to ground truth and predicted segments rather than to matching edges. We further show that the framework extends naturally to part-aware panoptic segmentation, and we explore part-aware evaluation on biomedical data. Across configurable case studies we report how different combinations of thresholds and matching strategies behave in practice. We release a unified open-source package built on Panoptica. It exposes Voronoi-based region-wise analysis, part-aware evaluation, and Area Under Threshold Curve computations as configurable options.

中文摘要翻译: 全景质量（Panoptic Quality, PQ）指标是联合评估实例分割和语义分割的标准。然而，其原始定义依赖于预测片段与真值片段之间的一对一匹配，这种匹配仅在IoU阈值超过0.5时是直接明确的。当阈值低于0.5时，在一个鲜有探索的问题空间中会涌现出多种匹配策略。我们通过将片段匹配重新构建为一个带约束的二分图分配问题，系统地阐明了这一空间。独立限制预测侧和真值侧的度数产生了四种匹配策略：一对一（One-to-One）、多对一（Many-to-One）、一对多（One-to-Many）和多对多（Many-to-Many）。我们表明，前三类在PQ框架内有明确定义，而多对多则超出了该框架的范围。当实例碎片化、相邻目标难以划分或标注存在噪声时，这些策略变得尤为重要。我们框架的核心是一种基于顶点的TP、FN和FP计数方法，该方法锚定于真值和预测片段本身，而非匹配边。我们进一步展示了该框架可以自然地扩展到部分感知的全景分割（part-aware panoptic segmentation），并在生物医学数据上探索了部分感知评估。通过可配置的案例研究，我们报告了不同阈值与匹配策略组合在实际中的表现。我们发布了一个基于Panoptica的统一开源工具包，公开了基于Voronoi的区域分析、部分感知评估以及阈值下面积（Area Under Threshold Curve）计算等可配置选项。

</details>


### [51] [iVGR: Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning for MLLMs with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31096)
*Chang-Bin Zhang,Yujie Zhong,Qiang Zhang,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出iVGR框架，通过强化学习将视觉定位能力内化到文本推理链中，避免推理时显式视觉定位带来的性能下降，在细粒度多模态任务上超越现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 发现强制在推理阶段使用显式目标框的视觉定位CoT反而会降低性能，假设视觉定位能力可以被内化到文本CoT中，显式定位会干扰模型的答案预测主目标。

Method: 提出iVGR框架，采用双流训练策略：一个文本流与一个高质量的视觉定位流通过一致性奖励进行对齐，使模型在推理时无需显式定位即可准确进行空间推理。

Result: 在细粒度基准上显著优于现有基线，同时保持对工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性支持。

Conclusion: 视觉定位能力可以被内化到文本推理过程中，无需在推理阶段强制显式定位，iVGR框架有效解决了这一问题。

Original Abstract: While visually grounded Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has emerged as a promising paradigm to enhance fine-grained perception in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), its efficacy during the inference phase remains underexplored. In this work, we empirically find that mandating explicit object boxes in visually grounded CoT during inference often degrades performance compared to standard textual CoT, which reasons without explicit visual grounding. We hypothesize that the visual localization capability can be internalized into the textual CoT and that the mandatory explicit grounding introduces unnecessary interference with the model's primary objective of answer prediction. To address this problem, we propose Internalizing Visually Grounded Reasoning (\textbf{iVGR}), a novel reinforcement learning framework that transfers localization capabilities into the textual reasoning process. We employ a dual-stream training strategy, where a textual stream is aligned with a high-quality visually grounded stream via a proposed consistency reward, enabling the model to localize accurately without explicit grounding during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines on fine-grained benchmarks, while maintaining the flexibility to support tool-assisted inference workflows.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然视觉引导的思维链（CoT）已成为增强多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）细粒度感知能力的一种有前景的范式，但其在推理阶段的有效性仍未得到充分探索。在本工作中，我们通过实证发现，在推理阶段强制要求视觉引导CoT中显式地生成目标框，其性能往往不如使用无需显式视觉定位的标准文本CoT。我们假设视觉定位能力可以被内化到文本CoT中，而强制性的显式定位会对模型答案预测这一主要目标引入不必要的干扰。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了内化视觉引导推理（iVGR），一种将定位能力迁移到文本推理过程中的新型强化学习框架。我们采用双流训练策略，通过提出的一致性奖励将文本流与高质量的视觉引导流对齐，使模型在推理时无需显式定位即可准确地进行空间定位。大量实验表明，我们的方法在细粒度基准上显著优于现有基线，同时保持了对工具辅助推理工作流的灵活性支持。

</details>


### [52] [Remembering by Reconstructing: Domain Incremental Learning With Test-Time Training on Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31108)
*Jonathan Swinnen,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的域增量学习方法，不避免而是主动利用灾难性遗忘，通过自监督MAE头在线测试时训练识别最匹配的LoRA适配器，使模型能在流式数据中动态"记住"当前域。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有域增量学习主要致力于避免灾难性遗忘，但难以应对高度相关且域渐变流式数据（如视频）。作者反其道而行之，允许遗忘发生，再通过在线测试时训练动态恢复域特定知识。

Method: 1）模型由主任务头和自监督MAE头组成；2）增量训练中学习域特定LoRA适配器，每个适配器专精于对应域，自然导致对其它域的遗忘；3）推理时，在MAE头上进行在线测试时训练，识别与当前输入最匹配的LoRA，从而"回忆"起该域。

Result: 在域增量动作识别和语义分割任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 允许遗忘并结合在线测试时训练的LoRA匹配机制，能有效处理流式数据中逐步发生的域偏移，尤其适用于视频等连续样本高度相关的场景。

Original Abstract: In this work we introduce a novel approach to domain incremental learning, adapting models over time to evolving, non-stationary data. In contrast to other works, we do not attempt to avoid catastrophic forgetting, but rather allow it and exploit it. Our model combines a main task head with a self-supervised masked autoencoder (MAE) head. We then learn domain-specific LoRA adapters during incremental training. Each adapter specializes to its domain, naturally inducing forgetting on other domains in both heads. At inference, we perform online test-time training on the self-supervised MAE head to identify which LoRAs best matches the current input, so the model can `remember' the domain again. Our scheme is especially well-suited to real-world streaming data, such as video, where consecutive samples are highly correlated and domain shifts are gradual. We demonstrate our method on domain-incremental action recognition and semantic segmentation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 在这项工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的域增量学习方法，使模型能够随时间适应演化的、非平稳的数据。与其它工作不同，我们并不试图避免灾难性遗忘，而是允许它发生并加以利用。我们的模型结合了一个主任务头和一个自监督掩码自编码器（MAE）头。在增量训练过程中，我们学习域特定的LoRA适配器。每个适配器专精于其对应域，自然地在两个头上引发对其他域的遗忘。在推理时，我们在自监督MAE头上进行在线测试时训练，以识别最匹配当前输入的LoRA，从而使模型能够"重新记住"该域。我们的方案特别适用于现实世界的流式数据，例如视频，其中连续样本高度相关且域偏移是逐步发生的。我们在域增量动作识别和语义分割任务上展示了该方法的效果。

</details>


### [53] [NTR: Neural Token Reconstruction for Scene Token Bottleneck in End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31116)
*Jiahui Li,Jiawei Sun,Zixiang Ren,Ming Liu,Jiamin Shi,Ruiteng Zhao,Zhiyang Liu,Liying Liu,Zuoguan Wang,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出神经令牌重建（NTR）框架，通过自蒸馏掩码潜在重建约束无感知端到端自动驾驶中的紧凑场景令牌瓶颈，在推理时不引入额外模块，在三个公开基准上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无感知端到端自动驾驶方法将密集图像块令牌压缩为紧凑场景令牌用于轨迹生成，但场景令牌仅受规划目标监督，对编码的视觉信息约束不足，导致视觉表示质量受限。

Method: 提出NTR框架：1) 引入自蒸馏掩码潜在重建目标，利用紧凑场景令牌作为重建记忆来重建被掩码的块级潜在特征，强迫重建梯度仅通过场景令牌瓶颈传播；2) 引入基于基础模型标注的语义先验作为弱语义接口，偏重建目标朝向驾驶相关结构；3) 推理时移除所有辅助重建组件，部署的规划器不变。

Result: 在Waymo E2E上达到8.0461 RFS，在NavSim1&2上达到94.1 PDMS / 90.9 EPDMS，均为SOTA。学习到的场景令牌具有更低的成对冗余和更高的有效秩。

Conclusion: 有效的瓶颈监督能够同时改善紧凑视觉表示学习和规划性能，NTR在无感知驾驶中实现了表示质量与规划能力的双提升。

Original Abstract: Recent perception-free end-to-end (E2E) autonomous driving methods bypass explicit perception outputs by compressing dense image patch tokens into compact scene tokens for downstream trajectory generation and scoring. While these scene tokens form a compact visual bottleneck for the planner, they receive supervision solely from the planning objective, providing limited constraints on the encoded visual information. To address this limitation, we introduce Neural Token Reconstruction (NTR), a representation learning framework to directly constrain the compact scene-token bottleneck in perception-free driving. NTR introduces a self-distillation masked latent reconstruction objective that reconstructs masked patch-level latent features using only compact scene tokens as reconstruction memory. This forces reconstruction gradients to pass exclusively through the scene-token bottleneck, encouraging scene tokens to preserve richer and less redundant visual representations for planning. We further introduce semantic priors derived from foundation-model annotations as a weak semantic interface biasing reconstruction targets toward driving-related structures without introducing explicit perception heads. All auxiliary reconstruction components are removed at inference time, leaving the deployed planner unchanged. NTR achieves state-of-the-art performance on three public autonomous driving benchmarks, including 8.0461 RFS on Waymo E2E and 94.1 PDMS / 90.9 EPDMS on NavSim1&2. The learned scene tokens exhibit lower pairwise redundancy and higher effective rank, indicating that effective bottleneck supervision improves both compact visual representation learning and planning performance.

中文摘要翻译: 近期无感知端到端自动驾驶方法通过将密集图像块令牌压缩为紧凑场景令牌用于下游轨迹生成和评分，从而绕过了显式的感知输出。虽然这些场景令牌为规划器形成了紧凑的视觉瓶颈，但它们仅接受规划目标的监督，对编码的视觉信息提供的约束有限。为解决这一局限性，我们提出神经令牌重建（NTR），一种直接约束无感知驾驶中紧凑场景令牌瓶颈的表示学习框架。NTR引入自蒸馏掩码潜在重建目标，仅使用紧凑场景令牌作为重建记忆来重建被掩码的块级潜在特征。这迫使重建梯度唯一地通过场景令牌瓶颈传播，鼓励场景令牌为规划保留更丰富且冗余度更低的视觉表示。我们进一步引入基于基础模型标注的语义先验，作为弱语义接口将重建目标偏向于驾驶相关结构，而无需引入显式感知头。所有辅助重建组件在推理时被移除，部署的规划器保持不变。NTR在三个公开自动驾驶基准上取得了最先进性能，包括Waymo E2E上的8.0461 RFS以及NavSim1&2上的94.1 PDMS / 90.9 EPDMS。学习到的场景令牌展现出更低的成对冗余和更高的有效秩，表明有效的瓶颈监督能够同时改善紧凑视觉表示学习和规划性能。

</details>


### [54] [QVGGT: Post-Training Quantized Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31124)
*Zhizhen Pan,Hesong Wang,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QVGGT量化框架，通过混合精度量化、令牌过滤与任务感知尺度搜索，在W4A16量化下实现近无损的3D感知性能，内存减少3~4.9倍，速度提升2.8倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VGGT模型参数规模达1.2B，严重限制了其在无人机、移动AR设备等资源受限平台上的部署，因此需要有效的模型压缩方法。

Method: 1) 基于逐块敏感性分析的选择性混合精度量化策略；2) 令牌过滤与相机信息补偿机制，去除激活校准中的离群令牌并恢复几何线索；3) 任务感知尺度搜索机制，通过多头监督和跨头几何一致性评估量化尺度。

Result: 在多个几何感知基准上，QVGGT实现了近无损的W4A16量化，保持了所有3D预测头的精度，同时内存减少3~4.9倍，实际硬件推理速度相比FP32提升最高2.8倍。

Conclusion: QVGGT使高保真3D感知在边缘设备上变得可行，推动了前馈式3D重建模型在真实受限环境中的实际部署。

Original Abstract: Estimating 3D attributes directly from images has advanced rapidly with the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), which predicts camera parameters, depth maps, and point clouds in a single forward pass. However, its 1.2B-parameter scale severely limits deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as UAVs and mobile AR devices. To address this limitation, we introduce QVGGT, a tailored quantization framework designed to compress VGGT. Our approach starts from the observation that transformer blocks within VGGT exhibit heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization. We thus analyze per-block quantization sensitivity and propose a selective mixed-precision strategy that allocates higher precision to the most fragile transformer blocks. To address the amplification of quantization error caused by high-variance camera and register tokens, we further introduce token filtering with camera information compensation, which removes these outliers from activation calibration and restores their geometric cues using a PCA-derived global compensation token. Finally, we develop a task-aware scale search mechanism that evaluates candidate quantization scales not only through layer reconstruction but also through multi-head supervision and cross-head geometric consistency among camera poses, depth maps, and point maps. Extensive experiments on multiple geometry perception benchmarks demonstrate that QVGGT achieves near-lossless W4A16 quantization, preserving the accuracy of all 3D prediction heads while delivering 3$\sim$4.9$\times$ memory reduction and up to 2.8$\times$ real hardware speedup over FP32. Our approach makes high-fidelity 3D perception feasible on edge devices, enabling practical deployment of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in real-world constrained environments.

中文摘要翻译: 直接从图像估计3D属性因视觉几何基础Transformer（VGGT）的提出而取得快速进展，该模型可在单次前向传播中预测相机参数、深度图和点云。然而，其12亿参数的规模严重限制了在无人机和移动AR设备等资源受限平台上的部署。为解决这一局限，我们提出QVGGT，一种专为压缩VGGT而设计的量化框架。我们的方法始于一个观察：VGGT中的Transformer块对量化表现出异质性敏感性。因此，我们分析了逐块的量化敏感性，并提出了一种选择性混合精度策略，为最脆弱的Transformer块分配更高的精度。为解决高方差相机令牌和注册令牌导致的量化误差放大问题，我们进一步引入了带有相机信息补偿的令牌过滤机制，从激活校准中移除这些离群值，并使用PCA导出的全局补偿令牌恢复其几何线索。最后，我们开发了一种任务感知的尺度搜索机制，不仅通过层重建，还通过多头监督以及相机位姿、深度图和点图之间的跨头几何一致性来评估候选量化尺度。在多个几何感知基准上的大量实验表明，QVGGT实现了近无损的W4A16量化，在保持所有3D预测头精度的同时，相比FP32实现了3~4.9倍的内存减少和高达2.8倍的实际硬件加速。我们的方法使高保真3D感知在边缘设备上成为可能，推动了前馈式3D重建模型在真实受限环境中的实际部署。

</details>


### [55] [FOCUS: Forcing In-Context Object Localization through Visual Support Constraints and Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31145)
*Mohammed Asad Karim,Vinay Kumar Verma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需类别监督的两阶段训练框架，通过优化上下文注意力和强化学习（GRPO）实现类别无关的实例级上下文定位（ICL），7B模型超越72B模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ICL方法依赖显式类别监督，导致类别偏见，使模型偏向语义先验而非视觉证据，且无法处理未命名或实例特定的对象，限制了在图像编辑、个性化搜索等场景中的应用。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：第一阶段显式优化支持集边界框与查询图像之间的上下文注意力，无需类别监督；第二阶段使用GRPO强化学习直接最小化定位误差，强化视觉对应关系。

Result: 7B参数模型在实例级定位上超越多达72B参数的模型，表明上下文感知的定位目标可以超越单纯扩大模型规模的收益；充分的消融实验验证了各模块的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过优化上下文注意力与强化学习相结合，可以在无类别监督下实现鲁棒的、类别无关的实例级上下文定位，且模型规模并非决定性能的唯一因素。

Original Abstract: In-context localization (ICL) seeks to localize a target object specified by a small set of support examples in a query image, operating on the fly without training or parameter updates. Despite rapid advances in vision-language models (VLMs), achieving category-agnostic and visually grounded ICL remains an open problem, even though it is essential for applications such as image editing, personalized visual search, and retrieval. Existing methods are fragile and rely on explicit category supervision, which not only limits applicability in realistic settings with unnamed or instance-specific objects but also introduces category bias that steers predictions toward semantic priors rather than visual evidence. We introduce a two-stage training framework that explicitly optimizes in-context attention between support bounding boxes and query images without category supervision. We further refine localization via reinforcement learning using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to directly minimize localization error. This formulation enforces visual correspondence over semantic priors, yielding robust instance-level localization. Empirically, a 7B-parameter model trained with our objectives outperforms models up to 72B parameters, demonstrating that context-aware localization objectives can surpass scaling alone. Comprehensive ablations validate the contribution of each component.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文定位（ICL）旨在根据查询图像中由少量支持示例指定的目标对象进行定位，无需训练或参数更新即可即时运行。尽管视觉-语言模型（VLM）取得了快速进展，实现类别无关且视觉 grounded 的 ICL 仍然是一个悬而未决的问题，尽管它对图像编辑、个性化视觉搜索和检索等应用至关重要。现有方法较为脆弱且依赖显式类别监督，这不仅限制了在包含未命名或实例特定对象的现实场景中的适用性，而且引入了类别偏见，使预测偏向语义先验而非视觉证据。我们提出了一个两阶段训练框架，该框架在无需类别监督的情况下显式优化支持集边界框与查询图像之间的上下文注意力。我们进一步通过使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习来细化定位，直接最小化定位误差。这种公式强化了视觉对应关系而非语义先验，从而产生鲁棒的实例级定位。实验表明，使用我们的目标训练的7B参数模型优于多达72B参数的模型，证明了上下文感知的定位目标可以超越单纯扩大模型规模的收益。全面的消融实验验证了每个组件的贡献。

</details>


### [56] [SpatialAct: Probing Spatial Reasoning-to-Action Capabilities of VLM Agents in 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31148)
*Tianhui Liu,Jie Feng,Zhiheng Zheng,Shengyuan Wang,Yiming Guo,Yanxin Xi,Hangyu Fan,Yong Li,Pan Hui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialAct 基准，用于评估 VLM 在 3D 场景中的动作条件空间推理能力，发现当前 VLM 存在推理到动作的鸿沟，在交互式多轮反馈中表现远逊于人类。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 VLM 在基于观察的空间感知与推理任务上表现良好，但它们能否构建连贯的空间理解、据此采取行动并通过多轮反馈优化行动尚不清楚，因此需要系统性基准来探测这一问题。

Method: 构建 SpatialAct 基准，包含最难的"多轮交互式细化"任务及其分解版本"单步错误检测与修正"，以及五项基础空间能力任务，用于诊断模型失败的根本原因。

Result: 当前 VLM 在孤立空间推理任务上表现良好，但在多轮交互中难以维持连贯空间信念并产生可靠动作，性能显著低于人类，存在明显的推理到动作鸿沟。

Conclusion: 即使抽象掉底层控制，当前 VLM 体在动作引发的环境变化下仍缺乏稳健的空间状态追踪能力，无法像人类一样进行有效的交互式空间推理。

Original Abstract: Humans can effortlessly perceive spatial layouts, form cognitive representations, reason about spatial relations, and translate such reasoning into actions in everyday 3D environments. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising performance on observation-conditioned spatial perception and reasoning tasks, it remains unclear whether they can build coherent spatial understanding, act upon it, and refine their actions through multi-turn feedback. To study this problem, we introduce \textbf{SpatialAct}, a simulator-grounded benchmark for probing \textit{action-conditioned spatial reasoning} in 3D scenes. Starting from the most challenging setting, Multi-turn Interactive Refinement, we further design its decomposed counterpart, Single-step Error Detection and Fix, together with five fundamental spatial ability tasks to diagnose the underlying causes of model failures. Experiments reveal a clear reasoning-to-action gap: current VLMs can perform well on isolated spatial reasoning tasks, but struggle to maintain coherent spatial beliefs and produce reliable actions during multi-turn feedback, substantially underperforming humans. These results suggest that current VLM agents still lack robust spatial state tracking under action-induced environment changes, even when low-level control is abstracted away.

中文摘要翻译: 人类可以在日常 3D 环境中轻松感知空间布局、形成认知表征、推理空间关系，并将这种推理转化为行动。尽管最近的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在基于观察的空间感知和推理任务上表现出了令人鼓舞的性能，但它们是否能够构建连贯的空间理解、据此采取行动并通过多轮反馈来优化行动仍不清楚。为了研究这一问题，我们提出了 \textbf{SpatialAct}，一个基于模拟器的基准，用于探测 3D 场景中的\textit{动作条件空间推理}。从最具挑战性的设置——多轮交互式细化出发，我们进一步设计了其分解版本——单步错误检测与修正，以及五项基础空间能力任务，以诊断模型失败的潜在原因。实验揭示了一个清晰的推理到动作的鸿沟：当前的 VLM 在孤立的空间推理任务上可以表现良好，但在多轮反馈过程中难以维持连贯的空间信念并产生可靠的动作，其性能显著低于人类。这些结果表明，即使抽象掉底层控制，当前的 VLM 体在动作引发的环境变化下仍然缺乏稳健的空间状态追踪能力。

</details>


### [57] [Light Interaction: Training-Free Inference Acceleration for Interactive Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31158)
*Jiacheng Lu,Haoyi Zhu,Sipei Yi,Enze Xie,Yu Li,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Light Interaction 提出了一种无需重新训练即可加速交互式视频世界模型推理的框架，通过自适应上下文管理、去噪缓存加速以及软硬件协同设计的 3D 块稀疏注意力，在保持视觉质量的同时实现最高 2.59 倍加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的交互式视频世界模型在扩展到长交互轨迹时面临严重的计算和内存瓶颈：上下文记忆不断增长、注意力计算复杂度呈二次增长、以及需要重复的去噪步骤，导致推理成本过高，难以实用化。

Method: 基于交互过程天然支持轨迹依赖的自适应计算这一洞察，Light Interaction 结合了三种关键技术：(1) 自适应上下文管理——探索新区域时丢弃已检索的空间记忆，根据局部潜在动态调整时间上下文；(2) 去噪缓存加速——当相机重新访问熟悉区域时复用早期模型输出；(3) 软硬件协同设计的 3D 块稀疏注意力及融合 Triton 内核。

Result: 在 HY-WorldPlay 和 Matrix-Game-3.0 两个基准上，Light Interaction 实现了最高 2.59 倍的推理加速，同时保持了具有竞争力的视觉质量，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: 本文证明，通过利用交互过程中固有的自适应计算特性，可以在不牺牲视觉质量且无需重新训练的前提下，显著加速交互式视频世界模型的推理过程，为实时交互应用提供了一种实用的加速方案。

Original Abstract: Interactive video world models generate video chunk by chunk in response to user-controlled camera movements, enabling applications such as real-time game simulation, virtual scene navigation, and embodied AI training. However, scaling to long interactive trajectories is prohibitively expensive due to growing context memory, quadratic attention complexity, and repeated denoising steps. We present Light Interaction, a training-free inference acceleration framework for interactive video world models. Our key insight is that interaction naturally enables trajectory-dependent adaptive computation: retrieved spatial memory can be discarded during novel exploration, temporal context can be adjusted according to local latent dynamics, and early-step model outputs can be reused when the camera revisits familiar regions. Based on this insight, Light Interaction combines adaptive context management, denoising cache acceleration, and hardware-software co-designed 3D block sparse attention with fused Triton kernels. Evaluated on HY-WorldPlay and Matrix-Game-3.0, Light Interaction achieves up to 2.59x speedup without model retraining while maintaining competitive visual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 交互式视频世界模型通过响应用户控制的相机移动，逐块生成视频，从而实现实时游戏模拟、虚拟场景导航和具身 AI 训练等应用。然而，由于上下文记忆不断增长、注意力计算呈二次复杂度以及重复的去噪步骤，将模型扩展到长交互轨迹的成本极其高昂。本文提出 Light Interaction，一种面向交互式视频世界模型的免训练推理加速框架。我们的关键洞察在于，交互过程天然支持轨迹依赖的自适应计算：在新区域探索时可丢弃已检索的空间记忆，时间上下文可根据局部潜在动态进行调整，当相机重新访问熟悉区域时可复用早期模型输出。基于这一洞察，Light Interaction 结合了自适应上下文管理、去噪缓存加速以及软硬件协同设计的 3D 块稀疏注意力（配以融合 Triton 内核）。在 HY-WorldPlay 和 Matrix-Game-3.0 上的评估表明，Light Interaction 在不重新训练模型的情况下实现了最高 2.59 倍的加速比，同时保持了具有竞争力的视觉质量。

</details>


### [58] [Guidance for Low-Level Perceptual Editing in Unconditional Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31162)
*Shreyansh Modi,Akshat Tomar,Aarush Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理时机制，通过在无条件扩散模型中提取退化概念向量并组合瓶颈修补与无分类器引导，实现对图像的低层级美学和感知质量提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无条件扩散模型虽然具备强大的生成先验，但如何引导其产生美学上更优的输出仍未被充分探索。现有的h-space修补方法无法有效实现全局、低层级的感知质量优化。

Method: 提出一种广义的无训练图像编辑框架：在推理阶段，通过提取退化概念向量，结合瓶颈修补（bottleneck patching）与无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance），将采样过程从退化流形中引导开，从而生成质量一致的改进图像。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够在不进行任何模型重训练的情况下，持续生成质量改善的图像，克服了现有h-space修补方法在全局低层级变换上的系统性失败。

Conclusion: 本文证明了一种无需训练的推理时机制可有效引导无条件扩散模型产生美学增强的输出，为训练自由的扩散模型图像编辑提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Unconditional diffusion models offer powerful generative priors, yet steering them toward aesthetically enhanced outputs remains largely unexplored. We show that h-space patching, the dominant paradigm for training-free diffusion editing, systematically fails for global, low-level transformations required for aesthetic and perceptual refinement. We introduce a novel, generalized framework for image-editing in unconditional diffusion models without explicit training. This inference-time mechanism operates on low-level features by extracting degradation concept vectors and combining bottleneck patching with classifier-free guidance to guide sampling away from the degraded manifold, producing consistently improved images without any model retraining.

中文摘要翻译: 无条件扩散模型提供了强大的生成先验，但如何引导其生成美学增强的输出仍是一个未被充分探索的问题。我们发现，作为无训练扩散编辑主导范式的h-space修补方法，在面对美感与感知质量优化所需的全局低层级变换时存在系统性失败。本文提出了一种新颖的、广义的无条件扩散模型图像编辑框架，无需显式训练。该推理时机制通过提取退化概念向量作用于低层级特征，并组合瓶颈修补与无分类器引导，将采样过程从退化流形中引导开，从而在不进行任何模型重训练的情况下持续生成质量改进的图像。

</details>


### [59] [Detect in Any Scene: An Agentic Framework for Object Detection with Experience-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31174)
*Wenlun Zhang,Jun Yin,Kentaro Yoshioka*
