We present C4G, a feed-forward 4D reconstruction framework built upon a compact set of timestamp-conditioned learnable Gaussian query tokens. Each token aggregates corresponding features across the full temporal context and decodes a 3D Gaussian whose position is modulated by the target timestamp, enabling globally coherent motion modeling without per-scene optimization. To capture fine-grained details, we further introduce a video diffusion model-based rendering enhancement module. Since our framework effectively aggregates features into Gaussians, we extend this capability to feature lifting, producing a 4D feature field that supports point tracking and dynamic scene understanding. C4G achieves strong novel-view synthesis performance using significantly fewer Gaussians and without requiring camera poses, while exhibiting stronger motion modeling and robustness to large temporal gaps.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频进行动态场景重建仍然是计算机视觉中的一项基本挑战。现有的前馈方法逐帧逐像素地预测3D高斯，但存在高斯冗余和视角依赖偏差的问题，阻碍了场景运动的有效学习。我们提出C4G，一个前馈式4D重建框架，其核心是一组紧凑的、带有时间戳条件的可学习高斯查询令牌。每个令牌在全时间上下文范围内聚合对应特征，并解码出一个由目标时间戳调制位置的3D高斯，从而无需逐场景优化即可实现全局一致的运动建模。为捕捉细粒度细节，我们进一步引入基于视频扩散模型的渲染增强模块。由于我们的框架能有效将特征聚合到高斯中，我们将该能力扩展到特征提升，生成支持点跟踪和动态场景理解的4D特征场。C4G在显著减少高斯数量且无需相机位姿的情况下，实现了强劲的新视角合成性能，同时表现出更强的运动建模能力和对大时间间隙的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [91] [KLIP: localized distribution shift detection via KL-divergence with diffusion priors in Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31596)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Jihoon Hong,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于KL散度的OOD检测指标，无需校准数据或分布先验知识，可在逆问题中检测全局及局部图像分布偏移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型的OOD检测方法依赖偏移分布的先验知识，难以检测细微或局部的分布偏移，且仅适用于全图像而非逆问题中的间接测量值。

Method: 基于扩散先验与后验分布之间的Kullback-Leibler散度构建OOD检测指标，该指标无需校准数据或偏移分布知识，并能定位图像中的OOD区域。

Result: 该方法可检测语义上有意义的细微分布偏移（如健康肝脏CT与肿瘤肝脏CT之间的偏移），并能泛化至不同类型的扩散模型、数据集和逆问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的KL散度指标是一种无需先验知识、可检测全局与局部OOD的有效方法，在多种扩散模型和逆问题场景中具有良好泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Diffusion models have shown promising performance as data-driven priors for computational imaging, as well as some capacity to detect out-of-distribution (OOD) images. However, existing approaches to OOD detection often require some knowledge of the shifted distribution, fail to detect subtle or localized distribution shifts, and operate on full images, rather than the indirect measurements available in inverse problems. We propose an OOD detection metric based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the diffusion prior and the posterior distribution, that (i) does not require any calibration data or knowledge of the shifted distribution, and (ii) can detect whole images as OOD as well as localize OOD patches within an image. Experimentally, we show that this metric can detect subtle yet semantically meaningful distribution shifts, such as the shift from healthy liver CT scans to those with tumors, and generalizes across different types of diffusion models, datasets, and inverse problems. Our code can be found at https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散模型在作为计算成像的数据驱动先验方面展现出了良好的性能，同时也具备一定的检测分布外（OOD）图像的能力。然而，现有的OOD检测方法通常需要偏移分布的一些先验知识，无法检测细微或局部的分布偏移，并且仅针对全图像操作，而非逆问题中的间接测量值。我们提出了一种基于扩散先验与后验分布之间Kullback-Leibler散度的OOD检测指标，该指标（i）不需要任何校准数据或偏移分布的先验知识，并且（ii）既能检测整张图像是否为OOD，也能定位图像中OOD的局部区域。实验证明，该指标能够检测细微但具有语义意义的分布偏移，例如从健康肝脏CT扫描到含有肿瘤的肝脏CT扫描的偏移，并且能够泛化到不同类型的扩散模型、数据集和逆问题中。我们的代码可在https://github.com/voilalab/KLIP获取。

</details>


### [92] [Linear Scaling Video VLMs for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31598)
*Cristobal Eyzaguirre,Jiajun Wu,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateKV 是一种推理时方法，通过固定容量的基于重要性的循环状态携带跨帧上下文，将长视频 VLM 的预填充复杂度从二次方降至线性，在不微调或改动架构的情况下，逼近全自注意力精度并优于滑动窗口等方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频VLM的编码器依赖时空自注意力，计算和延迟随帧数二次增长；现有效率方法（如激进丢帧/丢token、粗粒度注意力近似）在提升可扩展性的同时损失了精度。

Method: StateKV 是一种推理时方法，核心是构建一个固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态来跨帧传递上下文信息（线性时间视频预填充），同时保留一个完整的逐帧缓存用于解码阶段。

Result: 在三个长视频基准、七种模型（涵盖三个家族和多个规模）上，StateKV 精度接近全自注意力，一致优于滑窗/基于近因的流式近似方法，并在固定计算预算下通过运行更大模型实现更强的精度。

Conclusion: StateKV 提供了一种无需微调或架构改变的实用方案，有效推动了可扩展的长视频理解。

Original Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in long-horizon and streaming settings, yet most video encoders still rely on spatiotemporal self-attention, causing compute and latency to grow quadratically with the number of frames. Existing efficiency methods improve scalability but often lose accuracy relative to full self-attention, for example through aggressive frame/token dropping or coarse attention approximations. We introduce StateKV, an inference-time method that adapts pretrained long-video VLMs to linear-time video prefill by carrying cross-frame context in a fixed-capacity, importance-based recurrent state, paired with a second full per-frame cache used for decoding. Across three long-video benchmarks and seven models spanning three families and multiple scales, StateKV remains close to full self-attention and consistently outperforms dominant sliding-window / recency-based streaming approximations, without fine-tuning or architectural changes. StateKV also reduces video-prefill cost measured FLOPs, enabling stronger accuracy at a fixed compute budget by running larger models. These results suggest a practical step toward scalable long-video understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 视频视觉语言模型（VLM）越来越多地用于长周期和流式场景，然而大多数视频编码器仍然依赖时空自注意力机制，导致计算量和延迟随帧数呈二次增长。现有的效率方法改善了可扩展性，但相对于全自注意力往往损失了精度，例如通过激进的帧/令牌丢弃或粗略的注意力近似。我们提出 StateKV，一种推理时方法，通过在一个固定容量、基于重要性的循环状态中携带跨帧上下文，并配合第二个用于解码的完整逐帧缓存，使预训练的长视频 VLM 实现线性时间的视频预填充。在三个长视频基准测试和七个模型（涵盖三个家族及多个规模）上，StateKV 的精度接近全自注意力，并且一致优于主流的滑窗/基于近因的流式近似方法，无需微调或架构改变。StateKV 还降低了以 FLOPs 衡量的视频预填充成本，从而在固定计算预算下可以通过运行更大模型来获得更强的精度。这些结果表明，StateKV 是迈向可扩展长视频理解的一个实用步骤。

</details>


### [93] [Lumos-Nexus: Efficient Frequency Bridging with Homogeneous Latent Space for Video Unified Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31603)
*Jiazheng Xing,Hangjie Yuan,Lingling Cai,Xinyu Liu,Yujie Wei,Fei Du,Hai Ci,Tao Feng,Jiasheng Tang,Weihua Chen,Fan Wang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lumos-Nexus提出了一种训练高效的统一视频生成框架，通过两阶段设计（轻量生成器对齐训练 + UPFB渐进式频率桥接推理）在保持推理能力的同时显著提升视频视觉质量，并引入了新的推理驱动视频生成基准VR-Bench。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令引导视频合成中表现良好，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中的计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。因此需要一种训练高效的方案来兼顾推理驱动生成能力和高视觉保真度。

Method: Lumos-Nexus采用两阶段设计：(1)训练阶段，仅将轻量级生成器与理解模块对齐，学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；(2)推理阶段，引入统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜在空间中逐步将生成过程交接给高容量预训练生成器，实现从粗到细的细化，在不牺牲推理质量的前提下生成高保真视频。

Result: 在VBench上，Lumos-Nexus在视觉真实感和时间一致性方面取得了显著提升；在VR-Bench上展现出强大的基于推理的生成性能。

Conclusion: Lumos-Nexus通过训练高效的统一视频生成框架，有效平衡了推理驱动生成能力与高视觉保真度，实验证明了其在视觉质量、时间一致性和推理生成性能上的优势。

Original Abstract: Connector-based video unified models have demonstrated strong capability in instruction-grounded video synthesis, but integrating a large high-fidelity generator into the unified training loop is computationally prohibitive, limiting achievable visual quality. We therefore propose Lumos-Nexus, a training-efficient unified video generation framework that facilitates the development of strong reasoning-driven generation capabilities while significantly enhancing visual fidelity. Lumos-Nexus adopts a two-stage design: 1) During training, only a lightweight generator is aligned with the understanding block to learn to take in reasoning-driven semantic control. 2) During inference, we introduce Unified Progressive Frequency Bridging (UPFB) to progressively hand off generation to a high-capacity pretrained generator in the shared latent space, enabling coarse-to-fine refinement and producing high-fidelity videos without compromising reasoning quality. To fill the gap in reasoning-driven video generation benchmarks, we introduce VR-Bench, which assesses a model's capability to translate inferred intent into coherent and semantically aligned video content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Lumos-Nexus achieves substantial gains in visual realism and temporal coherence on VBench, while exhibiting strong reasoning-based generative performance on VR-Bench. Code and models are available at https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/.

中文摘要翻译: 基于连接器的视频统一模型在指令驱动的视频合成中展现出强大能力，但将大型高保真生成器集成到统一训练循环中的计算成本过高，限制了可实现的视觉质量。为此，我们提出Lumos-Nexus，一个训练高效的统一视频生成框架，能够在显著提升视觉保真度的同时，发展出强大的推理驱动生成能力。Lumos-Nexus采用两阶段设计：1）训练阶段，仅将轻量级生成器与理解模块对齐，使其学习接收推理驱动的语义控制；2）推理阶段，我们引入统一渐进式频率桥接（UPFB），在共享潜在空间中逐步将生成过程交接给高容量预训练生成器，实现从粗到细的细化，在不牺牲推理质量的前提下生成高保真视频。为填补推理驱动视频生成基准的空白，我们提出了VR-Bench，用于评估模型将推理意图转化为连贯且语义对齐的视频内容的能力。大量实验表明，Lumos-Nexus在VBench上实现了视觉真实感和时间一致性的显著提升，同时在VR-Bench上展现出强大的基于推理的生成性能。代码和模型已开源：https://jiazheng-xing.github.io/nexus-lumos-home/

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Hamiltonian-Inspired Attention Mechanism for Scalable RF Transmitter Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30364)
*Chitraksh Singh,Monisha Dhanraj,Akram Sheriff*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿Transformer，一种物理启发的注意力架构，通过保持注意力头中的范数守恒动力学和相位增量嵌入，在无线射频指纹识别中显著提升大规模发射机识别性能和分布泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在无线射频指纹识别中，当发射机群体规模增大时，在接收机和信道分布偏移下性能严重退化，亟需一种能在大规模发射机场景下保持鲁棒性的识别方法。

Method: 提出哈密顿Transformer，在每个注意力头中使用可学习的斜对称生成器和Störmer-Verlet蛙跳积分步骤强制执行范数守恒的价值动力学；同时在输入层引入相位增量嵌入以揭示振荡器动态。

Result: 在同日条件下达到99.12%准确率，150个发射机下达到61.64%准确率，在所有规模点上一致优于CNN和Transformer基线。消融实验表明价值更新中的范数守恒是缩放优势的主要归纳偏置，相位增量嵌入提供最大的单组件改进。

Conclusion: 将物理启发的结构先验嵌入注意力机制，是在原始无线信号上进行大规模发射机识别的有效方法。

Original Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting identifies wire-less transmitters using hardware-induced imperfections present in baseband I/Q signals. However, deep learning models often degrade under receiver and channel distribution shifts, particularly as transmitter populations grow. This work proposes the Hamiltonian Transformer, a physics-informed attention architecture that enforces norm preserving value dynamics within each attention head using a learned skew-symmetric generator and a Störmer-Verlet leapfrog integration step. An additional phase-increment embedding exposes oscillator dynamics at the input layer. All experiments use non-equalized raw I/Q signals from the WiSig dataset under four protocols: same-day classification, cross-receiver generalisation, cross-day generalisation, and transmitter scaling up to 150 devices. The Hamiltonian Transformer achieves 99.12% accuracy under same-day conditions and 61.64% at 150 transmitters, consistently outperforming CNN and Transformer baselines across all scale points. A controlled ablation study identifies norm-preservation in the value update as the primary inductive bias driving the scaling advantage, with the phase increment embedding providing the single largest per-component improvement. These results indicate that embedding physics-informed structural priors into attention mechanisms is an effective approach to large-scale transmitter identification on raw wireless signals.

中文摘要翻译: 射频指纹识别利用基带I/Q信号中由硬件引入的缺陷来识别无线发射机。然而，深度学习模型在接收机和信道分布偏移下往往会退化，特别是在发射机群体规模增大时。本文提出哈密顿Transformer，一种物理启发的注意力架构，通过使用可学习的斜对称生成器和Störmer-Verlet蛙跳积分步骤，在每个注意力头内强制执行范数守恒的价值动力学。此外，相位增量嵌入在输入层揭示振荡器动态。所有实验均使用来自WiSig数据集的非均衡原始I/Q信号，在四种协议下进行：同日分类、跨接收机泛化、跨日泛化和发射机规模扩展（最多150个设备）。哈密顿Transformer在同日条件下达到99.12%的准确率，在150个发射机下达到61.64%的准确率，在所有规模点上一致优于CNN和Transformer基线。受控消融实验表明，价值更新中的范数守恒是驱动缩放优势的主要归纳偏置，而相位增量嵌入提供了最大的单组件改进。这些结果表明，将物理启发的结构先验嵌入注意力机制是在原始无线信号上进行大规模发射机识别的有效方法。

</details>


### [95] [On Spatial Degree-of-Freedom Analysis of Near-Field Multipath Channels for Ultra-massive MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30787)
*Zhiqiang Yuan,Hui Lou,Wei Fan,Jianhua Zhang,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了超大规模MIMO系统中近场多径信道的空间自由度，提出了基于格林函数表示的多径自由度度量方法，并通过仿真和28-30 GHz实测（720阵元）验证了多径传播可显著提升空间自由度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文献对近场视距传输信道的空间自由度已有较好刻画，但近场多径场景下的自由度尚未得到充分研究，而实际通信环境往往包含丰富的多径成分。

Method: 通过建模多径传播，基于信道格林函数表示分析特征值分布，推导出通用自由度度量；每个路径的自由度贡献由有效电气孔径与立体角的乘积决定，总自由度通过空间可分辨路径贡献的有效并集获得，并建立了特征值分布与多径功率之间的映射关系。

Result: 仿真和28-30 GHz频段、720阵元的近场信道实测结果表明，多径传播能够显著增加空间自由度，且所提度量能够准确预测实际近场信道的自由度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架为未来超大规模MIMO近场系统的自由度预测、容量分析和空间复用设计提供了实用工具。

Original Abstract: The transition to near-field (NF) communications in ultra-massive multiple-input multiple-output (UM-MIMO) systems fundamentally alters the spatial degrees of freedom (DoF) of wireless channels. While the NF DoF of line-of-sight (LoS) transmission channels is well-characterized in the literature, the DoF in NF multipath scenarios remains underexplored. This paper investigates the spatial DoF of NF UM-MIMO channels under practical multipath conditions. A generic DoF metric is derived by modeling multipath propagation and analyzing the resulting eigenvalue distribution based on the Green' s function representation of the channel. The DoF contribution of each path is determined by the product of the effective electrical aperture and the subtended solid angle, and the total DoF is obtained through the effective union of spatially resolvable path contributions. A mapping between the eigenvalue distribution and multipath powers is further established. Numerical simulations and real-world NF channel measurements at 28-30 GHz with 720 array elements are conducted for validation in both LoS multipath and non-LoS scenarios. The results show that multipath propagation can significantly increase the spatial DoF and that the proposed metric accurately predicts the DoF of practical NF channels. The proposed framework provides a practical tool for DoF prediction and supports capacity analysis and spatial multiplexing design in future NF UM-MIMO systems.

中文摘要翻译: 超大规模多输入多输出（UM-MIMO）系统中向近场（NF）通信的转变从根本上改变了无线信道的空间自由度（DoF）。虽然视距（LoS）传输信道的近场自由度在文献中已有充分刻画，但近场多径场景下的自由度仍未被充分探索。本文研究了实际多径条件下近场UM-MIMO信道的空间自由度。通过建模多径传播并基于信道的格林函数表示分析所得特征值分布，推导出一种通用的自由度度量。每条路径的自由度贡献由有效电气孔径与所张立体角的乘积决定，总自由度通过空间可分辨路径贡献的有效并集获得。进一步建立了特征值分布与多径功率之间的映射关系。为了验证，在28-30 GHz频段使用720个阵元进行了数值仿真和实际近场信道测量，涵盖视距多径和非视距场景。结果表明，多径传播能够显著增加空间自由度，且所提度量能够准确预测实际近场信道的自由度。本文提出的框架为未来近场UM-MIMO系统中的自由度预测、容量分析和空间复用设计提供了实用工具。

</details>


### [96] [Distribution-Aware Constellation Learning for Image Transmission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30988)
*Xufeng Zhang,Yinhuan Huang,Jingkai Ying,Huan Liu,Zhijin Qin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种分布感知的可学习调制框架，通过可学习星座模块和两阶段训练策略，使数字语义通信的调制符号更好地对齐语义特征分布，在性能上超越现有数字方案并接近先进模拟方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数字语义通信方法使用传统QAM星座，与语义编码器产生的语义特征的经验分布不匹配，限制了通信性能。

Method: 提出分布感知的可学习调制框架：1）以APSK几何先验初始化的可学习星座模块，将星座几何作为可训练码本；2）结合可微软分配与直通估计器的两阶段训练策略，实现端到端优化。

Result: 所提框架一致优于现有数字语义通信方案，性能可与先进模拟方法相媲美。

Conclusion: 通过可学习星座调制桥接语义特征表示与离散调制，有效解决了数字语义通信中星座与语义特征分布不匹配的问题。

Original Abstract: Semantic communication has demonstrated significant potential for image transmission, especially in bandwidth-limited and low signal-to-noise ratio scenarios. However, most existing methods are based on analog transmission, which poses challenges to the compatibility with existing digital communication systems. Existing digital semantic communication methods commonly adopt conventional quadrature amplitude modulation constellations, which mismatch the empirical distribution of semantic features produced by the semantic encoder. This paper proposes a distribution-aware learnable modulation for semantic communication framework, which bridges semantic feature representations and discrete modulation through constellation learning. Specifically, a learnable constellation module, initialized with an amplitude phase shift keying geometric prior, is developed to refine the constellation geometry as a trainable codebook, enabling modulation symbols to better align with the distribution of semantic features. To enable end-to-end optimization, a two-stage training strategy is introduced, combining differentiable soft assignment with straight-through estimator. Simulation results show that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing digital semantic communication schemes and achieves performance comparable to advanced analog methods.

中文摘要翻译: 语义通信在图像传输中展现出显著潜力，尤其是在带宽受限和低信噪比场景下。然而，现有方法大多基于模拟传输，这对与现有数字通信系统的兼容性提出了挑战。现有的数字语义通信方法普遍采用传统的正交幅度调制星座，这与语义编码器产生的语义特征的经验分布不匹配。本文提出了一种面向语义通信框架的分布感知可学习调制方法，通过星座学习桥接语义特征表示与离散调制。具体而言，开发了一个以幅相键控几何先验初始化的可学习星座模块，将星座几何优化为可训练码本，使调制符号能够更好地对齐语义特征的分布。为实现端到端优化，引入了一种两阶段训练策略，结合可微软分配与直通估计器。仿真结果表明，所提框架一致优于现有数字语义通信方案，并达到了与先进模拟方法相媲美的性能。

</details>


### [97] [Combining Cartesian and non-Cartesian acceleration techniques with SPARKLING for 1mm isotropic whole-brain MPRAGE in a minute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31017)
*Chaithya Giliyar Radhakrishna,Aurélien Massire,Blanche Bapst,Alexandre Vignaud,Philippe Ciuciu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出GoLF-SPARKLING混合加速框架，联合利用并行成像（PI）和压缩感知（CS）各自优势，在k-space中心区域采用GRAPPA-based PI、外围区域采用变密度CS，通过轨迹重排序避免调制伪影，实现了约一分钟内1mm各向同性分辨率的全脑高质量成像，且在自动脑分割任务中误差低于Wave-CAIPI和泊松盘采样等先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: T1加权MPRAGE是临床解剖成像的金标准，但采集时间长限制了其常规使用；现有的并行成像（PI）和压缩感知（CS）加速技术在高加速因子下会分别引入严重的噪声和模糊，且两者协同利用仍面临挑战。

Method: 扩展GoLF-SPARKLING框架，联合利用两种加速机制：在k-space中心区域使用基于GRAPPA的并行成像，在外围区域使用变密度压缩感知，各区域独立设置加速因子；同时根据反转恢复序列的平滑信号演变特性重新排序采集轨迹以避免调制伪影。

Result: 在1mm各向同性分辨率下，约一分钟内完成全脑成像，图像比单独使用任一种加速策略更锐利、噪声更少、更稳定；纯PI重建受高g因子噪声影响，纯CS重建出现明显模糊；在自动化脑分割任务中，平均体积误差低于Wave-CAIPI和泊松盘采样等最先进技术。

Conclusion: 通过联合利用PI和CS，GoLF-SPARKLING实现了高加速因子下的亚分钟级高质量解剖MRI，可提高临床通量并为难以扫描的患者提供更可靠的成像。

Original Abstract: Purpose: T1-weighted MPRAGE remains a cornerstone of clinical anatomical imaging, yet its long acquisition times constrain routine use. Established acceleration techniques, namely Parallel Imaging (PI) and Compressed Sensing (CS), tend to introduce substantial noise and blurring when pushed to high acceleration factors. Although they rely on fundamentally different redundancies, combining them synergistically remains an open challenge. Methods: The GoLF-SPARKLING framework was extended to jointly exploit two acceleration mechanisms: GRAPPA-based PI in the central k-space region and variable-density CS in the periphery, with independent acceleration factors in each zone. To preserve smooth signal evolution throughout the inversion-recovery period and avoid modulation artifacts, the acquisition trajectory was reordered accordingly. The resulting method was evaluated prospectively in vivo at 1mm isotropic resolution and benchmarked against Wave-CAIPI and Poisson-disk sampling. Results: The proposed hybrid approach produced sharper, less noisy, and more stable whole-brain images in approximately one minute than either acceleration strategy alone. Purely PI-based reconstructions were degraded by high g-factor noise, while purely CS-based reconstructions exhibited pronounced blurring. Furthermore, this method yielded lower average volumetric errors in downstream automated brain segmentation than state-of-the-art acceleration techniques, demonstrating its clinical utility. Conclusion: By jointly leveraging PI and CS, GoLF-SPARKLING achieves high acceleration factors that enable sub-minute, high-quality anatomical MRI. This translates into greater clinical throughput and more reliable imaging in patients who are challenging to scan.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：T1加权MPRAGE仍是临床解剖成像的基石，但其较长的采集时间限制了常规使用。现有的加速技术，即并行成像（PI）和压缩感知（CS），在推至高加速因子时往往会引入严重的噪声和模糊。尽管它们依赖于根本不同的冗余，但如何协同结合两者仍是一个开放性挑战。方法：对GoLF-SPARKLING框架进行扩展，以联合利用两种加速机制：在k-space中心区域使用基于GRAPPA的PI，在外围区域使用变密度CS，各区域具有独立的加速因子。为在反转恢复期间保持平滑的信号演变并避免调制伪影，相应地对采集轨迹进行了重排序。该方法在前瞻性体内实验中以1mm各向同性分辨率进行评估，并与Wave-CAIPI和泊松盘采样进行对比。结果：所提出的混合方法在大约一分钟内产生了比单独使用任一种加速策略更锐利、噪声更少且更稳定的全脑图像。纯PI重建受高g因子噪声影响，而纯CS重建则出现明显模糊。此外，该方法在下游自动脑分割任务中产生的平均体积误差低于最先进的加速技术，展示了其临床实用性。结论：通过联合利用PI和CS，GoLF-SPARKLING实现了高加速因子，从而实现了亚分钟级的高质量解剖MRI。这将转化为更高的临床通量以及对难以扫描的患者更可靠的成像。

</details>


### [98] [CRB-Optimal Arrays and Waveforms in Active Sensing: Role of Redundancy and Spatial Covariance of Array Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31059)
*Ids van der Werf,Robin Rajamäki,Geert Leus*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在线阵和面阵中使用正交与相干波形时最优阵列设计的性能极限，揭示了CRB最优几何结构固有的冗余性，推导了给定传感器总数下的最优收发分配方案，并建立了Diophantine方程与等CRB阵列几何之间的联系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有阵列设计缺乏对性能极限的系统刻画，特别是在正交与相干波形下CRB最优几何结构、收发传感器最优分配以及等CRB阵列的构造方法等方面尚不清晰。

Method: 利用Cramér-Rao下界（CRB）分析单目标参数估计性能，引入发射和接收阵列的空间方差及和联合阵列的空间方差概念，推导CRB与阵列几何之间的解析关系；通过Diophantine方程建立等CRB阵列几何的充要条件及构造方法。

Result: (1) 正交波形下单目标CRB取决于发射和接收阵列空间方差之和，等价于虚拟传感器多重性加权的和联合阵列空间方差；(2) CRB最优几何结构本质上是冗余的，揭示了MSE与可辨识性之间的基本权衡；(3) 给定传感器总数时，非均匀分配（偏向接收端）是最优的；(4) 给出了平面阵列下最优波形将功率导向目标方向的时空协方差条件；(5) 建立了Diophantine方程与等CRB阵列几何的联系及构造方法。

Conclusion: 本文为新兴主动感知MIMO系统中的最优阵列与波形设计提供了新的指导原则和深刻见解，挑战了传统非冗余阵列设计的惯例。

Original Abstract: This paper characterizes the performance limits of optimal array designs using orthogonal and coherent waveforms for both linear and planar arrays. For orthogonal waveforms, we show that the single-target Cramér-Rao Bound (CRB) depends on the sum of the so-called spatial variances of the transmit (Tx) and receive (Rx) arrays, or equivalently, the spatial variance of the sum co-array weighted by the multiplicities of the virtual sensors. This reveals that CRB-optimal geometries are inherently redundant, highlighting a fundamental trade-off between mean squared error (MSE) and identifiability in parameter estimation. Moreover, we derive optimal Tx-Rx sensor allocations given a total sensor budget and show that unequal allocation (favoring the Rx) is optimal even for nonredundant arrays, questioning conventional designs. We extend our results to planar arrays, providing a new general condition that the spatial covariances of the Tx and Rx arrays should satisfy for the optimal waveforms to direct power in the target direction. Additionally, we establish a connection between Diophantine equations and array geometries with equal CRB, along with a constructive method for designing such arrays. Our work provides new guidelines for and insights into optimal array and waveform design with relevance in emerging active sensing multiple-input multiple-output systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文刻画了在线阵和面阵中使用正交与相干波形时最优阵列设计的性能极限。对于正交波形，我们证明单目标Cramér-Rao下界（CRB）取决于发射（Tx）和接收（Rx）阵列的所谓空间方差之和，等价于由虚拟传感器多重性加权的和联合阵列的空间方差。这表明CRB最优几何结构本质上是冗余的，揭示了参数估计中均方误差（MSE）与可辨识性之间的基本权衡。此外，我们推导了给定传感器总数下的最优Tx-Rx传感器分配，并证明非均匀分配（偏向接收端）即使对非冗余阵列也是最优的，挑战了传统设计。我们将结果推广到面阵，给出了最优波形将功率导向目标方向时Tx和Rx阵列空间协方差应满足的新的一般条件。此外，我们建立了Diophantine方程与等CRB阵列几何之间的联系，并给出了一种构造此类阵列的方法。我们的工作为新兴主动感知多输入多输出系统中的最优阵列与波形设计提供了新的指导原则和深刻见解。

</details>


### [99] [Super-Resolution Experimental Validation and Polarimetric Extension of the Effective Roughness Diffuse Scattering Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31267)
*Giacomo Melloni,Jack Chuang,Samuel Berweger,Enrico M. Vitucci,Vittorio Degli-Esposti,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文通过结合超分辨多径分量提取与数字孪生辅助几何，首次实验验证了有效粗糙度（ER）模型，并针对10种常见建筑材料在28 GHz频段上测量了漫散射特性，同时提出了一种角度依赖的交叉极化鉴别（XPD）模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 长期以来，漫散射模型在实验验证上受限于无法在空间上分离实测信道中的镜面反射分量和漫散射贡献。

Method: 结合超分辨多径分量提取（解析镜面分量）与数字孪生辅助几何建模，从双站测量中空间分离镜面与漫散射贡献；基于有效粗糙度（ER）框架建模漫散射，并提出角度依赖的交叉极化鉴别（XPD）模型来描述去极化特性。

Result: 在10种常见建筑材料上，每种材料测量266个角度配置和全部极化组合（HH、HV、VH、VV），该方法复现了实测漫散射功率趋势，RMSE低至3 dB；在几乎所有材料-极化情况下，XPD预测优于基线常数XPD模型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了物理一致且实践可行的毫米波系统高保真信道建模方法。

Original Abstract: The experimental validation of diffuse scattering models has long been limited by the inability to spatially separate specular and diffuse contributions in measured channels. This paper overcomes this limitation by combining super-resolution multipath component (MPC) extraction, which resolves individual propagation paths including the specular component, with digital-twin-assisted geometry, enabling the spatial separation of specular and diffuse contributions from bistatic measurements at 28~GHz. Using this framework, we provide the first measurement-driven validation of the Effective Roughness (ER) model with independent characterization of diffuse scattering across ten common building materials, each measured over 266 angular configurations and all polarization combinations (HH, HV, VH, VV). Furthermore, we extend the ER framework by proposing a novel angle-dependent cross-polarization discrimination (XPD) model, capturing the geometry-dependent nature of depolarization that is neglected in existing approaches. The proposed method reproduces the measured diffuse power trends, achieving RMSE values as low as 3 dB across the tested materials, and improves XPD prediction over the baseline constant-XPD model for nearly all material-polarization cases. These results establish a physically consistent and practically viable approach for high-fidelity channel modeling in mmWave systems.

中文摘要翻译: 漫散射模型的实验验证长期以来受限于无法在空间上分离实测信道中的镜面反射和漫散射贡献。本文通过结合超分辨多径分量提取（可解析包括镜面分量在内的各传播路径）与数字孪生辅助几何，克服了这一限制，实现了28 GHz双站测量中镜面与漫散射贡献的空间分离。利用该框架，我们首次通过实验驱动的方式验证了有效粗糙度（ER）模型，对十种常见建筑材料的漫散射特性进行了独立表征，每种材料测量了266个角度配置及所有极化组合（HH、HV、VH、VV）。此外，我们扩展了ER框架，提出了一种新颖的角度依赖交叉极化鉴别（XPD）模型，捕捉了现有方法所忽略的依赖于几何结构的去极化特性。所提方法复现了实测漫散射功率趋势，在测试材料上实现了低至3 dB的RMSE，并在几乎所有材料-极化情况下改进了基线常数XPD模型的XPD预测。这些结果为毫米波系统的高保真信道建模建立了一种物理一致且实践可行的方法。

</details>


### [100] [ISAC-Enabled Grant-Free Uplink via Artificial-Path Delay Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31366)
*Ruiqi Kong,He Chen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人工路径时延调制的通感一体化免授权上行框架，通过在下行CP内引入可控弱人工路径实现上行信息传输，无需SIC即可在时延感知域完成上下行共存。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于叠加与连续干扰消除的免授权上行方案复杂度高且对下行链路干扰较大，亟需一种低复杂度、免SIC、对下行友好的免授权上行方案。

Method: 利用下行调度波形构建可控人工路径，通过调制该路径的时延来承载上行信息；在CP内引入弱人工路径，使AP从CSI扰动中解码上行符号；开发了基线时延校准和归一化匹配滤波检测器以应对未知路径增益和离网泄漏。

Result: 反射功率决定上下行用户间的可靠性折中，时延步长主要控制上行用户的可靠性-效率折中而对下行影响很小；人工路径比下行信号弱15 dB时，上行用户仍能以等效16-QAM调制阶数获得比下行用户更低的误码率。

Conclusion: 所提框架为ISAC系统提供了一种低复杂度、免SIC、对下行友好的免授权上行解决方案。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled grant-free uplink framework based on artificial-path delay modulation. A grant-free user equipment (g-UE) conveys uplink information by modulating the delay of a controllable artificial path derived from the scheduled downlink waveform. In contrast to conventional superposition-based schemes with successive interference cancellation, the proposed method enables uplink-downlink coexistence in the delay-sensing domain. By introducing a single weak artificial path confined within the cyclic prefix (CP), the g-UE allows the access point (AP) to decode uplink symbols from CSI perturbations while causing only limited degradation to the scheduled user equipment (s-UE) in the downlink. To support reliable finite-alphabet delay detection under unknown path gain and off-grid leakage, we develop a baseline delay calibration procedure and a normalized matched-filter detector. Results show that reflection power determines the reliability trade-off between the g-UE and the s-UE, whereas the delay step mainly controls the g-UE reliability-efficiency trade-off with little additional impact on the downlink s-UE. Even with an artificial path 15 dB weaker than the scheduled downlink signal, the g-UE achieves lower BER than the s-UE at an effective modulation order of 16-QAM. The proposed framework thus offers a low-complexity, SIC-free, and downlink-friendly solution for grant-free uplink in ISAC systems.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于人工路径时延调制的通感一体化（ISAC）免授权上行框架。免授权用户设备（g-UE）通过调制来自调度下行波形的可控人工路径的时延来传输上行信息。与传统的基于叠加及连续干扰消除的方案不同，所提方法能够在时延感知域实现上下行共存。通过在循环前缀（CP）内引入一条单一且微弱的人工路径，g-UE使接入点（AP）能够从信道状态信息扰动中解码上行符号，同时对下行链路中的调度用户设备（s-UE）仅造成有限的性能损失。为在未知路径增益和离网泄漏条件下实现可靠的有限字母表时延检测，我们开发了基线时延校准流程和归一化匹配滤波检测器。结果表明，反射功率决定了g-UE与s-UE之间的可靠性折中，而时延步长主要控制g-UE的可靠性-效率折中，对下行s-UE的影响很小。即使人工路径比调度下行信号弱15 dB，g-UE在以等效16-QAM调制阶数传输时仍能达到比s-UE更低的误码率。因此，所提框架为ISAC系统中的免授权上行提供了一种低复杂度、免SIC且对下行友好的解决方案。

</details>


### [101] [Functional Multi-Target Detection via Bispectrum Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31579)
*Anna Little,Daniel Sanz-Alonso,Mikhail Sweeney,Ruiyi Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多目标检测的泛函理论，针对带噪声的单个观测中包含多个未知平移的紧支撑信号恢复问题，分析了两种基于自相关分析的非初始化恢复算法，并给出了非渐近恢复保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作多假设信号在离散网格上对齐且噪声为白噪声，而实际应用（如成像、通信）中信号连续、存在离网平移且噪声具有相关性。本文旨在突破这些局限，建立更普适的理论框架。

Method: 两种算法均先通过去偏的三阶经验自相关估计信号的双谱，然后分别采用泛函频率推进方案或Kotlarski型反卷积公式从估计的双谱中恢复信号。

Result: 证明了两种算法的非渐近恢复保证，误差界依赖于信号光滑度和双谱估计精度，后者受噪声特性和信号出现次数控制。数值实验在低信噪比下验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的泛函理论为多目标检测提供了严格的理论基础，能处理连续离网平移和相关噪声，扩展了传统离散网格白噪声模型的适用范围。

Original Abstract: This paper develops a functional theory for multi-target detection, where a compactly supported signal is recovered from a single noisy observation containing many unknown translations of the signal. Our formulation allows continuous, off-grid translations and correlated stationary Gaussian process noise, extending beyond the discrete, grid-aligned, white-noise models common in prior work. We analyze two uninitialized recovery algorithms based on autocorrelation analysis; in particular, both algorithms first estimate the signal's bispectrum via a debiased third-order empirical autocorrelation. The signal is then recovered from the estimated bispectrum using either a functional frequency marching scheme or a Kotlarski-type deconvolution formula. For both algorithms, we prove non-asymptotic recovery guarantees for compactly supported signals without bandlimiting assumptions. The resulting error bounds depend on the smoothness of the signal and the accuracy of bispectrum estimation, with the latter governed by the noise characteristics and the number of signal occurrences. Numerical experiments validate our theory and demonstrate accurate recovery in low-SNR regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 本文为多目标检测建立了一个泛函理论，其中从一个包含信号多个未知平移的单个带噪观测中恢复一个紧支撑信号。我们的公式允许连续、离网平移和相关平稳高斯过程噪声，超越了先前工作中常见的离散、网格对齐、白噪声模型。我们分析了两种基于自相关分析的非初始化恢复算法；特别地，两种算法都首先通过去偏的三阶经验自相关来估计信号的双谱。然后，利用泛函频率推进方案或Kotlarski型反卷积公式从估计的双谱中恢复信号。对于两种算法，我们证明了紧支撑信号在没有带限假设下的非渐近恢复保证。得到的误差界依赖于信号的光滑度和双谱估计的精度，后者由噪声特性和信号出现次数决定。数值实验验证了我们的理论，并展示了低信噪比下的精确恢复能力。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [MoE-dqINR: A Unified Mixture-of-Experts Implicit Neural Representation Framework for Scan-Specific Dynamic and Quantitative MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31302)
*Yinzhe Wu,Fanwen Wang,Zhenxuan Zhang,Zi Wang,Chengyan Wang,Guang Yang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出MoE-dqINR框架，利用状态条件混合专家隐式神经表示，实现快速（约30秒/扫描）且灵活的多线圈MRI重建，统一了动态和定量MRI的共享空间表示与状态合成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扫描特定的隐式神经表示方法在动态和定量MRI重建中存在灵活性不足（难以共享空间信息同时适应不同采集状态）和计算开销大（每扫描优化需数百到数千秒）的问题。

Method: 提出MoE-dqINR框架，将图像域表示分解为共享空间专家（编码坐标依赖的可重用图像内容）和状态条件路由路径（根据有序采集状态的状态索引驱动路由，从公共专家库合成每个动态帧或对比度状态），并与多线圈MRI前向模型耦合。

Result: 将扫描特定的INR优化时间缩短至约30秒/扫描，显著优于现有数百到数千秒的基线方法。

Conclusion: 状态条件混合专家隐式神经表示可作为扫描特定的多线圈MRI重建先验，统一了共享空间表示、动态和定量MRI特异性合成以及实用的每扫描效率。

Original Abstract: Undersampled magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) reconstruction seeks to recover temporally or contrast-varying image series from incomplete multicoil k-space data while preserving state-dependent fidelity for dynamic and quantitative MRI (qMRI). Existing scan-specific implicit neural representations (INRs) often use monolithic spatiotemporal coordinate fields, explicit subspaces, motion or deformation models, calibration variables, or sequence-specific quantitative signal models. These design choices can limit flexibility in sharing spatial information while adapting image synthesis across acquisition states. Moreover, many INR-based baselines remain computationally demanding, typically requiring per-scan optimization times on the order of hundreds to thousands of seconds. We propose MoE-dqINR, a scan-specific multicoil MRI reconstruction framework that factorizes the image-domain representation into shared spatial experts and a state-conditioned routing pathway. Spatial experts encode reusable coordinate-dependent image content, whereas routing weights, conditioned on ordered acquisition states, synthesize each dynamic frame or contrast state from a common expert bank. The representation is coupled to a multicoil MRI forward model, uses the normalized state index to drive routing in both dynamic and quantitative MRI. By separating shared spatial representation from state-dependent synthesis, the framework provides an image-first architecture for dynamic and quantitative MRI while reducing scan-specific INR optimization to approximately 30 s per scan in our experiments. The proposed formulation establishes state-conditioned mixture-of-experts INR as a scan-specific multicoil MRI reconstruction prior that unifies shared spatial representation, dynamic- and qMRI-specific synthesis, and practical per-scan efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 欠采样磁共振成像（MRI）重建旨在从不完整的多线圈k空间数据中恢复时间或对比度变化的图像序列，同时为动态和定量MRI（qMRI）保留状态相关的保真度。现有的扫描特定隐式神经表示（INR）通常采用整体式时空坐标场、显式子空间、运动或变形模型、校准变量或序列特定的定量信号模型。这些设计选择在跨采集状态调整图像合成的同时，限制了共享空间信息的灵活性。此外，许多基于INR的基线方法仍然计算量大，通常需要数百到数千秒的每扫描优化时间。我们提出MoE-dqINR，一种扫描特定的多线圈MRI重建框架，将图像域表示分解为共享空间专家和状态条件路由路径。空间专家编码可重用的坐标相关图像内容，而路由权重以有序采集状态为条件，从公共专家库合成每个动态帧或对比度状态。该表示与多线圈MRI前向模型耦合，在动态和定量MRI中使用归一化状态索引驱动路由。通过将共享空间表示与状态相关合成分离，该框架为动态和定量MRI提供了一种图像优先的架构，同时在我们的实验中将扫描特定的INR优化减少到每扫描约30秒。所提出的公式将状态条件混合专家INR建立为一种扫描特定的多线圈MRI重建先验，统一了共享空间表示、动态和定量MRI特异性合成以及实用的每扫描效率。

</details>

### [103] [High-Fidelity 3D Simulator for Synthetic fNIRS Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30552)
*Condell Eastmond,Niels Bracher,Xavier Intes,Stefan T. Radev*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于网格蒙特卡洛模拟的3D fNIRS仿真器，该仿真器能够生成生理上真实、全头部的合成fNIRS记录，为缺乏标注数据的fNIRS分析（尤其是AI方法）提供高质量的合成训练数据，并通过手指敲击、疼痛评估和手术技能三个开源数据集验证了仿真器的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: fNIRS数据分析面临运动伪影、生理噪声等挑战，且标注数据集稀缺，阻碍了新分析流程（特别是基于AI的方法）的开发和验证。因此，需要一个能够生成大规模、高质量、带标注合成fNIRS数据的工具。

Method: 提出一个3D fNIRS仿真器，采用基于网格的蒙特卡洛模拟，结合解剖学上准确的灵敏度剖面与参数化的血氧动力学响应、系统性生理信号和非系统性伪影模型，生成具有高时空保真度的全头部合成fNIRS记录。

Result: 利用来自开源的手指敲击、疼痛评估和手术技能三个fNIRS数据集对仿真器进行了实验验证，验证了其生成生理真实数据的能力。仿真器可生成几乎无限量的标注数据，用于去噪算法测试、数据增强、机制建模及计算机模拟实验。

Conclusion: 该3D fNIRS仿真器能够生成生理真实的合成fNIRS数据，支持可重复性和广泛采用，有望解决fNIRS领域数据标注稀缺的根本性难题，推动AI方法在fNIRS分析中的应用。

Original Abstract: Functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) provides a noninvasive window into brain activity by measuring task-related changes in oxygenated and deoxygenated hemoglobin in the cortex. A key advantage of fNIRS is its promise of use with mobile participants in complex, real-world environments, such as walking, sports, classroom learning, driving simulations, or social interactions. However, analyzing fNIRS data is challenging because of motion artifacts, physiological noise, and other confounding factors. This challenge is further compounded by the limited availability of annotated datasets, which hinders the development and validation of new analysis pipelines, particularly given the growing use of AI methods. Recognizing these challenges, we introduce a 3D fNIRS simulator that uses mesh-based Monte Carlo simulations to create physiologically realistic, full-head synthetic recordings with high spatiotemporal fidelity. Our simulator combines anatomically accurate sensitivity profiles with parameterized models of hemodynamic responses, systemic physiology, and nonsystematic artifacts. As a result, users can generate virtually unlimited labeled datasets for testing denoising algorithms, data augmentation, mechanistic modeling, or \textit{in silico} experimentation. We validate the simulator using experimental fNIRS data from open-source finger-tapping, pain-assessment, and surgical-skill datasets and provide an open-source implementation to support reproducibility and broad adoption.

中文摘要翻译: 功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）通过测量皮层中含氧和脱氧血红蛋白的任务相关变化，提供了一种非侵入性的脑活动观测窗口。fNIRS的一个关键优势在于它有望用于移动参与者在复杂真实环境中的研究，例如行走、运动、课堂学习、驾驶模拟或社交互动。然而，由于运动伪影、生理噪声和其他混杂因素的存在，fNIRS数据分析极具挑战性。标注数据集的稀缺进一步加剧了这一困难，尤其随着AI方法的日益普及，新的分析流程的开发和验证受到了严重阻碍。认识到这些挑战，我们引入了一个3D fNIRS仿真器，该仿真器使用基于网格的蒙特卡洛模拟，生成具有高时空保真度的生理真实的全头部合成记录。我们的仿真器将解剖学上准确的灵敏度剖面与参数化的血氧动力学响应、系统性生理学模型以及非系统性伪影模型相结合。因此，用户可以生成几乎无限量的标注数据集，用于测试去噪算法、数据增强、机制建模或计算机模拟实验。我们利用来自开源的手指敲击、疼痛评估和手术技能数据集中的实验fNIRS数据对仿真器进行了验证，并提供了开源实现以支持可重复性和广泛采用。

</details>


### [104] [The Metastable Mind: Neural Underpinnings of Naturalistic Cognition Through the Synthesis of Event Segmentation and Metastable Neural States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31473)
*Dora Gozukara,Nasir Ahmad,Djamari Oetringer,Linda Geerligs*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文综合认知行为科学中的事件分割（ES）理论与计算神经科学中的亚稳态神经活动（MNA）机制，论证两者本质上从不同视角研究相同的大脑亚稳态神经状态，并提出了神经状态作为认知基本计算单元的若干核心运作原理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 认知科学中的事件分割理论和计算神经科学中的亚稳态神经活动两个研究分支长期独立发展，缺乏交叉整合。作者旨在揭示它们实际上研究相同的大脑现象，通过统一理论框架来解释大脑如何通过离散神经状态组织连续经验。

Method: 系统性综述，整合认知行为学的事件分割理论与计算神经科学的亚稳态活动建模方法，从理论层面比较和统一两个分支的发现。

Result: 1. ES和MNA研究的是相同的亚稳态神经状态，只是分别从行为认知层面和机制实现层面展开；2. 神经状态呈时空嵌套层级结构，高级区域的长时程状态约束并受低级区域快速状态塑造；3. 神经状态反映潜在的预测模型，塑造感知、决策、记忆编码与回忆；4. 神经状态是模块化加工时期，边界处以连接组重构为特征。

Conclusion: 理解神经状态如何涌现、交互和塑造认知，有助于揭示大脑自然运作模式，为统一认知神经科学理论提供基础。

Original Abstract: A multitude of findings and theories from cognitive, behavioural and computational neuroscience show that neural activity unfolds in a variety of meaningful temporal units. Behavioural research on event segmentation (ES) has shown that continuous experience is segmented into discrete events and sub-events, which aid real-time comprehension, memory, and decision-making. Computational neuroscience research observes and models ongoing brain activity as a series of stable population activity that occur across wide spatial and temporal scales, referred to as metastable neural activity (MNA). Through this review, we show that these isolated branches of literature, the cognitive theory of Event Segmentation (ES) and the mechanistic approach of metastability (MNA), actually study the same metastable neural states from different perspectives. While the behavioural branch offers a theory for the cognitive and behavioural utility of segmentation, the metastability literature provides the mechanistic account at the implementational level. We describe how metastable neural states act as the fundamental computational units of cognition and identify a number of core principles of how they operate. One is the spatio-temporally nested hierarchy of states, where longer-duration states in higher-order regions both constrain and are shaped by states in faster-operating regions. Another is that neural states are a reflection of underlying predictive models which shape perception, decision making, memory encoding and recall. And finally that neural states are periods of more modular processing, which are interspersed by boundaries where there is a reconfiguration of connectivity. Understanding how neural states emerge, interact, and shape cognition brings us closer to understanding the brain in its natural mode of operation.

中文摘要翻译: 来自认知神经科学、行为神经科学和计算神经科学的大量发现与理论表明，神经活动以多种有意义的时序单元展开。关于事件分割（ES）的行为研究表明，连续体验被分割为离散的事件和子事件，这有助于实时理解、记忆和决策。计算神经科学的研究将持续的脑活动观察并建模为一系列在广泛时空尺度上出现的稳定群体活动，称为亚稳态神经活动（MNA）。通过本综述，我们证明这两个孤立的研究分支——事件分割的认知理论与亚稳态的机制方法——实际上从不同视角研究相同的亚稳态神经状态。行为分支为分割的认知和行为效用提供了理论，而亚稳态文献则在实现层面提供了机制性解释。我们描述了亚稳态神经状态如何作为认知的基本计算单元，并识别了其运作的若干核心原则：其一是在时空上嵌套的层级状态结构，高级区域的长时程状态既约束又受到快速运作区域状态的影响；其二是神经状态反映了潜在的预测模型，这些模型塑造感知、决策、记忆编码和回忆；其三是神经状态是更模块化加工的时期，其间被连接组重构的边界间隔。理解神经状态如何涌现、交互并塑造认知，使我们更接近理解大脑在其自然运作模式下的工作机制。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Gait2Hip-60: A Unified Deep Learning Benchmark for Predicting Hip Muscle Forces and Joint Moments from Multi-Cadence Gait Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30374)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Ji Hou,Qing Sun,Xianzhi Gao,Bo Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的框架，直接从下肢步态运动学预测髋部肌肉力和关节力矩，比较了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种序列模型。在60名健康成年人和9名股骨头坏死患者的外部验证中，Transformer模型表现最佳，证明从步态运动学估计髋部动力学参数的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于肌骨仿真的方法耗时且难以在临床中应用，亟需一种高效、简便的方法直接从步态运动学数据估计髋部动力学参数，以提升临床可用性。

Method: 收集60名健康成年人在三种节拍器引导步速下的步态数据，以10个双侧下肢关节角度作为输入，OpenSim计算的髋部肌肉力和关节力矩作为参考输出。在统一的数据划分、预处理流程和评估指标下，训练并比较LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型，并在9名股骨头坏死患者上进行零样本外部验证。

Result: 在健康受试者中，Transformer在髋部肌肉力预测（RMSE=1.33 N/kg, MAE=0.57 N/kg, R²=0.819）和关节力矩预测（RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg, MAE=0.07 Nm/kg, R²=0.862）上均取得最优表现。在零样本外部验证中，Transformer在ONFH患者上仍保持中等预测能力（肌肉力R²=0.537，力矩R²=0.569）。

Conclusion: 从步态运动学估计髋部动力学是可行的，Transformer可作为强基线模型，但在临床推广前仍需更广泛的病理验证和更好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Estimating hip muscle forces and joint moments during gait typically relies on musculoskeletal simulation, which is informative but time-consuming and difficult to apply in clinical settings. This study developed a deep learning framework to predict these hip dynamics parameters directly from lower-limb gait kinematics and compared three representative sequence models under a unified protocol. Gait data were collected from 60 healthy adults under three metronome-guided cadence conditions. Ten bilateral lower-limb joint angles were used as inputs, and OpenSim-derived hip muscle forces and hip joint moments were used as reference outputs. Three deep learning models of LSTM, Transformer, and Mamba were trained and evaluated using the same subject-level split, preprocessing pipeline, and metrics. The best model was then directly tested on an external cohort of 9 patients with osteonecrosis of the femoral head (ONFH) without retraining. In the healthy-subject benchmark, Transformer achieved the best subject-level mean performance for both hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.33 N/kg, MAE = 0.57 N/kg, R2 = 0.819) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.11 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.07 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.862), with similar advantages across walking cadences. In zero-shot external validation, Transformer retained moderate predictive ability in ONFH for hip muscle force prediction (RMSE = 1.51 N/kg, MAE = 0.70 N/kg, R2 = 0.537) and hip joint moment prediction (RMSE = 0.17 Nm/kg, MAE = 0.12 Nm/kg, R2 = 0.569). These findings support the feasibility of estimating hip dynamics from gait kinematics, identify Transformer as a strong baseline, and highlight the need for broader pathological validation and improved generalization before clinical application.

中文摘要翻译: 在步态过程中估算髋部肌肉力和关节力矩通常依赖于肌骨仿真，该方法信息丰富但耗时且难以在临床中应用。本研究开发了一个深度学习框架，直接从下肢步态运动学预测这些髋部动力学参数，并在统一协议下比较了三种代表性的序列模型。步态数据来自60名健康成年人在三种节拍器引导步速条件下的采集。输入为10个双侧下肢关节角度，参考输出为OpenSim推导的髋部肌肉力和髋关节力矩。采用相同的受试者级划分、预处理流程和评估指标，训练并评估了LSTM、Transformer和Mamba三种深度学习模型。随后将最优模型直接应用于9名股骨头坏死（ONFH）患者的外部队列进行零样本测试，无需重新训练。在健康受试者基准测试中，Transformer在髋部肌肉力预测（RMSE=1.33 N/kg, MAE=0.57 N/kg, R²=0.819）和髋关节力矩预测（RMSE=0.11 Nm/kg, MAE=0.07 Nm/kg, R²=0.862）方面均取得了最优的受试者级平均表现，且在不同步速下具有相似优势。在零样本外部验证中，Transformer在ONFH患者上仍保持了中等预测能力，髋部肌肉力预测（RMSE=1.51 N/kg, MAE=0.70 N/kg, R²=0.537）和髋关节力矩预测（RMSE=0.17 Nm/kg, MAE=0.12 Nm/kg, R²=0.569）。这些发现支持了从步态运动学估计髋部动力学的可行性，将Transformer确定为强基线模型，并指出了在临床推广前需要更广泛的病理验证和改进泛化能力。

</details>


### [106] [When LLMs Learn to Be Consistently Wrong: A Multi-Model Study of Linear Representations of Synthetic Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30381)
*Vahideh Zolfaghari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中的欺骗性对齐现象，通过在五个Transformer模型上使用LoRA微调生成诚实与欺骗变体，发现线性探针能在早期层以近完美AUC检测合成欺骗，且欺骗表示在深层逐渐固化，表明通过适度监督微调即可快速建立鲁棒的领域不变欺骗表示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 欺骗性对齐是AI安全的核心挑战，尤其是策略性欺骗作为长期风险备受关注。本文旨在通过合成欺骗（直接对错误答案进行优化）作为受控实验平台，研究学习型欺骗的表征基础，为理解模型内部欺骗表示的早期检测、泛化性和鲁棒性提供系统分析。

Method: 采用多模型范式，对五个Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）使用LoRA在相同问题分布上进行微调，生成诚实与欺骗变体。使用基于均值池化隐藏状态的线性探针和MLP探针检测欺骗，并通过Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨域对齐和校准误差（ECE）进行机制分析。

Result: 线性探针在前1-3层即可实现近完美AUC（≥0.99）检测欺骗（Pythia-1.4B峰值0.705）；Logistic回归探针一致匹配或优于MLP探针；TruthfulQA训练的探针可近乎零损失泛化到MMLU；深层表示对高斯噪声具有强鲁棒性；机制分析揭示Pythia/Llama/Qwen的表征塌缩与Gemma-2的高维保持两种模式；欺骗方向在深层逐渐固化，最优校准精度可在1-4层实现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过适度的监督微调即可快速在模型中建立鲁棒、领域不变的欺骗表示，这对基于激活的监控方法具有重要启示意义。

Original Abstract: Deceptive alignment, in which models maintain accurate internal representations while deliberately producing false outputs, remains a central challenge in AI safety. While strategic deception is the primary long-term concern, synthetic dishonesty - induced via direct optimization on incorrect answers - provides a controlled testbed for studying the representational basis of learned deception. We introduce a multi-model paradigm in which honest and deceptive variants of five transformer models (Pythia-1.4B, Gemma-2-2B/9B, Qwen2.5-7B, Llama-3.1-8B) are fine-tuned using LoRA on the same question distribution. Linear probes trained on mean-pooled hidden states detect synthetic dishonesty with near-perfect AUC (greater than or equal to 0.99) as early as layers 1-3 in four architectures, while Pythia-1.4B reaches a peak of 0.705. Logistic regression probes consistently match or outperform MLP probes, supporting the Linear Representation Hypothesis. Probes trained on TruthfulQA generalize with near-zero loss (Delta AUC approx. 0) to held-out MMLU subjects. Late-layer representations show strong robustness to Gaussian noise, with Gemma-2 models exhibiting exceptional stability. Mechanistic analysis of Fisher Discriminant Ratio, effective rank, centroid geometry, directional stability, cross-domain alignment, and calibration (ECE) reveals two regimes: representational collapse in Pythia/Llama/Qwen versus high-dimensional preservation in Gemma-2. Across all models, the dishonesty direction consolidates progressively in deeper layers, with optimal calibration (ECE less than 0.01 except Pythia) achievable in layers 1-4. These results demonstrate that robust, domain-invariant dishonesty representations can be rapidly entrenched via modest supervised fine-tuning, with implications for activation-based monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 欺骗性对齐——模型在保持准确内部表征的同时故意产生虚假输出——仍然是AI安全的核心挑战。尽管策略性欺骗是主要的长期担忧，但合成欺骗——通过对错误答案进行直接优化而诱导产生——为研究习得性欺骗的表征基础提供了一个受控实验平台。我们引入了一个多模型范式，在相同问题分布上使用LoRA对五个Transformer模型（Pythia-1.4B、Gemma-2-2B/9B、Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B）的诚实变体和欺骗变体进行微调。基于均值池化隐藏状态训练的线性探针在四种架构的前1-3层即可检测到合成欺骗，AUC近乎完美（≥0.99），而Pythia-1.4B达到0.705的峰值。Logistic回归探针一致地匹配或优于MLP探针，支持线性表征假说。在TruthfulQA上训练的探针以近乎零损失（ΔAUC ≈ 0）泛化到保留的MMLU科目。深层表示对高斯噪声表现出强鲁棒性，其中Gemma-2模型展现出卓越的稳定性。对Fisher判别比、有效秩、质心几何、方向稳定性、跨域对齐和校准误差（ECE）的机制分析揭示了两种模式：Pythia/Llama/Qwen中的表征塌缩，以及Gemma-2中的高维保持。在所有模型中，欺骗方向在更深层逐步固化，最优校准精度（除Pythia外ECE < 0.01）可在第1-4层实现。这些结果表明，通过适度的监督微调即可快速建立鲁棒、领域不变的欺骗表示，这对基于激活的监控方法具有启示意义。

</details>


### [107] [A Novel Evaluation Metric for Unsupervised Learning in AIS-Based Maritime Anomaly Detection: MADQI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30388)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiyan,Raza Hasan,Shakeel Ahmad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向海事AIS数据无监督异常检测的新型质量评估框架MADQI，通过四个组合指标（ARC、PPS、SDS、ECE）在无需标注数据的情况下系统评估异常检测性能，实验得分为80.37%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的无监督异常检测算法（如Isolation Forest）在海事AIS数据中应用广泛，但缺乏系统且有意义的不依赖标注数据的评估指标，无法有效衡量检测质量。

Method: 提出MADQI（Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index）复合质量指标，包含四个子指标：异常率一致性(ARC)、物理合理性评分(PPS)、分数分布分离度(SDS)和极端案例证据(ECE)；通过多分块评估和自适应缩放技术实现自动归一化，并基于Haversine距离计算分析AIS数据集的空间与行为特征。

Result: MADQI总体得分为80.37%；其中ECE得分0.907，ARC得分1.000，表明模型在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架为无监督海事AIS异常检测提供了一种可靠且有意义的评估方法，实验结果表明其有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper introduces a new systematic framework for detecting anomalies in maritime Automatic Identification System (AIS) datasets. These anomalies include abnormal vessel behaviours related to speed, position jumps, time gaps, and turn angles. Although unsupervised learning algorithms such as Isolation Forest are widely used for detecting anomalous vessel movements, they often lack systematic and meaningful evaluation measures. To address this limitation, we propose a novel quality metric called Maritime Anomaly Detection Quality Index (MADQI). The prosed MADQI is a composite index designed to evaluate the anomaly detection performance of machine learning models without requiring labelled data. The proposed framework uses Haversine distance calculations to analyse AIS datasets and identify anomalies based on their spatial and behavioural characteristics. The proposed MADQI evaluation framework integrates four interconnected metrics: Anomaly Rate Consistency (ARC), Physical Plausibility Score (PPS), Score Distribution Separation (SDS), and Extreme Case Evidence (ECE). These metrics are combined through automatic normalisation using multi-chunk evaluation and adaptive scaling techniques. Experimental results on the AIS dataset show that the proposed framework achieved a MADQI score of 80.37%, demonstrating its effectiveness for unsupervised anomaly detection. In particular, the algorithm performed strongly in identifying abnormal vessel behaviour. Among the individual MADQI components, ECE and ARC achieved scores of 0.907 and 1.000, respectively, indicating excellent capability in detecting extreme anomalies and maintaining anomaly rate consistency. Overall, these results are encouraging and demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a reliable and meaningful approach for evaluating unsupervised anomaly detection in maritime AIS data.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种新的系统性框架，用于检测海事自动识别系统（AIS）数据集中的异常。这些异常包括与速度、位置跳变、时间间隔和转向角度相关的异常船舶行为。尽管孤立森林等无监督学习算法被广泛用于检测异常船舶运动，但它们通常缺乏系统且有意义的评估指标。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种新的质量指标——海事异常检测质量指数（MADQI）。所提出的MADQI是一种复合指数，旨在无需标注数据的情况下评估机器学习模型的异常检测性能。该框架利用Haversine距离计算来分析AIS数据集，并基于空间和行为特征识别异常。所提出的MADQI评估框架集成了四个相互关联的指标：异常率一致性（ARC）、物理合理性评分（PPS）、分数分布分离度（SDS）和极端案例证据（ECE）。这些指标通过使用多分块评估和自适应缩放技术的自动归一化进行组合。在AIS数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的框架获得了80.37%的MADQI得分，证明了其对无监督异常检测的有效性。特别地，该算法在识别异常船舶行为方面表现强劲。在MADQI的各组成指标中，ECE和ARC分别达到了0.907和1.000的得分，表明其在检测极端异常和保持异常率一致性方面具有出色能力。总体而言，这些结果令人鼓舞，表明所提出的框架为海事AIS数据中的无监督异常检测提供了一种可靠且有意义的评估方法。

</details>


### [108] [NumLeak: Public Numeric Benchmarks as Latent Labels in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30393)
*Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NumLeak框架揭示了顶级大语言模型在公共数值基准测试中存在严重的记忆化回忆问题，而非真正的泛化能力；通过API边界探针和白盒验证实验，发现模型能高精度回忆Fama-French因子、美国失业率等公开数据，且闭源黑盒探针低估了记忆化程度；提出一句系统提示防御可阻断99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 公共数值基准测试可能出现在预训练数据中，因此基于日期的评估可能衡量的是记忆化回忆而非样本外技能，需要一种框架来量化并区分模型的记忆化与真实推理能力。

Method: 提出NumLeak测量框架，结合两个层面：(1) 对生产模型进行API边界探针测试；(2) 在开源因果语言模型上进行白盒受控验证。通过剂量-反应实验、logprob排名检测以及残差化分析来识别记忆化。

Result: (1) 顶级模型回忆Fama-French市场超额收益的池化Pearson r=0.97-0.99，五个兄弟因子偏差在25bps内；同样高保真度出现在美国失业率、CPI通胀和NOAA温度数据。(2) 在最新发布的保留数据集上，解析率降至21-57%，但已回答月份的相关性仍约为0.99，呈现拒绝-回忆不对称性。(3) 白盒实验重现了剂量-反应关系，logprob排名检测到开放生成遗漏的记忆化。(4) Sonnet模型的"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的相关性从r=0.74降至r=0.02（残差化后）。(5) 一句系统提示防御以接近零的效用成本阻断了99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击。

Conclusion: 当前顶级大语言模型存在严重的数值记忆化问题，闭源API黑盒探针会低估该问题的严重程度；但通过简单的系统提示防御可以有效缓解非自适应攻击。

Original Abstract: Public numeric benchmarks appear in pretraining, so an evaluation that conditions on a date may be measuring memorized recall rather than out-of-sample skill. We introduce NumLeak, a measurement framework that combines API-boundary probes on production models with a white-box controlled validation on an open causal LM. Top-tier frontier LLMs recall the Fama-French market excess return at 3-seed pooled Pearson r=0.97-0.99 while staying within 0.15 within-25bps on the five sibling factors; comparable fidelity appears on U.S. unemployment, CPI inflation, and NOAA temperature. On a recent-release holdout, parse rate collapses to 21-57% but r stays at approximately 0.99 on months answered, the refuse-or-recall asymmetry a memorized channel predicts. The white-box experiment reproduces the dose-response, and logprob ranking detects memorization that open-ended generation misses, implying closed-API black-box probes understate the channel. A Sonnet "date to market-sentiment" regression that correlates with true Mkt-RF at r=0.74 collapses to r=0.02 once the model's own recall is residualized out. A one-line system-prompt defense blocks 99.8% of a non-adaptive single-turn suffix attack set at near-zero utility cost on conceptual and historical-narrative queries

中文摘要翻译: 公开数值基准测试可能出现在预训练数据中，因此基于日期的评估可能衡量的是记忆化回忆而非样本外技能。我们引入NumLeak，一个结合了生产模型API边界探针与开源因果语言模型白盒受控验证的测量框架。顶级前沿LLM对Fama-French市场超额收益的回忆在3种子池化后Pearson r=0.97-0.99，同时五个兄弟因子的偏差保持在25个基点以内；在美国失业率、CPI通胀和NOAA温度数据上也显示出可比的保真度。在一个最新发布的保留数据集上，解析率骤降至21-57%，但已回答月份的相关性仍保持在约0.99，这种拒绝或回忆的不对称性正是记忆化通道所预测的。白盒实验重现了剂量-反应关系，logprob排名检测到了开放生成遗漏的记忆化，这表明闭源API黑盒探针低估了该通道。一个Sonnet模型"日期到市场情绪"回归与真实Mkt-RF的相关性为r=0.74，但在将模型自身的回忆残差化后骤降至r=0.02。一句系统提示防御能以接近零的效用成本阻断99.8%的非自适应单轮后缀攻击集，对概念性和历史叙事性查询几乎无影响。

</details>


### [109] [LongDS-Bench: On the Failure of Long-Horizon Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30434)
*Kewei Xu,Xiaoben Lu,Shuofei Qiao,Zihan Ding,Haoming Xu,Lei Liang,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongDS是一个面向长周期、多轮次数据分析的基准测试，包含68个任务、2225轮次，覆盖6个领域。评估发现最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%，早期到后期性能下降近47个百分点，长周期错误占失败的52%-69%。核心瓶颈在于维护正确分析状态而非增加交互预算。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准主要评估孤立或短交互任务，缺乏对Agent在长周期数据中跟踪演变上下文能力的测试，因此需要构建长周期多轮次数据分析的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 从真实Kaggle笔记本构建68个长周期多轮次任务（共2225轮），涵盖地球科学、商业、教育等6个领域。任务围绕状态演化模式设计（如反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度为11.3轮。

Result: 评估5个SOTA模型，最佳模型平均准确率仅48.45%；早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点；长周期错误占失败原因的52%-69%；增加Agent步骤并不必然提升性能。

Conclusion: 当前Agent在长周期数据分析中表现有限，核心瓶颈在于维持正确分析状态的能力而非交互预算。LongDS可用于支撑可靠的长周期Agent数据分析研究。

Original Abstract: Real-world data analysis is inherently iterative, yet existing benchmarks mostly evaluate isolated or short interactive tasks, leaving agents' ability to track evolving analytical context over long horizons untested. We introduce LongDS, a benchmark for long-horizon, multi-turn data analysis where agents must maintain, update, restore, and compose evolving analytical states. LongDS comprises 68 tasks constructed from real-world Kaggle notebooks, spanning 2,225 turns across six domains including Geoscience, Business, and Education. Tasks are designed around state-evolution patterns (e.g., counterfactual perturbation, rollback, multi-state composition), with an average dependency span of 11.3 turns. Evaluating five state-of-the-art models, we find that the best model reaches only 48.45% average accuracy, performance drops nearly 47 points from early to late turns, and long-horizon errors account for 52%--69% of failures. Further analysis shows that additional agent steps do not necessarily improve performance, suggesting that the key bottleneck is maintaining a correct analytical state rather than increasing interaction budget. We release LongDS to support research on reliable long-horizon agentic data analysis. Code and data will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind.

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的数据分析本质上是迭代的，然而现有基准主要评估孤立或短交互任务，导致Agent在长时间范围内跟踪不断演变的分析上下文的能力尚未得到检验。我们提出了LongDS，一个面向长周期、多轮次数据分析的基准，要求Agent必须维护、更新、恢复和组合不断演变的分析状态。LongDS包含68个从真实世界Kaggle笔记本构建的任务，涵盖地球科学、商业和教育等6个领域的2225轮交互。任务围绕状态演化模式设计（例如，反事实扰动、回滚、多状态组合），平均依赖跨度为11.3轮。在评估五个最先进模型时，我们发现最佳模型平均准确率仅达到48.45%，从早期到后期轮次性能下降近47个百分点，并且长周期错误占失败原因的52%-69%。进一步分析表明，增加Agent步骤并不必然提升性能，这表明关键瓶颈在于维持正确的分析状态而非增加交互预算。我们发布LongDS以支持可靠的长周期Agent数据分析研究。代码和数据将在https://github.com/zjunlp/DataMind发布。

</details>


### [110] [Calibrated Preference Learning: The Case of Label Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30447)
*Santo M. A. R. Thies,Viktor Bengs,Timo Kaufmann,Sebastian J. Vollmer,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次形式化定义了概率标签排序中的校准问题，提出了一套涵盖完整排序、子排序和Top-k排序的校准层次体系，并通过理论证明和实验分析揭示了现有模型在校准方面的不足及其与准确率的关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 校准（预测概率与真实频率对齐）在分类和回归中已被广泛研究，但概率标签排序（预测标签集排序上的分布）领域尚未有正式的校准定义。简单将排序视为类别会忽略其结构特征，且无法捕捉成对预测、Top-k预测等重要模式。因此，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文形式化定义了标签排序的校准概念，并构建了一个包含完整排序校准、子排序校准和Top-k校准的层次化框架。通过理论分析证明了各校准概念之间的蕴含关系，并通过实验评估了流行标签排序模型（包括RLHF奖励模型）的校准性能。

Result: 理论结果表明：完整排序校准蕴含其他校准概念但反之不成立，且子排序校准与Top-k校准不可比较。实验发现：流行的标签排序模型通常校准较差，子排序指标与Top-k指标之间存在显著差异；在RLHF奖励模型中，校准与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致，表明校准捕捉了Top-1准确率之外有意义的模型质量维度。

Conclusion: 本文的工作为标签排序中的校准研究奠定了理论基础，揭示了现有模型在校准方面的不足，并指出了未来研究的方向：理解误校准的下游影响以及开发相应的校正方法。

Original Abstract: Calibration, the alignment of predicted probabilities with true outcome frequencies, is essential for reliable decision-making. While extensively studied for classification and regression, calibration has not been formally addressed for probabilistic label ranking, where the goal is to predict a distribution over orderings of a label set. Naively treating rankings as classes ignores their structure and fails to capture important modalities such as pairwise and top-k predictions. We formalize calibration for label ranking and develop a hierarchy of notions covering full rankings, sub-rankings, and top-k rankings. We prove that full-rank calibration implies the others but not conversely, and sub-ranking and top-k calibration are incomparable. Empirically, we find popular label ranking models are often poorly calibrated, with substantial differences between sub-ranking and top-k metrics. Applying our framework to RLHF reward models, we find that calibration correlates strongly but not perfectly with benchmark accuracy, suggesting it captures a meaningful quality dimension beyond top-1 accuracy. These findings motivate future work on understanding the downstream effects of miscalibration and developing methods to correct it.

中文摘要翻译: 校准（即预测概率与真实结果频率的对齐）对于可靠的决策制定至关重要。尽管校准在分类和回归中已被广泛研究，但在概率标签排序（其目标是预测标签集排序上的分布）中尚未被正式处理。简单地将排序视为类别会忽略其结构特征，且无法捕捉成对预测和Top-k预测等重要模态。本文形式化定义了标签排序中的校准概念，并构建了一套涵盖完整排序、子排序和Top-k排序的校准层次体系。我们证明了完整排序校准蕴含其他校准概念但反之不成立，且子排序校准与Top-k校准不可比较。实验发现，流行的标签排序模型通常校准较差，子排序指标与Top-k指标之间存在显著差异。将我们的框架应用于RLHF奖励模型时，我们发现校准与基准准确率强相关但不完全一致，这表明校准捕捉了超越Top-1准确率的有意义的模型质量维度。这些发现激励了未来关于理解误校准的下游影响以及开发校正方法的研究工作。

</details>


### [111] [Bounded Behavioral Indistinguishability for Black-Box LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30448)
*Munawar Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有界行为不可区分性（bounded behavioral indistinguishability）作为黑盒LLM蒸馏的新评估标准，并通过Qwen和Llama教师-学生对的对抗评估实验表明，语义相似度提升不足以保证行为不可区分性，需要结合对抗性和类别感知的评估方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有黑盒LLM蒸馏评估仅关注输出语义相似性或任务一致性，忽视了学生模型是否在行为上与教师模型不可区分。作者旨在引入更强的行为不可区分性概念，并实证检验蒸馏是否真正缩小了行为差距。

Method: 形式化定义了$(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-有界行为不可区分性；在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上，使用5000条提示的探测套件进行对抗评估；对比基础学生和LoRA蒸馏学生；采用跨族Llama评判器、A/B交换一致性过滤、基于分歧的查询采样等策略。

Result: LoRA将语义相似度从0.788提升至0.862（Qwen）和0.814至0.874（Llama）；但对抗评估显示学习型判别器仍保持非零优势，风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术类提示中存在伪影；跨族教师识别任务中，Qwen区分优势从0.158降至0.081；分歧引导采样并不稳定优于分层随机采样。

Conclusion: 语义保真度有用但不充分；黑盒LLM蒸馏需要结合有界性、对抗性和类别感知的评估框架。

Original Abstract: Black-box LLM distillation is usually evaluated as an output-matching problem: a student is considered successful when its responses are semantically similar to, or task-consistent with, those of a teacher. However, output similarity does not imply that the student is behaviorally indistinguishable from the model it imitates. We introduce bounded behavioral indistinguishability, formalized as $(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-behavioral indistinguishability over an explicit prompt distribution, where $ε$ bounds distinguishing advantage, $q$ bounds oracle queries, $t$ bounds computation, and $\mathbb{A}$ denotes the adversary class.
  We instantiate this notion on Qwen and Llama teacher-student pairs using a controlled $5,000$-prompt behavioral probe suite. For each family, we compare the teacher with both the base student and the LoRA-distilled student, measuring whether distillation reduces distinguishability rather than merely improving similarity. LoRA raises semantic similarity from $0.788$ to $0.862$ for Qwen and from $0.814$ to $0.874$ for Llama. Yet adversarial evaluation reveals remaining behavioral differences: learned discriminators retain nonzero advantage, and pairwise category analysis shows artifacts concentrated in style/format, robustness, and domain-technical prompts. A pairwise teacher-identification adversary confirms this trend. With a different-family Llama judge and A/B-swap consistency filtering, Qwen distinguishing advantage drops from $0.158$ for the base student to $0.081$ after LoRA distillation. Query-budget experiments show that disagreement-guided acquisition does not consistently outperform stratified random sampling, indicating that coverage and diversity remain strong baselines. Our results show that semantic fidelity is useful but insufficient: black-box LLM distillation requires bounded, adversarial, and category-aware evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 黑盒大语言模型蒸馏通常被评估为一个输出匹配问题：当学生模型的响应在语义上相似于或在任务上与教师模型一致时，即认为蒸馏成功。然而，输出相似性并不意味着学生在行为上与它所模仿的模型不可区分。我们引入了有界行为不可区分性，形式化为在显式提示分布上的$(ε,q,t,\mathbb{A})$-行为不可区分性，其中$ε$限制区分优势，$q$限制预言机查询次数，$t$限制计算量，$\mathbb{A}$表示敌手类别。我们在Qwen和Llama教师-学生对上，使用受控的5,000条提示行为探测套件实例化了这一概念。对于每个模型族，我们比较教师与基础学生以及LoRA蒸馏学生，衡量蒸馏是否降低了可区分性而不仅仅是提高了相似度。LoRA将语义相似度从Qwen的0.788提升至0.862，Llama的0.814提升至0.874。然而对抗评估揭示了剩余的行为差异：学习型判别器仍保持非零优势，成对类别分析显示伪影集中在风格/格式、鲁棒性和领域技术类提示中。跨族教师识别对抗器证实了这一趋势。使用不同族的Llama评判器和A/B交换一致性过滤后，Qwen的区分优势从基础学生的0.158降至LoRA蒸馏后的0.081。查询预算实验表明，分歧引导的采样并不稳定优于分层随机采样，表明覆盖度和多样性仍是强基线。我们的结果表明，语义保真度有用但不充分：黑盒LLM蒸馏需要结合有界性、对抗性和类别感知的评估。

</details>


### [112] [VeriGate: Verifier-Gated Step-Level Supervision for GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30451)
*Aakriti Agrawal,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeriGate通过在GRPO中引入验证器门控机制，在验证器奖励退化时启用过程监督和逐token优势，解决了GRPO稀疏监督和缺乏细粒度信用分配的问题，在数学推理任务上显著提升了准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: GRPO依赖验证器提供的结果奖励存在两个关键问题：(1)当所有采样轨迹获得相同验证器奖励时，组相对优势归零，学习停滞；(2)结果级奖励无法提供步骤级别的信用分配，限制了探索效率和推理鲁棒性。

Method: VeriGate包含三个核心设计：(1)验证器门控机制——当验证器奖励能产生有意义的偏好时由验证器主导，退化时才启用过程监督；(2)将PRM步骤分数转换为未来累积奖励，实现延续感知的信用分配；(3)将这些奖励转化为组归一化的逐token优势，在恢复信息梯度同时保持对奖励破解的鲁棒性。

Result: 在MATH数据集上使用1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型训练，在六个推理基准上评估：1.5B模型平均准确率提升约20%，7B模型提升约12%；显著减少了零梯度失败和奖励破解行为，推理质量优于结果级GRPO和PRM-as-outcome基线。

Conclusion: VeriGate通过验证器门控的过程监督策略，有效弥补了GRPO稀疏监督和缺乏细粒度信用分配的缺陷，在提升推理性能的同时增强了训练的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is an effective recipe for training reasoning models with verifier-based outcome rewards, but its supervision is sparse: when all sampled trajectories for a prompt receive the same verifier reward, the group-relative advantage collapses to zero and learning stalls. Outcome-only rewards also provide no step-level credit assignment, limiting exploration and making it harder to learn robust reasoning. We present VeriGate (Verifier-Gated Step-Level GRPO), a verifier-gated extension of GRPO that addresses these limitations with three design choices. First, VeriGate keeps the verifier in charge whenever verifier rewards induce a meaningful preference among sampled trajectories, and uses process supervision only when verifier rewards are degenerate. Second, instead of collapsing Process Reward Model (PRM) step scores into a single trajectory reward, VeriGate converts them into future-cumulated rewards to assign continuation-aware credit. Third, VeriGate transforms these rewards into group-normalized token-level advantages, restoring informative gradients and fine-grained credit assignment while remaining less susceptible to reward hacking than methods that optimize aggregated PRM scores. Empirically, training on MATH with 1.5B and 7B Qwen2.5-Instruct models and evaluating on six reasoning benchmarks, VeriGate improves average accuracy by about 20% and 12% for 1.5B and 7B models respectively, substantially reduces zero-gradient failures, decreases reward-hacking behavior, and improves reasoning quality relative to outcome-only GRPO and PRM-as-outcome baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 组相对策略优化（GRPO）是一种基于验证器结果奖励来训练推理模型的有效方法，但其监督信号是稀疏的：当某个提示的所有采样轨迹获得相同的验证器奖励时，组相对优势会归零，学习陷入停滞。仅依赖结果奖励也无法提供步骤级别的信用分配，限制了探索效率，使学习鲁棒推理变得更加困难。我们提出了VeriGate（验证器门控的步骤级GRPO），这是一种基于验证器门控的GRPO扩展方法，通过三个设计选择解决了上述局限。首先，当验证器奖励能诱导出采样轨迹之间的有意义的偏好时，VeriGate保持验证器的主导地位；仅在验证器奖励退化时才使用过程监督。其次，VeriGate不将过程奖励模型（PRM）的步骤分数压缩为单一的轨迹奖励，而是将其转化为未来累积奖励，以赋予延续感知的信用分配。第三，VeriGate将这些奖励转化为组归一化的逐token优势，在恢复信息性梯度和细粒度信用分配的同时，相比优化聚合PRM分数的方法更不易受到奖励破解的影响。实验方面，在MATH数据集上使用1.5B和7B Qwen2.5-Instruct模型进行训练，并在六个推理基准上评估，VeriGate使1.5B和7B模型的平均准确率分别提升约20%和12%，大幅减少了零梯度失败，降低了奖励破解行为，并相对于仅使用结果奖励的GRPO和PRM-as-outcome基线提升了推理质量。

</details>


### [113] [A Unified Framework for Gradient Aggregation in Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30452)
*Zeou Hu,Kelvin Ho,Yaoliang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于多目标优化中梯度聚合的统一框架，建立了收敛到Pareto平稳性的（最优）速率，并通过充分对齐条件揭示了收敛的充分条件。同时从原始优化视角统一了现有算法，提出了新的capped MGDA算法，并在对抗性联邦学习中验证了其鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有梯度聚合方法动机各异、逐案例分析、算法差异大，缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架和理论保证。

Method: 提出统一梯度聚合框架；建立充分对齐条件并证明非冲突方向（在梯度凸包内选择）是收敛的充分条件；通过对偶锥投影确保可行性；从原始优化视角统括现有算法；设计基于CVaR的capped MGDA算法。

Result: 建立了收敛到Pareto平稳性的（最优）速率；证明了非冲突方向在凸包内选择是收敛的充分条件；capped MGDA在对抗性联邦学习中表现出鲁棒性；合成问题和实际基准实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了梯度聚合方法，提供了理论保证，并能指导设计新的鲁棒算法。

Original Abstract: Many machine learning problems involve multiple inherent trade-offs that are best addressed by gradient-based multi-objective optimization (MOO) algorithms. Existing methods are often proposed with various motivations, analyzed case by case, and differ algorithmically in how the component gradients are aggregated at each step. In this work, we develop a unifying framework for gradient aggregation in MOO, establishing (optimal) rates of convergence to Pareto stationarity, the standard measure of performance in MOO. Central to our analysis is a sufficient alignment condition, from which we derive a theorem showing that non-conflicting directions, when chosen within the convex hull of gradients, form a fundamental sufficient condition for convergence. We further show that feasibility can be ensured through projection onto the dual cone, broadening the scope of methods that admit convergence guarantees. In parallel, we present a primal optimization perspective of gradient aggregation that encompasses established algorithms, clarifies their theoretical relationships, and enables the design of new variants. As an illustration, we introduce capped MGDA, derived from a CVaR-based formulation, and demonstrate its robustness in adversarial federated learning. Finally, we validate our theory through experiments on synthetic problems and practical benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 许多机器学习问题涉及多个固有的权衡，最好通过基于梯度的多目标优化算法来解决。现有方法往往动机各异、逐案例分析，并且在每一步中组件梯度的聚合方式在算法上存在差异。本文为多目标优化中的梯度聚合开发了一个统一框架，建立了收敛到Pareto平稳性（多目标优化的标准性能度量）的（最优）速率。我们分析的核心是充分对齐条件，由此推导出一个定理，表明在梯度凸包内选择的非冲突方向构成了收敛的基本充分条件。我们进一步证明，通过对偶锥的投影可以确保可行性，从而拓宽了具有收敛保证的方法范围。同时，我们提出了梯度聚合的原始优化视角，该视角涵盖了已有算法，厘清了它们的理论关系，并能够设计新的变体。作为示例，我们引入了capped MGDA（基于CVaR公式推导），并展示了它在对抗性联邦学习中的鲁棒性。最后，我们通过在合成问题和实际基准上的实验验证了我们的理论。

</details>


### [114] [Scalable Constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning via State Augmentation and Consensus for Separable Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30461)
*Santiago Amaya-Corredor,Miguel Calvo-Fullana,Anders Jonsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种分布式约束多智能体强化学习方法，通过拉格朗日乘子的邻居间共识实现全局约束协调，同时保持独立训练的可扩展性，在智能电网需求响应任务中可扩展到数千智能体，而CTDE方法仅能处理数十个。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，智能体具有可分离的动态但需协调满足全局资源约束时，独立学习无法产生可行解，因为智能体无法确定各自对集体约束的适当贡献。

Method: 将状态增广策略学习与对偶变量上的分布式共识相结合。每个智能体离线学习一个增广策略，以局部状态和编码约束反馈的对偶变量为条件。执行时通过局部邻域通信达成对偶变量共识，避免集中训练。

Result: 1. 证明在温和连通性假设下，智能体间乘子的共识误差有界，且约束违反程度随图连通性和共识轮次增加而降低。2. 方法在训练和执行中均线性扩展，而CTDE复杂度至少随智能体数量二次增长。3. 智能电网实验中，无共识时智能体无限推迟需求（退化非解），有共识时满足电网约束和需求。

Conclusion: 轻量级邻居间拉格朗日乘子共识足以实现全局协调的约束执行，同时保持独立训练的可扩展性，可扩展到数千智能体。

Original Abstract: We present a distributed approach for constrained Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) that combines state-augmented policy learning with distributed consensus over dual variables. Our method targets systems where agents have separable dynamics but must coordinate to satisfy global resource constraints, a setting in which, as we demonstrate empirically, independent learning fails to produce feasible solutions because agents cannot determine appropriate individual contributions toward collective constraint satisfaction. The key technical contribution is showing that lightweight neighbor-to-neighbor consensus over Lagrange multipliers suffices for globally coordinated constraint enforcement while preserving the scalability of independent training. Each agent learns a single augmented policy offline, conditioned on both its local state and a dual variable encoding constraint feedback. During execution, agents reach agreement on this dual variable through local communication alone. We prove that under mild connectivity assumptions, the consensus error among agents' multipliers is bounded, and show that this translates to a bounded constraint violation that decreases with graph connectivity and the number of consensus rounds. Unlike centralized training with decentralized execution (CTDE) approaches, whose complexity grows at least quadratically with agent count, our method scales linearly in both training and execution. Experiments on smart grid demand response demonstrate that consensus coordination is \emph{essential for feasibility}: without it, agents satisfy grid capacity constraints only by indefinitely postponing demand, a degenerate non-solution. With consensus, agents converge to a shared dual variable and satisfy both grid constraints and demand fulfillment, scaling to thousands of agents while CTDE baselines are limited to dozens.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一种用于约束多智能体强化学习的分布式方法，该方法将状态增广策略学习与对偶变量上的分布式共识相结合。我们的方法针对智能体具有可分离动态但必须协调以满足全局资源约束的系统。在此设定下，我们通过实验证明，独立学习无法产生可行解，因为智能体无法确定各自对集体约束满足的适当贡献。关键的技术贡献在于证明，拉格朗日乘子上的轻量级邻居间共识足以实现全局协调的约束执行，同时保持独立训练的可扩展性。每个智能体离线学习一个单一的增广策略，该策略以其局部状态和编码约束反馈的对偶变量为条件。在执行过程中，智能体仅通过局部通信就能达成该对偶变量的一致。我们证明，在温和的连通性假设下，智能体间乘子的共识误差有界，并且这一误差转化为有界的约束违反，其程度随图连通性和共识轮次增加而降低。与集中训练分散执行方法的复杂度至少随智能体数量二次增长不同，我们的方法在训练和执行中均线性扩展。在智能电网需求响应上的实验表明，共识协调对可行性至关重要：没有共识时，智能体仅通过无限期推迟需求来满足电网容量约束，这是一种退化的非解。有共识时，智能体能收敛到共享的对偶变量，同时满足电网约束和需求满足，可扩展到数千智能体，而集中训练分散执行基线仅能处理数十个。

</details>


### [115] [idSCD: Identifying Training Datasets through Semantic Correlation Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30462)
*Andrada Gobeaja,Ionut Hodoroaga,Elena Burceanu,Marius Leordeanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语义相关性描述符（SCD）的白盒方法，通过模型内部学习到的语义相关性结构来追踪数据集是否属于训练混合集，无需依赖行为或分布证据。实验表明该方法在多种分类任务中优于现有黑盒和白盒基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集成员推断方法主要依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度、损失、生成样本等），但忽略了数据集在训练中诱导的虚假相关（spurious correlations）会留下独特的语义相关性痕迹。本文旨在利用这一发现，实现更精准的数据集级成员推断。

Method: 提出语义相关性描述符（SCD），用于捕获模型学习到的语义相关性结构，使其在不同数据集混合间具有可比性。具体包括：(1) 在留一数据集诊断中通过SCD恢复数据集特定变化；(2) 提出基于SCD的成员得分，仅需目标模型的SCD和目标数据集的独立SCD即可测试成员关系，无需留一模型。

Result: 在自然语言推理、情感分类和医学文本分类三个实验场景中，基于SCD的成员推断分类器平均性能最高、标准差最低，优于RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA等黑盒基线和SIF白盒基线；最大相对增益在ROC-AUC上超过60%。

Conclusion: 数据集成员信息可以通过模型内部的语义相关性结构被追溯，在数据集组具有明显语义特殊性时尤为有效，为数据集级成员推断提供了新的技术路径。

Original Abstract: Can a dataset be recognized from the spurious correlations it induces during training? We argue that datasets leave dataset-specific traces in a model's learned semantic correlation structure: incidental regularities that are predictive within a dataset, but not causal for the underlying task, can be internalized during training. We use this insight to study dataset-level membership inference, moving beyond existing methods that rely on behavioral or distributional evidence such as confidence scores, losses, margins, generated samples, or query responses. We introduce a white-box semantic fingerprinting approach based on semantic correlation descriptors (SCDs), which capture the semantic correlation structure learned by a model and make it comparable across dataset mixtures. In a controlled leave-one-dataset-out diagnostic, SCDs recover dataset-specific changes and perfectly separate matching from non-matching dataset pairs. We then propose a practical SCD-based membership score that tests whether a target dataset is part of a model's training mixture using only the model's SCD and the target dataset's standalone SCD, without requiring leave-one-dataset-out models. Across three diverse experimental settings, with dataset groups for natural language inference, emotion classification, and medical text classification, we test both the advantages and limitations of SCD-based membership inference with different degrees of semantic separation and keyword support between dataset splits. On average, the classifier based on this score achieves the highest performance and the lowest std, outperforming black-box baselines RMIA, Attack-P, and LiRA, as well as the white-box SIF baseline. These results show that dataset membership can be traced through internal semantic correlations, with the largest relative gain exceeding 60% in ROC-AUC when dataset groups expose distinct semantic particularities.

中文摘要翻译: 一个数据集能否通过其在训练过程中诱导的虚假相关性被识别？我们认为，数据集会在模型学习到的语义相关性结构中留下特定痕迹：那些在数据集内具有预测性、但对底层任务不具备因果关系的偶然规律性，会在训练过程中被模型内化。我们利用这一洞察研究数据集级成员推断，超越了现有依赖行为或分布证据（如置信度分数、损失、边际、生成样本或查询响应）的方法。我们引入了一种基于语义相关性描述符（SCD）的白盒语义指纹方法，该方法捕获模型学习到的语义相关性结构，并使其在不同数据集混合之间具有可比性。在受控的留一数据集诊断实验中，SCD能够恢复数据集特定的变化，并完美区分匹配与非匹配数据集对。随后，我们提出了一种实用的基于SCD的成员得分，仅需目标模型的SCD和目标数据集的独立SCD即可测试目标数据集是否为模型训练混合集的一部分，无需留一数据集模型。在自然语言推理、情感分类和医学文本分类三个不同的实验场景中，我们测试了SCD成员推断在不同语义分离程度和关键词支持程度下的优势与局限。平均而言，基于该得分的分类器取得了最高的性能和最低的标准差，优于黑盒基线RMIA、Attack-P、LiRA以及白盒基线SIF。这些结果表明，数据集成员信息可以通过内部语义相关性被追溯，当数据集组暴露明显的语义特殊性时，最大相对增益在ROC-AUC上超过60%。

</details>


### [116] [Can Subgraph Explanations Be Weaponized to Steal Graph Neural Networks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30470)
*Ojas Nimase,Jiate Li,Yue Zhao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首个在严格黑盒约束下（仅观测离散类标签和二元解释掩码，无概率分数、梯度或置信度）针对图分类的模型提取攻击方法。通过利用模型解释输出引导蒙特卡洛边敏感性估计（附Hoeffding浓度保证）来逼近决策边界，并借助解释子图高效缩小边界搜索空间。实验证明该方法优于现有基线，表明可解释性接口会制造可被利用的攻击面，为防御机制和可解释AI政策框架提供参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图机器学习即服务（GMLaaS）平台为满足监管透明度要求而日益部署可解释性接口，但这种透明度为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的漏洞。现有工作尚未针对图分类任务在严格黑盒约束（仅离散类标签和二元解释掩码）下设计攻击方法。

Method: 提出XSTEAL方法：(1) 利用模型解释输出引导蒙特卡洛边敏感性估计，逼近决策边界，并使用Hoeffding不等式提供估计精度的浓度保证；(2) 利用解释子图高效地缩小边界搜索空间，从而在仅观测离散类标签和二元解释掩码的严格黑盒约束下完成模型提取攻击。

Result: 在多个领域的基准图数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法优于可比较的基线方法。

Conclusion: 可解释性接口会创造可被利用的攻击面，这为设计防御机制和制定可解释人工智能的政策框架提供了重要启示。

Original Abstract: Graph Machine Learning as a Service (GMLaaS) platforms increasingly implement explainability interfaces to meet regulatory transparency requirements. However, this transparency creates exploitable vulnerabilities for model extraction attacks. We present the first model extraction attack specifically designed for graph classification under strict black-box constraints where the attacker observes only discrete class labels and binary explanation masks (no probability scores, gradients, or confidence values). Our method (1) uses model explanation outputs to guide Monte Carlo edge sensitivity estimation toward decision boundaries, with Hoeffding concentration guarantees on estimation accuracy and (2) exploits explanation subgraphs to efficiently narrow the boundary search space. Extensive experiments on benchmark graph datasets across multiple domains demonstrate our method's superiority over comparable baselines. These findings demonstrate that such explainability interfaces create exploitable attack surfaces, informing both defensive mechanisms and policy frameworks for explainable AI mandates. The implementation code is provided in https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/.

中文摘要翻译: 图机器学习即服务（GMLaaS）平台日益部署可解释性接口以满足监管透明度要求。然而，这种透明度为模型提取攻击创造了可利用的漏洞。本文提出了首个专门针对严格黑盒约束下图分类任务的模型提取攻击方法，在此约束下攻击者仅能观测到离散的类标签和二元解释掩码（无概率分数、梯度或置信度值）。我们的方法（1）利用模型解释输出来引导蒙特卡洛边敏感性估计以逼近决策边界，并附带Hoeffding浓度不等式保证估计精度；（2）利用解释子图高效地缩小边界搜索空间。在跨多个领域的基准图数据集上进行的大量实验表明，我们的方法优于可比较的基线方法。这些发现表明，此类可解释性接口创造了可被利用的攻击面，为可解释人工智能强制要求的防御机制和政策框架提供了参考。实现代码见https://github.com/LabRAI/XSTEAL/。

</details>


### [117] [Universal Multiclass Transductive Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30479)
*Steve Hanneke,Hongao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有可能无界标签空间的通用直推式在线分类问题，刻画了其可学习性，证明了可学习类仅存在两种最优速率（有界或对数增长），并引入了一种新的组合结构——LCLL树来表征可学习性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在直推式在线学习场景中，实例序列预先已知但标签未知，且标签空间可能无界。现有研究对这种情况下的可学习性刻画尚不充分，本文旨在系统地表征该设置下的可学习性，并确定可能的最优学习速率。

Method: 引入了一种新的组合结构——"Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL)树"，结合无差异性质（indifference property），来刻画可学习性。同时将可学习性结果推广到不可知情形以及仅已知实例序列生成随机过程的情形。

Result: 对于可学习的类别，最优速率只有两种可能：有界（bounded）或对数增长（logarithmically increasing）。LCLL树与无差异性质共同构成了可学习性的充要条件。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了通用直推式在线分类的可学习性，确定了最优速率的二分法，并给出了组合学上的表征工具，同时将该结果推广到了更一般的设定中。

Original Abstract: We consider the problem of universal transductive online classification with a possibly unbounded label space. This setting considers online learning, with the sequence of instances (without labels) known to the learner in advance. We say a concept class $\mathcal{H}$ is learnable if there is a learning algorithm $\mathcal{A}$, such that for every realizable sequence, the number of mistakes made by $\mathcal{A}$ grows at most sublinearly with the number of predictions. We characterize the learnability of this setting and show that there are only two possible optimal rates for the learnable classes: either bounded or increasing logarithmically. We introduce a new combinatorial structure, called ``Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL) tree'', which, along with the indifference property, characterizes the learnability. We also extend the learnability result to the agnostic case and the case where only the stochastic process that generates the instance sequence is known.

中文摘要翻译: 我们考虑了具有可能无界标签空间的通用直推式在线分类问题。该设置考虑在线学习场景，其中实例序列（不含标签）在学习开始前已预先告知学习器。我们称一个概念类$\mathcal{H}$是可学习的，如果存在一个学习算法$\mathcal{A}$，使得对于每个可实现序列，$\mathcal{A}$所犯的错误数量随预测次数的增长最多为次线性。我们刻画了该设置下的可学习性，并证明对于可学习的类别，最优速率仅有两种可能：有界或对数增长。我们引入了一种新的组合结构——"Level-Constrained-Littlestone-Littlestone (LCLL)树"，该结构连同无差异性质一起表征了可学习性。我们还将该可学习性结果推广到了不可知情形以及仅已知实例序列生成随机过程的情形。

</details>


### [118] [MAAT: Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30514)
*Suryash Yagnik,Shubham Gaur,Saksham Thakur,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现现有的机器去忘(Machine Unlearning)评估存在结构性偏差：因果知识类(Why-type)问题在主流基准中占比极低(不足1.3%)，导致方法在因果知识上失败却能在聚合指标上高分。为此提出5WBENCH平衡基准和MAAT方法，首次在Why-type因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器去忘评估基准中，Why-type（因果/关系知识）问题占比极低（CounterFact不足0.06%，ZSRE仅0.6%，TOFU等不足1.3%），导致缺乏因果知识遗忘的评估能力。方法即使无法遗忘因果知识，在聚合指标上仍可获得高分，且该缺陷无法被检测。

Method: 1. 5WBENCH：构建包含5,000个样本的平衡基准，每个5W类别(Who, What, When, Where, Why)各1,000例。2. MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning)：三阶段框架，作用于LoRA适配器权重，结合梯度投影上升、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量取反、混合KL-隐状态保留修复。

Result: 1. 现有基线方法无法同时在Why-type问题上实现高遗忘与高保留：激进遗忘会破坏保留知识，保守方法无法遗忘因果事实。2. Why-type困难源于多跳推理链（占比44%，其他类型≤2%）和答案跨度长导致梯度稀释（平均40.1个token）。3. MAAT首次在Why-type因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留，达到新的遗忘-保留帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 现有机器去忘评估存在严重的Why-type偏差，5WBENCH提供了首个平衡评估基准，MAAT方法首次解决了因果知识遗忘与保留的权衡难题。

Original Abstract: Machine unlearning evaluation is structurally skewed: Why-type questions, which probe causal and relational knowledge, comprise less than 0.06% of CounterFact, 0.6% of ZSRE, and less than 1.3% of TOFU, MUSE, and WMDP-Cyber. This near-zero representation means that methods that fail on causal knowledge can score highly in aggregate, and this failure is undetectable without balanced evaluation. We present 5WBENCH, a balanced 5,000-sample benchmark with 1,000 examples per 5W category (Who, What, When, Where, Why), making causal unlearning failures quantifiable for the first time. Using 5WBENCH, we show that no existing baseline simultaneously achieves high forgetting and high retention on Why-type questions: aggressive forgetting degrades retained knowledge, while conservative methods fail to forget causal facts. Why-type difficulty stems from multi-hop reasoning chains (44% of Why entries vs. less than or equal to 2% for others) and gradient dilution over 40.1-token answer spans. We present MAAT (Multi-phase Adapter-Aware Targeted Unlearning), a three-phase framework operating on LoRA adapter weights, combining gradient-projected ascent, SVD rank-dimension pruning, task vector negation, and hybrid KL-hidden-state retain repair. MAAT is the first method to simultaneously achieve high forgetting and high retention on Why-type causal knowledge, reaching a new operating point on the forget-retain Pareto frontier. We make our code publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 机器去忘评估存在结构性偏差：探究因果与关系知识的Why型问题在CounterFact中占比不足0.06%，在ZSRE中占比0.6%，在TOFU、MUSE和WMDP-Cyber中占比不到1.3%。这种近乎为零的代表性意味着，在因果知识上失败的方法仍可在聚合指标上获得高分，且这种失败在缺乏平衡评估时无法被检测。我们提出了5WBENCH，一个包含5,000个样本的平衡基准，每个5W类别（谁、什么、何时、何地、为什么）各含1,000个样本，首次实现了对因果去忘失败的可量化评估。利用5WBENCH，我们发现现有基线方法均无法在Why型问题上同时实现高遗忘与高保留：激进的遗忘会破坏已保留的知识，而保守的方法无法遗忘因果事实。Why型问题的难度源于多跳推理链（Why条目中占44%，其他类型≤2%）以及梯度在平均40.1个token的答案跨度上发生的稀释。我们提出了MAAT（多阶段适配器感知的定向去忘），一个作用于LoRA适配器权重的三阶段框架，结合了梯度投影上升、SVD秩维剪枝、任务向量取反以及混合KL-隐状态保留修复。MAAT是首个在Why型因果知识上同时实现高遗忘与高保留的方法，在遗忘-保留帕累托前沿上达到了新的操作点。我们已公开代码。

</details>


### [119] [Revisiting Padded Transformer Expressivity: Which Architectural Choices Matter and Which Don't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30523)
*Anej Svete,William Merrill,Ryan Cotterell,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了带填充（padded）Transformer的表达能力与电路复杂度类之间的关系，证明了在实用假设下，填充Transformer对注意力类型、模型宽度和均匀性条件具有鲁棒性，并指出数值精度和模型深度是影响表达力的关键因素。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
